Chapter 2451: A Minor Flaw...

"Yes, General Li is right."

Although the man had a lower military rank and was nominally under his command, Boss Bai did not dare to be cocky at all before the real trusted subordinate of Marquess Youngchun.

He treated Li Yuanzhen exactly the same way he treated Li Wuji. Rubbing his hands, he said humbly, "You must excuse me, General Li. This is the first time that I have ever been a commander of, hehe, this National Protection Army. Besides, space pirates are all unrestrained and uncontrollable in nature. Now that they are having a great time at the party, it's not easy for me to stop it."

Li Yuanzhen sniffed in dissatisfaction, not showing any respect to Boss Bai, the commander and his nominal superior.

"Rest assured, General Li, I am certainly not an ignorant child."

Boss Bai continued with a smile, "Although the lord asked General Li to follow my command, how can I, a stealthy thief, possibly command General Li, a battle-tested general? I'll be completely at a loss in a real battlefield where thousands of starships are fighting magnificently, and I'll certainly have so many questions on which I need to ask for General Li's advice. When that day comes, I do hope that General Li would… kindly enlighten me!"

"Of course."

Li Yuanzhen said unenthusiastically, "We are all loyal to our country. If there is any military issue, we certainly need to discuss it with each other. Nobody is enlightening anybody."

"Exactly. Exactly. That's what I'm talking about!"

Boss Bai said, "In my opinion, let's not care about the difference between the commander and the deputy commander. If there is any military issue, we'll just discuss with each other on General Li's opinions. After all, you are the out-and-out regular army!

"Also, hehe, when General Li meets the lord in the future, it would be great if you could speak well of me…"

"Of course."

Li Yuanzhen nodded his head without giving consent. "As long as Boss Bai works dutifully and proves your loyalty to the lord and the four families, I will naturally report it truthfully to the lord."

"Then, I'll thank General Li in advance!"

Boss Bai crossed his claws and bowed. Then, glancing around, he leaned close and said mysteriously, "General Li, why don't we let them party for a while longer? There won't be any trouble if we call it a day half an hour later. Can we talk somewhere else, General Li? I found some unusual treasures from a secret warehouse in the Blue Sky Market, and I'm thinking that they may be an eye-opener for General Li too…"

"I don't think… it is necessary."

Li Yuanzhen knew what the guy was up to, seeing the grimace on his face. But of course, a noble must have a noble's manners. How could he agree to it so quickly? Pondering for a moment, he said coldly, as if he were not interested at all, "The Li family has all the treasures in the universe. I don't think this small Blue Sky Market has anything that I've never seen before. I have other military issues that I need to attend to. So, I don't think that I will see them."

"Of course, of course. Military issues are always the most important."

Boss Bai rolled his eyes again and again and then craned his pointy face over, saying stealthily in a low voice, "However, it is possible that what I found in the secret warehouse of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors is a 'Heart Sinking Stone' of a high quality, but I don't know if it is authentic, and I'm thinking that I can use some extra help to determine it. It won't take much time."

"A Heart Sinking Stone?"

Li Yuanzhen was slightly dazed. The features and value of the ultimate treasure immediately popped up in his head, and greediness flashed in his eyes.

"Yes, at the edge of the fourth quadrant, there is a half-desolate world named 'Mystic Tortoise Sector'. The fourth planet of the Sector is not suitable for the survival of human beings due to the incomplete atmosphere and the high-density of the planet itself. So, it has never been developed deeply, and only some primitive lives are struggling to live on it."

Boss Bai said colloquially, "But there is a wonder on the planet, which is the 'unsinkable ocean' that has an extremely high density and a percentage of heavy metal elements. Although it has 'ocean' in its name, it is in fact a collection of dead lakes without water sources. The lakes are of such a high density that they are like melted metal.

"However, the lakes are extremely self-contradictory. When the light objects, such as feathers or paper, are put in the lake, they will immediately sink, but when the heavy objects like metals or stones are put on the surface, they will be rejected by the lake water and float on it. That is how the 'unsinkable ocean' got its name.

"Because of the weirdness of the ocean, its ecological circle is entirely different from the regular oceans. There are some tiny creatures at the bottom of the lake. For thousands of years, the bugs, feeding on the heavy metals in the lake, have been procreating and dying. After their death, their dead bodies would not decay but turn into bones that are like stones and wood. When the bones are piled up, they grow from the bottom of the lakes like bizarre-shaped trees. The mechanism is somewhat similar to the corals in the general oceans.

"The peculiar trees at the bottom of the lakes that have grown for more than thirty thousand years are known as 'Unsinkable Wood', and when the rind is peeled and the essence at the core is taken, it will be the 'Heart Sinking Stone'!

"General Li, you should know that the 'Heart Sinking Stone' is the most tranquil and peaceful treasure. Not only can it stabilize rune arrays and magnetic fields, but it can also soothe the violent brainwaves and get the trainers out of mental derangement, which makes it the best material for the trainers who are about to break into higher levels or to curb the messy thoughts.

"In the auctions of the four families, even a piece of Heart Sinking Stone the size of a baby's finger can be sold for millions of coins. Those larger in size and more effective can be ten times more expensive!"

Li Yuanzhen finally couldn't hold it back anymore. He blurted out, "How big exactly is this Heart Sinking Stone that you have discovered?"

Boss Bai chuckled and made a "follow me" gesture. "About that, General Li will know after you appreciate and play with it in person!"

Li Yuanzhen's eyes became unsteady, and his nose flapped. He looked at the few guards with him.

"The warriors with General Li can come with us."

Boss Bai said with a smile, "I certainly have a few more gifts for the warriors here. I like the tough and macho warriors more than anything else."

"Well, it won't matter to take a look at it."

Li Yuanzhen finally changed his attitude. Pointing at the messy celebration dinner and the drunk space pirates casually, he said, "While we appreciate the magical equipment, let your men clean up the mess here. This does not befit the manners of the regular army!"

"Yes, yes, we are cleaning it up!"

Boss Bai quickly brought Li Yuanzhen away from the celebration dinner to a small warehouse not far away.

A few members of the Big Bai Pirate Gang stepped forward and led Li Yuanzhen's guards to a secret room nearby for their "gifts".

The warehouse was not far away from the garrison of Li Wuji's fleet. Li Yuanzhen was a seasoned expert himself too. Too proud of himself, the noble strode into the warehouse without any suspicion.

The warehouse, having been robbed by the space pirates, was in a total mess, with ragged boxes and closets everywhere. But since it was a private deal between the two of them, there was not a third person on the spot.

Boss Bai closed the door carefully and directed Li Yuanzhen to a fragrant wooden box. Opening the box softly, he smiled and said, "Please take a look, General Li, this is the 'Heart Sinking Stone'. Even if you never run into an accident, it will still be greatly beneficial to absorb the tranquilizing brilliance from it during the daily training."

While talking, he stepped sideways and stood straight.

Li Yuanzhen narrowed his eyes and looked, only to discover that a necklace made of gray metals was floating and revolving slowly in the midair, emanating pure brilliance like moonlight that flowed slowly from his eyes to his heart like a brook.

"Is this the 'Heart Sinking Stone'?"

Li Yuanzhen was not in a high position in the Li family. Otherwise, he wouldn't have been only a secondary general below Li Wuji. Although he had heard of such an ultimate treasure as the Heart Sinking Stone before, he had never seen one with his own eyes. He was immediately attracted by the beautiful and charming light, and he moved closer to the box without knowing it.

Just like the rumors had it, when he was bathed in the silver brilliance, his nerves and his veins seemed to be soaked in a warm spring, and he couldn't feel more comfortable and gratified. Even his soul seemed to be stretching out.

It was truly the best treasure to calm and soothe the mind, with exactly the same effects as described in the encyclopedia of Heavenly Materials and Earthly Treasures.

Li Yuanzhen swallowed hard and couldn't help but retrieve the necklace of the Heart Sinking Stone from the anti-gravity rune arrays. Touching it softly in his palm, he sensed the smoothness and silkiness of the treasure while he pretended to be angry. "Such an ultimate treasure should've been offered to the lord when he was here a moment ago. Why did you never bring it out? Commander Bai, you are not loyal enough!"

"Aya!"

Boss Bai slapped his thigh and defended himself, "It was only because the treasure was not found until just now due to the turmoil! Besides, there are a lot of rarely-seen Heavenly Materials and Earthly Treasures in the Blue Sky Market. I naturally have selected dozens of gifts that are in perfect condition for the lord. Although this necklace of the Heart Sinking Stone is great, there is a minor flaw on it, and it seems inappropriate to offer it to our noble lord.

"Therefore, I'm thinking that General Li could play with it for a while first, or maybe I can ask General Li to help me find a capable craftsman to repair it. After all, with all the connections General Li has in the four families, you certainly know much more excellent refiners than I do. If it could truly be repaired, we'll see how we are going to deal with it. How does it sound, General Li?"

Li Yuanzhen was overjoyed first but more or less upset when he heard that the necklace was flawed. But on the other hand, the delicate and precious necklace would have been an unbearable gift for him if it was devoid of any flaw.

While appreciating the necklace in fascination, Li Yuanzhen asked, "Where is the flaw? Why haven't I noticed it?"

"It's just a tiny, tiny, tiny flaw, which actually doesn't hurt at all."

With the most brilliant smile, Boss Bai rubbed his hands and leaned close. "Here, allow me to point out the flaw for General Li…"

Chapter 2452: Revealed!

Standing next to Li Yuanzhen, Boss Bai extended one finger and pointed it at the necklace of the Heart Sinking Stone.

Li Yuanzhen suddenly had an extremely subtle sense of warning, as if his soul had just been stung by a mosquito.

He felt that the brightness from the necklace of the Heart Sinking Stone was too gentle and hypnotizing, and the soothing effect on the soul was so strong that his soul was not being soothed but being paralyzed.

The more he stared at the necklace of the Heart Sinking Stone, the more he felt that there was an immeasurable swirl inside the necklace, which absorbed his soul and senses and lowered his nerve reactions to the minimum.

Also, the necklace felt too sticky, as if a certain layer of something was attached to it.

When he touched the necklace with his fingers, the sticky stuff turned into tiny worms that crawled into his pores in a hurry and dashed all the way upward along the arm, as if their target was his heart.

This… is not the Heart Sinking Stone?

It was not until the sticky feeling almost entered his heart that Li Yuanzhen finally realized what was going on in his trance. The descriptions on the "Heart Sinking Stone" in the encyclopedia of Heavenly Materials and Earthly Treasures did not seem to include so many weird features.

But it was too late.

Due to the dual attacks of illusions and the lethal poisons, he couldn't constrict his pupils even if he wanted to. All that he could do was to watch Boss Bai extending his finger at him with a smile.

Li Yuanzhen's heart shrank hard, and he intended to evade Boss Bai's finger intuitionally. Watching the look on Boss Bai's face, he noticed that the head of space pirates had completely changed. Not only were the rat-like feelings entirely gone, but there was not even much satisfaction now that his cunning scheme had worked out. All that could be found on his wrinkled, pointy head was peace and casualness, giving the feeling that he was actually… bored.

Yes, bored. It seemed that for Boss Bai, a noble descendant such as Li Yuanzhen did not deserve to be his opponent or even his prey at all. He was simply yawning while he did his uneventful, daily work at ease.

His easiness made Li Yuanzhen so intimidated as if he had fallen into an ice cave.

He wanted to shout, so loudly and desperately that all the soldiers outside would hear him.

However, Boss Bai's finger that was aimed at the Heart Sinking Stone, like a dragon waving its tail, poked at his throat from an unbelievable angle.

Crack!

After a gentle touch, the strength already pierced through Li Yuanzhen's neck like a helical drill, breaking the Adam's apple without any trouble. The bones and the blood blocked his vocal cord, and his screams were stuffed back to his chest.

"Lightning" did not even begin to describe the speed of his attack. Even if Li Yuanzhen had not suffered the dual scourges of the illusions and the venom, he wouldn't have been able to dodge the astounding finger at all!

Li Yuanzhen was in such pain that veins were almost breaking out on his forehead. Out of his natural instincts, he was already controlling his right fingers to retrieve powerful magical equipment from his Cosmos Rings. He had realized that the Boss Bao standing before him was certainly not a filthy rat that had crawled out of a gutter, but a terrifying monster that was too overwhelming for him to estimate at all.

Even though he summoned his magical equipment, it might not be enough to kill the enemy, but there would at least be noises that would let the soldiers outside know that a shocking battle was going on inside the warehouse.

But even such a tiny wish fell out in the excruciating pain.

Boss Bai grabbed his right hand where the Cosmos Rings were worn and closed it unhurriedly. His right hand immediately turned from half solid to a mess of fluids. All his bones and his flesh were crushed although they were still enveloped by a tiny layer of skin.

Li Yuanzhen's tears were running out in pain. It was not entirely because of the pain but more of a biophysical reaction because of the extreme fear.

Even so, he still gnashed his teeth to perform the final counterattack. Folding his right leg like a saber, he kicked Boss Bai's lower abdomen brutally.

Bam!

Having no time to respond, Boss Bai was hit precisely.

Li Yuanzhen was overjoyed. He was about to launch a flood of continuous counterattacks when he felt that the feedback of his knee was weird.

It did not seem to have crashed into flesh and broken the bones, but instead, it seemed to have broken into a certain highly attractive gel, which dispersed all his spiritual energy into nothingness.

"Ah—"

Li Yuanzhen was too shocked to react anymore.

Boss Bai, on the other hand, did not give him even 0.01 seconds to react either. Sniffing, he kicked the guy back.

Li Yuanzhen felt that a full-speed starship had just crashed into his groins.

His entire reproductive system had been reduced into broken cells and enchylema.

It was a soul-stirring attack—literally "soul-stirring".

Li Yuanzhen opened his mouth and vomited a mouthful of plasma that was mixed with broken bones, which spurted out like a weird spring before he completely dried and collapsed like a balloon that had lost the air.

Expressionlessly, with thirty percent boredom, Boss Bai twisted Li Yuanzhen's neck by 180 degrees.

Then, he stuck to the man's back and exerted his strength, giving the high and mighty noble a chance to see his own ass clearly.

"How… dare…"

Li Yuanzhen's eyes were protruding violently, and the veins that were deeply buried in his face were all breaking apart. He couldn't have looked more awful.

The tough vitality of the Immortal Cultivators made him able to talk after he vomited the broken bones and flesh in his throat, but before he finished his sentence, Boss Bai had already opened his fingers and stabbed them deeply into the guy's face like burning iron sticks, crunching his entire skull.

BOOM!

Violent white flames flooded out of Boss Bai's fingers and palms and pierced through Li Yuanzhen's broken skull, destroying the brain tissues and the soul that was hidden inside. Very soon, the deputy leader of Li Wuji's fleet was burnt into ashes.

Then, Boss Bai yawned so hard in his dullness that even his tears were almost squeezed out.

While whistling the weird tunes of space pirates, he raised his wrist to check the time and the progress in other procedures. Then, right next to Li Yuanzhen's ashes, he retrieved a human-shaped spiritual puppet from his Cosmos Ring.

The spiritual puppet was of a similar height and size to Li Yuanzhen.

Boss Bai activated the spiritual puppet and pointed at the operation menu on the breastplate of the puppet quickly. An amazing scene happened. Gentle brilliance flowed out of the gaps of the spiritual puppet and completely enshrouded the puppet until it became a vivid, cubic "skin" that was exactly Li Yuanzhen's appearance!

Under Boss Bai's remote control, the spiritual puppet that was pretending to be Li Yuanzhen stood up and stepped forward rather rigidly.

This was exactly a "stand-in" spiritual puppet, which was designed to increase the sense of participation during the remote meetings.

If it was close or detected by spiritual energy, it would naturally be seen through easily.

However, if it was only observed from far away, maybe it could fool other people, at least for ten to twenty minutes.

Right then, door knocks echoed from outside.

"Come in."

Boss Bai said casually.

The adulation, arrogance, and aggressiveness that had covered his face since days ago were entirely gone. Although he was still an emaciated, hideous man, his eyes were like the clearest mirror absorbing all the stars in the universe before washing them with his own ambitions and reflecting them ten times more brightly. His brilliant eyes and his hideous face were a major contrast, which further added to his captivating, idiosyncratic charisma.

Even if he were a rat, he would be a super-alloy rat that could bite a hole in the three-dimensional universe.

If Li Wuji and the many leaders of space pirates had seen his current face earlier, they would die before they "cooperated" with such a Boss Bai.

The gate of the warehouse was slightly opened, and two soldiers of the Big Bai Pirate Gang strode in.

No, they were not "soldiers". They were the notorious pirates who had fought the Black Wind Fleet the hard way with Boss Bai and jumped to the Imperium from the federation to bring disasters to this land!

"Boss, the few guards have been taken care of. All the preparations are done too. The traps will be closed as soon as you command."

The two seasoned bandits from the Star Glory Federation, however, couldn't hold back their excitement.

"What's the hurry? The stew is in the pot now, and it's not getting anywhere."

Boss Bai glanced at them and said casually, "If you are excited by such a minor incident, wouldn't you have a heart attack when we loot the royal palace of the Imperium of True Human Beings or the Supreme Emperor's mausoleum? There's no need to be anxious. Bring me my battle robe first."

Taking a deep breath, one of the two space pirates from the federation stepped forward and held a tacky colorful shirt and a pair of beach pants with both hands, saying respectfully, "Boss, here's the battle robe!"

"Alright."

Boss Bai took off the leather coat and the messy clothes on his body and put on the fancy shirt and the short pants. Then, he said, "And my boots."

"Boss, here are your battle boots!"

The other space pirate brought over a pair of blue-and-white flip-flops—ones that were commonly seen in the public bathhouses.

After Boss Bai put on his clothes, he finally wiped his mouth with a smile of satisfaction.

"Boss, should we bring you a cup of ice juice of tropical fruits and vegetables?"

His two subordinates asked considerately.

"No. Give me a cup of oolong tea. Make it strong."

Boss Bai sniffed and said, "It is too unchallenging to deal with those morons. They are not even as good as the tough guys of the Black Wind Fleet. How boring."

"That's right!"

His subordinates grinned.

He stepped out of the warehouse, with unpleasant noises echoing from his shoes nonstop. Looking at the Blue Sky Market ahead of him where nobody knew that the god of death had arrived, he waved his hands and said, "There's nothing we can do now. Let's go and teach the idiots at the center of the cosmos a lesson about what real 'space pirates' are!"

Chapter 2453: The Unparalleled Freedom

At this moment, the leadership of the other twenty-eight pirate gangs apart from the Big Bai Pirate Gang were partying together.

Many of them were stoned. Even those who were not drunk were also blushing and staggering; their basic wariness gone. They did not realize at all that the members of the Big Bai Pirate Gang were fewer and fewer.

From the hundred docks and ports around the Blue Sky Market, the starships, which belonged to the Big Bai Pirate Gang and which seemed to be in dire need of maintenance due to the fierce battle just now, turned their turrets in silence and aimed the powerful cannons at the other pirate heads as well as the soldiers of Li Wuji's fleet.

"Huh. Where… Where are the people of the Big Bai Pirate Gang?"

However inert they were, when the warriors of the Big Bai Pirate Gang gradually disappeared, leaving only the flag of a white mouse that was shaking its bottom, a lot of space pirates could still realize that something wasn't right.

Looking at each other in bewilderment, they all felt sharp icy cones stinging their spines.

The liquor that they just poured in all turned into cold sweat, leaking out of the pores all over their bodies.

"Not good!"

Finally, some of the experienced pirates exclaimed desperately, "We're tricked! Disperse now!"

It was a shame that they were exactly like Li Yuanzhen just now, and it was too late when they realized they were tricked.

At this moment, beyond the manmade atmosphere above the Blue Sky Market and in the deep space, twisted waves were spreading out as the starships ripped each other apart.

In the bleeding space, Li Wuji's fleet suffered the critical strike from "Fist King" Lei Zonglie, and the first miserable firewall just burst out.

At the central square in the Blue Sky Market, destructive explosions broke out among the atrocious space pirates too.

The first explosions did not come from the giant cannons on the docks and ports, but from the wine buckets everywhere around them.

Most of the wine buckets had a hidden layer at the bottom where crystal bombs were hidden. Just now, when the soldiers of the Big Bai Pirate Gang carried those "powder keg" right before their eyes openly, nobody noticed anything wrong.

Some of the larger buckets that were delivered in the last few rounds only had the deep red wine on the top layer, and the main body of the buckets was entirely filled with crystal bombs. After chugging three buckets of wine liquor with Boss Bai in the beginning, nobody was interested in the hard liquor anymore, and none of them ever tried to tear apart the wine buckets and see what was inside.

All the buckets were rolling around the party randomly. Some were even used by the space pirates as tables or chairs. Who could've thought that those buckets were actually life-enders?

At this moment, when all the wine buckets exploded at the same time, it was like ten thousand fireballs had crawled out of the ground, swallowing all the pirate heads!

The first crystal bombs that had been detonated were mainly in two types. The first type was naturally the explosive bombs and incendiary bombs with a high damage. However, the more sordid ones were the interferential bombs that boasted extremely powerful sabotaging abilities. Once they were detonated, they could unfold an extremely violent, chaotic magnetic field in the space nearby, preventing most magical equipment, particularly the space magical equipment, from functioning. To be more specific, they could temporarily block the release and retrieval of the Cosmos Rings!

Of course, many advanced Cosmos Rings with powerful performances could still operate steadily without being affected.

But such advanced goods were clearly not something that those pirates could afford.

Having come here for a celebration dinner, the pirate heads naturally had no reason to be fully-armed. They had hidden all their crystal suits and attack magical equipment in the relatively common Cosmos Rings.

The batch of interferential bombs that Boss Bai had arranged paralyzed their Cosmos Rings for one to three minutes.

It was a fatal delay!

Many pirate heads were blown apart in the first second, their limbs flying everywhere in the midair and burnt into ashes in the furious flames.

Most pirate heads were already sozzled, and the circulation of spiritual energy was extremely slow. It was already remarkable if they still had half of their combat ability left. How could they resist such overwhelming flames?

What was even more dreadful was that ominous thunders echoed from the sky before they put out the fire on them. The warships of the Big Bai Pirate Gang docked at the periphery of the Blue Sky Market had insanely aimed all their cannons at the central square of the Blue Sky Market, launching a merciless blanket bombardment again and again.

The common cannons on the regular warships could easily have a caliber of half a meter to one meter. The advanced cannons were even more astonishing.

When tens of thousands of tons of bombs fell on the top of the many space pirates like a storm, their power was beyond imagination. It was an out-and-out cataclysm.

They might have been melted into garbage even if they were wearing crystal suits, not to mention that they had no chance to retrieve their crystal suits at all and could only resist it with their bodies of flesh and blood and the unsteady spiritual shields that were condensed in a haste. It was simply irresistible!

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

The Big Bai Pirate Gang let out a firepower that was fiercer than in any battle they had before. One couldn't help but worry that the whole Blue Sky Market would be destroyed in the barrages.

After several rounds of bombardments, the ground of the central square was lowered by half a meter. There were no traces of the past left at all, and the only things that could be found were the overlapping craters.

"Ahhh… Ahhh… Ahhh…"

Many "things" that did not look like humans at all were writhing in fire among the craters, letting out screams from the surging smoke.

Huala! Huala! Huala!

From the depths of the smoke, the sounds of clattering crystal suits came over, like the laughter of the god of death.

With a smile of iron and blood, Boss Bai gradually emerged from the smoke.

His left hand was placed on his back, and he was holding a cup of iced oolong tea in his right hand. He moved in the middle of fire and smoked with a pair of blue-and-white slippers as if he were taking a walk in the gentle breeze after enjoying a delicious lunch. He couldn't have looked more easy and comfortable.

"Boss Bai! Boss Bai!"

In the central square that had almost been melted into a pool of magma, several space pirates in ragged clothes with a blackened skin, whose nerves had been ripped apart by the blasts, staggered at Boss Bai like ghosts seeking their revenge.

Boss Bai did not even bother to look at them. Airwaves that were even more violent than the blasts of the barraging cannons surged out of his body, as if an invisible god of slaughter were rising to the sky, and slapped down on behalf of his hands. He did not even move his little finger when the few pitiful wretches were pressed into meat pies.

"Brothers…"

Boss Bai narrowed his eyes and took a deep breath of the scorching and stinky air. Smiling, he declared, one word after another, "Let's wash this place with blood!"

Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep!

Countless warriors of the Big Bai Pirate Gang in heavy crystal suits with five times more ammunitions, who were not armed to their teeth but to their nose and hair, leaped out of the smoke and split apart to Boss Bai's two sides like iron torrents. They charged into the smoke ferociously, reaping the lives of the lucky survivors ruthlessly.

The pirate heads, having suffered the series of traps and strikes, were already ninety percent dead. How could they have been a match for the federal space pirates who had been well-prepared?

To make things worse, they had the support of Boss Bai, an expert in the Divinity Transformation Stage, who would go forward and slaughter anyone with the slightly more exuberant spiritual waves. Which pirate head could possibly escape from him?

"Why?"

One of the pirate heads, who was toasted by Boss Bai enthusiastically a moment ago, waved his burning arms and screamed in confusion, "We have obviously surrendered to you and elected you as the commander of the National Protection Army. Why are you still killing all of us? Why?"

"Hehe. Are you obeying me sincerely, or are you pushing me out as a shield and a scapegoat?"

Boss Bai said with a smile, "It's true that I'm very interested in your starships and the experienced, skillful sailors and artillerymen on them, but their bosses are of no use to me and will only add to my trouble.

"Besides, the National Protection Army? F*ck the National Protection Army! It's just the cannon fodder for the four families. I would rather be sh*tted on than be humiliated as such!"

Hardly had he concluded his sentence when he withdrew a burnt saber from a crater and slashed the pirate who was on fire into two pieces.

"Boss Bai, what… what is your Cultivation?"

Another brutal-looking pirate charged over fearlessly and roared, "I don't buy it. Are you not in the Nascent Soul Stage?"

"I haven't been in the Nascent Soul Stage since three hundred years ago."

Boss Bai cut through the man's heart in one attack before crumbling his hair remotely. He then sighed at the mist of blood and said casually.

"You've lost your mind, Boss Bai, you've truly lost your mind!"

The third pirate head, seeing that Boss Bai was tough enough to kill two of the most ferocious pirates more easily than squeezing two bugs, was too shocked to attack Boss Bai and get himself killed. He simply shouted to cover his fear, "You pissed off the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors by sweeping across the Blue Sky Market for the four families first, and then you are swallowing us right in front of Marquess Youngchun. You are asking to be killed, you'd better consider the consequences, you—"

"Captain Zhao, you're wrong about one thing."

Boss Bai said, as if it were not a big deal, "I am not swallowing you right in front of Marquess Youngchun. I'm planning to devour Marquess Yongchun's fleet too. As a matter of fact, they are the main dish, whereas you are just appetizers. If my purpose were to swallow the small fries like you, such trouble would've been highly unnecessary!"

"You—"

The pirate head was dumbfounded by Boss Bai's craziness. "You want to swallow Marquess Yongchun's fleet? You've pissed off two greatest forces in the Imperium of True Human Beings. There's no place in the entire universe for you right now. Are you planning to fight everyone at the same time?"

"We are space pirates. Space pirates are born to fight everyone, the whole world, and the whole universe."

Boss Bai sighed in confusion. "You people are not even ready to do that, and yet you are mimicking other people to be space pirates?"

"What do you want exactly?"

Shuddering, the pirate asked, "What benefits would you get by infuriating the reformists and the four families? What can you possibly get?"

"Freedom."

Boss Bai stepped forward, slashed him apart, and smiled. "Unparalleled freedom!"

Chapter 2454: A Donkey in a Tiger's Skin

In fact, the half fleet that Li Wuji left in the Blue Sky Market could've stopped Boss Bai from the massacre—with at least a 5% chance.

They were too proud of themselves to join the carnival of the space pirates. Naturally, they drank far less wine, and they were still rather vigilant and organized. If they had realized what was going on, they could've caused great trouble to the Big Bai Pirate Gang, which was, in fact, terribly outnumbered.

However, they were stalled by another bunch of ferocious lunatics, who were the members of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the reformists that were unfortunately captured in the battle previously.

The important captives, such as the deacons of the Business Alliance and Zuo Tianying, the market manager, were naturally tied up and delivered to Li Wuji's starship, ready to be escorted to the four families' territory.

But for the general warriors and members of the Business Alliance and the reformists, it was neither necessary nor possible to bring all of them away. Resources were highly precious in space voyages. Every one additional person on board would mean more fuel consumption during space jumps. Their daily needs could be quite costly too. Even if they were to enter the hibernation state, they would still consume freezing drugs!

After all, the most important culprits had been taken away, so the unimportant captives were simply locked in the Blue Sky Market, to be either executed or sold to mines and farms as slaves. In such a way, they could yield more value and more or less make up the cost to capture them!

The captives were caged not far away from Li Wuji's half fleet, which was also perfectly reasonable, because it would be easier for them to watch over the captives.

However, when the space pirates were partying passionately, and when Li Wuji's soldiers were tipsy and letting down their wariness, undercurrents were flowing among the captives.

"Let's fight them!"

"I was told that we are the first 'rebels' that have been caught in the peripheral worlds of the Imperium. We will certainly be killed to set an example for other people!"

"Let's fight them!"

"I was told that when the new emperor ascends to the throne, he will cut us apart and offer our blood to his royal robe!"

"No, we can't live to see the new emperor being crowned. I was told that we will be executed as offerings to the flag before the coalition fleet of the four Kurfürsten attack the Seven Seas Grand Market. It-It won't be long!"

The anxious roars of the captives congregated into a torrent of fury.

However, even if they intended to fight anyone, they couldn't do so when they were imprisoned.

Every one of them was carrying manacles and shackles weighing dozens of kilograms, which would release extremely powerful electric arcs when pulled by force.

The bars on their cage had been connected to high-voltage electricity too, and blueish sparks were spluttering nonstop.

Outside, tough-looking space pirates and regular soldiers were defending the place at the same time and would summon Li Wuji's fleet if anything went wrong.

Theoretically, the defense was flawless.

It was also the reason why the space pirates and the regular soldiers were relaxed and enjoying themselves.

However, even the strongest fortress could be breached from within, particularly when some sleazy "rats" had snuck in when the fortress was built.

When the captives were all panicked and helpless, an astonishing change suddenly took place.

The few pirates from the Big Bai Pirate Gang who were watching over them outside of the prison floated to the back of the regular soldiers like white ghosts, covering their mouths, cutting their throats, and stabbing the poisoned daggers into their chests by the way.

A lot of people of unknown identities walked in with dozens of Cosmos Rings and a few boxes of magical equipment. Opening the doors of the prison cells, they threw the keys to the manacles and shackles to the captives.

"Fellow Cultivators, don't panic. We're all spies that General Deacon Jin Yuyan sent to the Big Bai Pirate Gang for fear that the Bai-surnamed scoundrel would betray us.

"As it turns out, he is truly a treacherous man. After taking so many benefits from the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, he has sold us to the enemy so quickly!

"Right now, Boss Bai has possibly grasped the list of the spies. We have nowhere to run to now. So, let's simply go big with the brothers here before we die!

"Brothers of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and reformists, we are all supposed to be hopeless. One moment of hesitation, we may even end up more miserable than death. What's there to think about? Let's do it! If you kill one person, it will be worth your life; if you kill two, you will be making a profit!"

Huala! Huala! Huala!

The captives were freed, and the chainswords, vibration sabers, and storm bolters were distributed to the hands that were shuddering in fury.

To be honest, the mysterious persons' claim that they were spies that Jin Yuyan sent to Boss Bai had obvious loopholes.

However, the loopholes could've only been noticed with enough time in a peaceful environment, where one could be focused on thinking.

But right now, the captives were already dead for sure and had nothing to lose now.

Now that the leadership such as Zuo Tianying had been taken away by Li Wuji, the remaining captives were mostly middle- and low-level members who did not have enough information or wisdom.

When they were about to be killed, somebody had offered them a chance for the last attempt. How could they calm themselves down to think?

"Let's go! Kill them all!"

The captives congregated into a violent tide and leaped out of the prison cells, charging at the closest garrison of Li Wuji's fleet under the leadership of the few "spies".

Ten seconds ago, the cannonballs from the starships in the ports just passed by their heads and landed on the garrison of the regular army in earsplitting shrieks, which was almost a perfect coordination of soldiers and cannons that Boss Bai had practiced for hundreds of years.

It must be admitted that although Li Yuanzhen was not famous in the Imperium's army and had been killed by Boss Bai in one attack, he was truly an experienced general.

Even the temporary garrison of his troops had been constructed among a few underground fortresses and firing points that were in the Blue Sky Market in the beginning. Even the defense arrays had been repaired. Watch towers had been deployed too.

Therefore, the first round of barrages from the Big Bai Pirate Gang dealt limited damage to the garrison of the regular army. They did not end as miserably as the space pirates in the central square.

The regular army who escaped into the underground fortresses in time maintained most of their combat ability.

The attack that the captives launched at them without armor couldn't destroy them at all. The best it could achieve was to create some minor interferences.

Of course, due to the lack of leadership from Li Yuanzhen, the minor interferences might escalate into the turmoil of half to one hour.

For Boss Bai, the turmoil of one hour was enough.

While Li Wuji's fleet was stalled by the captives, Boss Bai destroyed all the pirate heads who participated in the celebration dinner. Having no time to wipe the bloodstains on his crystal suit, he had pointed his saber at the middle- and low-level sailors of the twenty-eight pirate gangs that were ten times larger than the Big Bai Pirate Gang.

"Everybody, listen up. By the order of the lord, General Li Yuanzhen and I will regroup the National Protection Army together!"

Boss Bai waved his saber aggressively and walked together with the spiritual puppet that had been disguised into the appearance of Li Yuanzhen as he spoke to the dumbfounded and panicked space pirates.

"The lord and I have been treating you fairly and sincerely meaning to recruit you into the glorious army of the Imperium! However, a few voracious and treacherous men were bold enough to keep their connection with the rebels and even instigate the captives to rise up, hoping to kick me out of my place.

"Those shameless scoundrels have been suppressed by General Li and I together. Here are their heads!"

"If you are devoted to the country, lay down your magical equipment and weapons obediently, squat where you are, and raise your hands. I know that this is none of your business, and you will not be punished. After the regrouping, your positions and your military ranks will be no lower than they are right now. There will also be tremendous awards tomorrow morning.

"Whoever is dogged enough to resist, just follow those dead pirates and eat crap in hell!"

Boss Bai's cries, as well as the bleeding, deformed heads, scared all the space pirates out of their wits. They felt that their bones had been softened.

Nobody knew that a celebration dinner could end up like this. Why were the two parties fighting each other uncannily?

However, it was anything but unusual for the regular army to perform bloody regrouping thunderously through iron and blood after they suppressed the bandits and the rebels.

Boss Bai said that it was Li Wuji's intention, which was not—not entirely impossible.

Even the bloodiest "regrouping", or to be more straightforward, "factional fight", often only involved the leadership of the pirate gangs. For the sailors, artillerymen, and other workers at the bottom level, they would not be greatly affected. After all, the starships had to be maneuvered by them, and they were the ones who did the actual work during the killing and looting outside. If they were all killed, the pirate gangs would only be empty shells, and Boss Bai would be a commander who could only command himself. Then, why would he bother to launch a factional fight at all?

What Boss Bai said befitted the tradition of the circle of space pirates. Many space pirates were thirty-percent convinced the moment they heard it.

As space pirates, they had long fed their loyalty and devotion to dogs, and they always followed whoever had bigger fists. It didn't matter if their bosses were slain by other people, because they could always seek a new boss who was stronger and tougher. What's the big deal?

Besides, it was chaos right now. Their starships were waiting to be maintained in the docks, unorganized. The space pirates around them were mostly from other places, and they never knew the strangers until half a day ago. Also, they were mostly half drunk and could barely grab their sabers. Even if they meant to avenge their bosses, how were they capable of it?

Charging forward to slay Boss Bai first and then Li Wuji later?

That was certainly a joke!

Clang! Clang!

Many veterans who had been space pirates for decades, having been through a dozen factional fights and changed multiple bosses, immediately tossed their chainswords and storm bolters far away. Holding the wine containers, they squatted and smiled at the rookie space pirates who were less experienced. "It's alright, it's alright. The bosses are fighting, which is none of our business. Everything will be fine as long as you squat and do not do anything!"

Chapter 2455: The Kleptomaniac

Naturally, not all the space pirates were as "smart". Part of them, who were either fearless or brainless, went on a rampage and resisted.

However, under the suppression of the thunderous might of the Divinity Transformation Stage from Boss Bai, their resistance was obliterated without the least influence.

Half an hour later, all the space pirates were disarmed, and the critical places of the Blue Sky Market had all fallen to the hands of the Big Bai Pirate Gang.

Li Wuji's half fleet was the only resistance left, who was fighting based on the hidden fortresses and the firing points.

Compared to the space pirates who were quick to shift sides, they were much more determined and had a higher combat ability. If they were to be entirely destroyed, the Big Bai Pirate Gang's insufficient soldiers would be greatly tested again.

Boss Bai did not intend to completely annihilate those people either. The well-trained, experienced soldiers in the regular army had more sense of honor and discipline than the space pirates did. They were too great a treasure to be killed easily.

"Then…"

Waving the hot blood on his saber, Boss Bai stared at the shaking stars deep inside the burning sky. "The next battlefield will be space!"

Thousands of tides spurted out of the power rune arrays of his crystal suit, while Boss Bai turned into a streak of white brilliance and dashed at the battlefield in space!

…

The fierce battle in the space battlefield was still going on.

"Eternal Spring", the flagship of Li Wuji's fleet, was surrounded by more than ten rapid warships of the Cultivators, like a buffalo besieged by hyenas. It marched to all directions crazily but failed to break out of the enemy's web of fire. In the meantime, the enemy simply poured fierce firepower into the starship through the damage on the body that was torn apart.

The spiritual shield and the defense rune arrays of Eternal Spring had completely gone out of control. Every round of barrage would raise an astonishing explosion. Tremendous broken materials and even the limbs of the Immortal Cultivators were absorbed into the vacuum, spurting out like the blood of a behemoth.

Suddenly, a cluster of dazzling brilliance burst out of the head of Eternal Spring, and a Colossus that was as pure as jade jumped out of it. The honeycomb-type cannons on its shoulders blossomed like lotus flowers, letting out hundreds of streaks of brilliance spectacularly. Dozens of space shuttles and even one rapid warship on the Cultivators' side were blown up in a row, significantly reducing the pressure of Eternal Spring.

The Colossus was exactly the "Lotus Buddha", driven by Li Wuji in person!

The arrival of the Colossus made the heated battlefield pause briefly, as everybody was deeply astounded by the flames of spiritual energy that he unleashed.

Li Wuji's mood, however, did not turn better at all because of such a pause.

As far as his eyes could reach, there was nothing but mayhem and goriness in the space battlefield. He felt that his head was dizzy, and he almost felt like vomiting blood and crying out aloud.

The fleet that he had been working on for decades with all the resources of himself and even his allies had been doomed.

Most of the starships had been squeezed apart or bounced back the moment they entered the four-dimension. They had either been turned into cold iron coffins, where all the crew members were killed by the flying blades made of space ripples, or the gigantic, twisted baguettes where serial explosions were breaking out nonstop, and the crew members were darting out from the damage of the starships like miserable sparks.

The main starships such as Eternal Spring, which had a firmer structure and a higher defense ability, might have luckily survived the scourge of the four-dimensional storm, but when they devoted their extreme computational ability to the activation of the defense rune arrays and the damage control system, the "backdoors" were wide open, and the Cultivators' viruses marched in unstoppably.

The wretched "Fist King" Lei Zonglie, through the "backdoors" that were implanted in advance, had hacked in their battle network and completely paralyzed their commanding and coordination system.

A modern war was a systematic war. A single starship or Colossus was far from enough to decide the outcome of it.

Commanding and communication was the top priority!

In terms of comprehensive capability, the Cultivators' fleet, which was jumbled in the recent half year through robbery and which was as poor as beggars, was absolutely no match for Li Wuji's fleet.

However, they had chosen the perfect timing for the attack, and they had sabotaged the commanding and communication system in Li Wuji's fleet, isolating all the starships of the Immortal Cultivators that were still capable of fighting. As a result, they boasted absolute advantages in number, firepower, and mobility in the regional battlefields, thereby reversing the situation miraculously.

Naturally, the miracle for the Cultivators was undoubtedly the most ridiculous nightmare for the Immortal Cultivators.

Seeing that his starships were being blown up by the Cultivators one after another, leading to greater serial explosions, Li Wuji felt that his own heart was about to explode too.

"Damn it! I will not spare any of you! Not any!"

His eyes bloodshot and his hair in a mess, Li Wuji screamed inside the spiritual residence of his Colossus. Soon, he locked onto the largest warship of the Cultivators, which had the most intense spiritual waves, and marched at it in his Colossus.

It should be the flagship of the Cultivators.

Taking down the Cultivators' flagship with the unparalleled combat ability of the Colossus, as a neat decapitation strategy, was perhaps the only opportunity for him to reserve the outcome of the battle.

Accelerated to the highest speed, the Lotus Buddha covered the thousands of kilometers in between within a breath. The emblems of the crowned fist on the two sides of the enemy's flagship were already clearly visible.

Suddenly, the gate below the Cultivators' flagship was slowly opened, and a cluster of things that looked like iron stars was released. With dazzling helical flames behind, it charged at Li Wuji without dodging.

"How dare you!"

The invisible mystic rays from the Lotus Buddha penetrated through the iron stars like a tide quickly but did not detect any intense spiritual waves.

Knowing that the target was not a Colossus, Li Wuji couldn't help but sneer. "This is just a cluster of garbage, yet you are bold enough to stand in my Colossus' way? How can you stop me with this thing? Ten seconds? Twenty? Or half a minute?"

While he was talking, the iron stars had dashed to Li Wuji's front and were slowly stretched out in space, growing into an iron giant hundreds of meters tall!

Although it was hundreds of meters tall, it was exactly like what Li Wuji said. All the cables, gears, and tubes were exposed to the vacuum, suggesting that it might've been jumbled up by wasted metals. It was essentially a human-shaped mountain of rubbish and did not seem to hold any combat ability.

"Are you 'Fist King' Lei Zonglie?"

Li Wuji sneered. Ripples of spiritual energy in the shape of lotus flowers spread out of the arms of his Colossus while he punched at the chest of the iron giant that was hundreds of meters tall. "Big but worthless!"

BOOM!

The tides of spiritual energy raised by the Lotus Buddha left waves in the shape of lotus flowers on the chest of the iron giant, piercing through the stomach unstoppably. Even the limbs that were loosely connected seemed about to break apart.

It seemed truly as "worthless" as Li Wuji declared.

However, before Li Wuji let out a smile of mockery, the iron giant hundreds of meters tall had exploded on its own!

Tremendous crystal bombs that were deeply buried in the chest were detonated, blowing out a highly-unstable magnetic field that swallowed Li Wuji's Lotus Buddha like a bloody mouth. The Colossus' scanning and Li Wuji's senses were greatly disturbed, making him blind and deaf for one second.

One second later, the vipers made of the remnants of the iron giant tied up the limbs of the Lotus Buddha.

There were also dozens of small spiritual puppets, which had been lurking inside the iron giant sordidly and now spread after the explosion, crawling on the Colossus and searching for the tiniest gap while the Lotus Buddha was overwhelmed.

Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi!

The fingers of those tiny spiritual puppets could turn into any random delicate tools. The moment they located a gap on the Colossus, they would immediately start cutting and digging, trying to break into the Colossus and block the tubes of spiritual energy and fuel, or even to enter the spiritual residence and catch Li Wuji directly.

Li Wuji had planned to perform the decapitation strategy on the Cultivators. Little did he expect that "Fist King" Lei Zonglie, who had an incredible life form, was also trying to crawl into his spiritual residence and perform the decapitation strategy on him!

Hardly had he come back to himself from the serial explosions when he found dozens of annoying bugs on his Colossus. Li Wuji was both exasperated and scared, realizing that he had been tricked by "Fist King" Lei Zonglie. The iron giant hundreds of meters tall was not the Fist King's real body at all but just an exaggerated disguise. One of those small spiritual puppets was his actual self!

"Damn it! It's truly as expected of the shameless Cultivators!"

Li Wuji bellowed while tides of spiritual energy were unleashed from the Lotus Buddha, trying to blow away or burn up the spiritual puppets that had crawled on his body.

He was partly successful. A couple of spiritual puppets were indeed burnt up into garbage and blown far away.

However, there were some other spiritual puppets that were made of unknown materials. Even the temperature of thousands of degrees was not enough to dysfunction them. After tearing apart the tiny cracks on the Colossus, they even extended sharp thorns from their "mouths" and stabbed in.

Astronomical information immediately flooded into the Lotus Buddha's mainframe crystal processor, which attacked the firewall and fought with Li Wuji over the control of the Colossus.

It was almost like the Fist King intended to occupy Li Wuji's Colossus for himself!

Caught unprepared by the enemy's weirdest tactics, Li Wuji was at a loss about what to do.

However, it took him only half a second before he realized what was going on. After a shriek, he controlled the crystal processor crazily and established new, temporary, and dynamic firewalls to block the Fist King's "self-awareness" or "soul" or "aggressive program".

Li Wuji's resistance was successful—for now.

The main crystal processor of a Colossus was of too high a level for the Fist King to hack so quickly.

But such resistance also drained all of the Colossus' computational ability, to the point that Li Wuji did not notice… another Colossus that had appeared behind him without a sound.

It was a bright gray, inconspicuous, and even somewhat weaselly Colossus, like a super-large iron mouse more than twenty meters tall.

No. The Colossus that had arc-shaped blades on the elbows and the toes was more ferocious and aggressive than a mouse. It was more like a velociraptor that was agile, swift, and highly belligerent in the primeval age.

The Colossus was exactly Boss Bai's mount, the Kleptomaniac!

Chapter 2456: All Is Settled

The Kleptomaniac was a Colossus that highlighted agility, swiftness, stealth, assassination, and other features and could almost be called the sleaziest Colossus in the entire universe.

The sleaziness was even vastly different from Li Yao's battle style.

No matter how Li Yao behaved in his daily life, or whether he could be somewhat…"cautious" when it came to strategic choices, his style was always open and straightforward in the head-on clashes.

The Kleptomaniac, on the other hand, looked like an invisible whirl of air. But whenever the wind passed by, it would steal something from the target.

The first time it passed the Lotus Buddha, the Kleptomaniac stole one piece of the breastplate.

The second time, it stole a power unit on the left rear of the enemy's torso.

The third time, it even stole the entire left shank of the Lotus Buddha!

In terms of combat ability, Boss Bai—Bai Xingjian, who had broken into the Divinity Transformation Stage decades ago in the Star Glory Federation and who had the battle experiences of two lives after the soul of "Pirate Potentate Bai Xinghe" was melted with the soul of "Supreme Pirate Yan Xinjian"—was far stronger than Li Wuji in the first place.

Even if the two of them were in a duel, Li Wuji wouldn't have been a match for him.

But of course, in a battle between two experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage, Li Wuji could've always fled even if he couldn't defeat Boss Bai.

However, there was still a Fist King here.

The Fist King was not a human being. Fundamentally, he was a "permanent battle puppet with auto-thinking and auto-fighting abilities". Although he was not entirely the same as the "information lives" such as Xiaoming and Wenwen, they were still quite similar.

The Fist King's real self was not any of the spiritual puppets that were clinging to the Lotus Buddha right now. Instead, he was the collection of all the spiritual puppets. He had even crammed into the Lotus Buddha's database and operation system through the invasion of the spiritual puppets, significantly disturbing the Lotus Buddha's battle.

It was some sort of…"possession" of a machine on another machine!

When the terrifying Boss Bai and the weird Fist King attacked Li Wuji together, the marquess of the Imperium truly had nowhere to run to at all!

For a moment, he was blown by tides of spiritual energy back and forth while the world was swirling around him. All the pores on his body were spurting out blood, staining the dustless spiritual residence into a mess.

The operation menu on the virtual light beam inside the Colossus, however, spread out fuzzy ripples that minced all the data and instructions. His Colossus was quickly getting out of his control, as if a sharp blade had stabbed into his nerves that were connected to the Colossus, separating his soul from the Colossus brutally.

Huala!

Under the relentless attacks of the two experts, Li Wuji's defense completely collapsed. The limbs of the Lotus Buddha were wide open while it cramped crazily.

The Kleptomaniac took the opportunity to crouch into a giant iron ball and crashed into its chest heavily, both breaking into the bridge of Eternal Spring with a trail of sparks and lightning.

The blast caused by the collision of two Colossi was like a thousand crystal bombs exploding at the same time. The bridge of Eternal Spring seemed to have just suffered a tornado and was completely paralyzed.

Shua! Shua!

The blades on the elbows of the Kleptomaniac crossed and pierced into the Lotus Buddha's spiritual residence, narrowly passing Li Wuji's body.

The sharp edge stimulated every nerve inside Li Wuji's body like a gloomy wind from hell.

Part of the super-alloy spiritual puppets that made up the Fist King's body also crawled into the spiritual residence through the wide-open breastplate of the Lotus Buddha. All kinds of blades and guns were rolled out of their bodies and aimed at Li Wuji's temples and chest, making a silent threat.

Li Wuji had been dizzy under the blast. When he realized what was going on, he had lost all chances to fight back.

He was angry, anxious, and ashamed. His head was blank for a long time, and he vomited aloud in the end!

…

On the ground of the Blue Sky Market, the resistance of Li Wuji's half fleet was still going on.

The Big Bai Pirate Gang merely controlled the firing points around to prevent them from fleeing, but they did not show any intention of attacking the place.

Now that the Big Bai Pirate Gang did not intend to attack, the remaining troop inside, which had just been bombarded by heavy cannons and lost touch with their leader Li Yuanzhen, would certainly not leave their base recklessly either.

They were still under the illusion that Li Wuji would notice something wrong and reinforce them with the intact fleet if they could persist for a while longer.

However, their beautiful wish was shattered by the cruel reality.

While they were in an impasse against the Big Bai Pirate Gang in the hidden fortresses and the firing points, a twisted, broken Colossus flew in from the manmade atmosphere and stopped above them.

It was exactly Li Wuji's Colossus, the Lotus Buddha.

The soldiers naturally recognized their master's Colossus. Thinking that Li Wuji had returned to help them, they were about to cheer and counterattack when they noticed that something was not right. The Colossus was blackened and ragged, as if it had just been savaged. Was it nearly as clean and unstained as Li Wuji usually kept it?

"What happened? How did our lord's Colossus fall into such a miserable state?"

The soldiers of the troop looked at each other in bewilderment, all feeling creepy.

"Surrender now!"

The remaining half breastplate was kicked away from inside, and Boss Bai and the Fist King leaped out of the spiritual residence and stood above everyone magnificently.

Between the two of them was the swollen, tied, and bleeding Li Wuji who couldn't have looked more terrible.

In a trance, Li Wuji was too devastated to face his soldiers, but the blades in Boss Bai and the Fist King's hands were crossed on his neck, forcing him to raise his head so that the soldiers could see his face clearly.

"Surrender now!"

Boss Bai shouted again, "Your master has been caught by us alive. This man does not have the determination of the Immortal Cultivators at all. He did not dare to kill himself when he was offered an opportunity to. Are you going to serve and even die for such a man?

"I am unwilling to kill you because you are all the best specialists to maneuver starships. If I want, I can surround this place and summon all the starships here to bombard you for a good five hours. Who among you can survive or even keep your body intact?

"Surrender now. This is the third warning, and I will not repeat it for a fourth time. You will either surrender or die!"

Hardly had he finished his sentence when swirls spread out on the manmade atmosphere and daunting starships barged in, further adding to the weight of Boss Bai's announcement.

What was pulled by a few small starships was exactly "Eternal Spring", which had been crippled and lost basic mobility.

The flagship was disabled, the commander was captured alive, the direct superior went missing, and the enemy had surrounded them. With the battle coming to this point, it was not disgraceful for any troop to choose to surrender.

Even if it were a troop of the Cultivators, it would have to surrender now.

The regular army of the four Kurfürst families, especially the young officers, the ordinary soldiers, and the hominoid slaves in it, was unsteady in the first place because of the turmoil of the reformists. The only reason they resisted hard without retreating was that the enemies were humble and lowly space pirates. They were merely supported by the sense of honor.

But right now, when they looked at the most humiliating appearance of Li Wuji, what were they dying for exactly?

Clang! Clang!

With complicated sighs, the soldiers dropped the weapons that were stained by sweat and blood in their hands, surrendering to Boss Bai and the Fist King.

It was not until this moment that the regular space pirates in the other twenty-eight pirate gangs, who had the greatest advantage in number, vaguely realized that something was not right.

"This… This can't be right. Didn't they say that Boss Bai had joined hands with Marquess Yongchun to regroup us? Why is Boss Bai fighting Marquess Yongchun's troop?"

The space pirates looked at each other in confusion, having no idea what was going on.

It was one thing to be swallowed by Marquess Yongchun who represented the four Kurfürst families, but it would be too absurd if they were only swallowed by Boss Bai and even forced to face the dual fury of the reformists and the four families together with him!

"Boss Bai! The treacherous, shameless, and insane Boss Bai!"

Many space pirates had finally realized what was going on. They wanted to pick up their weapons again, but it was too late.

Their magical equipment and their crystal suits had long been taken away by the soldiers of the Big Bai Pirate Gang. They had also been asked to move to the lower places that did not have any cover. Their enemies were watching them coldly from the commanding heights and the firing points with obvious contempt.

Besides, more and more starships were tearing apart the manmade atmosphere and breaking the gravity sphere of the Blue Sky Market from the sky. They were parked above them like iron beasts.

Many space pirates whose eyes were keen had recognized the emblem of the crowned fist on those starships. Realizing that it was the fleet of Cultivators who had been unpredictably active in the peripheral worlds of the Imperium in the recent year, they couldn't help but feel greatly shocked.

"Boss Bai is joining hands with the Cultivators and the terrible 'Fist King' Lei Zonglie!"

Such an understanding smashed the last backbone of the space pirates, who intended to resist, like a thousand-kilogram hammer.

The starships of the Cultivators' fleet were all stolen from the Imperium's army or even the space pirates. Some were even modified from armed mining starships or merchant starships. They were ragged, and their tonnage and firepower were below average.

But starships were starships after all. They were more than enough to deal with the "ants" that were scattered on the ground.

One wrong move, and they'd lose the entire game!

Realizing that they had stepped into the most vicious trap since the very beginning, those space pirates could only sigh and accept the ruling of Boss Bai and the Fist King with their eyes closed.

Just like that, in less than twenty-four hours, the Blue Sky Market, the comprehensive business and trade center with the most abundant resources, and the most advanced maintenance facilities with the best upgrade and supply abilities changed three masters in a row.

At first, it belonged to the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. Then, it was the space pirates and the nobles' armies. Eventually, it fell to the hands of the federal space pirates and the Cultivators!

Chapter 2457: Boss Bai's Play

"Hooray! Hooray! Hooray!"

When the Big Bai Pirate Gang and the fleet of the Cultivators successfully joined, and when the flag of white mouse twisting its bottom, the flag of Starlight, and the flag of the crowned fist fluttered in the burning sky of the Blue Sky Market together, all the space pirates from the Star Glory Federation and the Cultivators of Starlight began to cheer from the bottom of their hearts.

It was needless to say the central members of the Big Bai Pirate Gang.

Although they had almost been invincible in the hundreds of fierce battles after they jumped to the Imperium from the federation under the leadership of Boss Bai, and they had returned with loads of trophies every time, they had never conquered such a resourceful and sturdy city as the Blue Sky Market before, nor had they ever crushed a whole fleet of the regular army!

Such an unprecedented triumph was not only the glory of the space pirates but also the glory of the federation from the edge of the cosmos.

Even though they were humble space pirates who might have been in jail for decades in the federation, the federation was their homeland after all, where their family and friends still stayed. The connection between them and the federation could never be excised.

The "bumpkins" from the edge of the cosmos, after barging into the splendid worlds at the center of the cosmos, were still having a great time and even turning the worlds upside down.

Hehe. In terms of technologies and advancement of civilization, the wealthy places at the center of the cosmos were naturally better, but when it came to brutality and toughness, it was certainly the vicious folks from the rough lands that were more fearsome!

One day, the brutality of the federal space pirates would be known by everybody at the center of the cosmos, and the very first flash that illuminated their name would be the battle today, where the Blue Sky Market was conquered, twenty-eight pirate gangs were swallowed, and Li Wuji's entire fleet was crushed!

Boss Bai did not lie to them at all. They had a good chance to be the freest people ever and the greatest space pirates in the universe!

It was even more needless to say the Cultivators from the Imperium.

During the hundreds of years before today, they had been struggling on the brink of destruction, and it was already their best if they could make a voice in their fury. Even the magnificent uprisings in the mines and the farms often died as abruptly as they took place. They would easily be crushed when the regular army of the Imperium came to suppress them.

Little had they ever dreamed that they could conquer a gigantic business and trade center or shatter an elite fleet of the regular army with their barrages. Even a marquess of the Imperium had been captured alive by them!

More importantly, after the Blue Sky Market was occupied, they had earned sufficient assets. The training resources in the trophies would be enough to help many of them to break into new levels and significantly improve their combat ability.

The starships of the space pirates and the regular army that had been captured could also be maintained and upgraded with the facilities in the Blue Sky Market, comprehensively upgrading their fleet that was as ragged as beggars right now.

In such a way, the fire of Cultivators that had been suppressed for a thousand years had the hope of being reignited and spread out again!

How could they not be cheering in euphoria?

The whole Blue Sky Market had turned into an ocean of joy. The federal space pirates and the Cultivators of the Imperium, the weird combination, sang and danced merrily. Naturally, in light of what happened to the space pirates and the regular army of the Imperium, alcohol was certainly not an option. The victors could only wave their flags vehemently to let out their excitement and ecstasy.

Below the wonderful flags, two partners that had been separated for a long time met again. They were exactly Han Te and Liu Li, the young man and the girl from the Martial Meritocrats Sector.

The two fledgling and vigorous teenagers embraced each other tightly.

The whole world seemed to have disappeared, and they were all there was.

"Han Te!"

"Liu Li!"

The two pairs of eyes that were staring at each other were both filled with tears. Reviewing their extraordinary experiences in the last half a year, they both felt that it was almost a dream.

When Han Te, Liu Li, "Fist King" Lei Zonglie, and Gao Dashan, the experienced captain from Starlight, were rescued from the main planet of the Martial Meritocrats Sector by Li Yao, the Martial Meritocrats Sector was still occupied by the Immortal Cultivators.

Having no alternatives, Li Yao sacrificed himself to distract all the Immortal Cultivators, allowing whatever left of Starlight to run to the edge of the fourth quadrant at the edge of the Imperium to search for Boss Bai.

Despite Li Yao's confident declaration, Han Te and Liu Li did not have much faith in Boss Bai, the head of space pirates. They had heard a thing or two about space pirates and knew that they were all ferocious men who did not have any integrity or credit. Besides, even the strongest space pirates couldn't have been a match for the regular army of the Immortal Cultivators.

This Boss Bai did not know them at all. How could he have accepted such a crushed team of Cultivators recklessly, which would only be a burden for him?

However, they had no choices back then and could only turn to the last resort. Through all difficulties, thanks to the leadership and protection of the Fist King, they finally arrived at the periphery of the Imperium and got in touch with Boss Bai by chance.

Out of their expectation, after observing their tiny team up and down for a long time, Boss Bai did not turn them down and even gave them a piece of surprising advice.

"You, the Cultivators, are too eye-catching. If you stay in the Big Bai Pirate Gang, it will be easy for the Immortal Cultivators to notice you, which will be troublesome for the both of us.

"However, for the sake that… you are Cultivators, I will not watch you die without doing anything. Here's a solution for you.

"My Big Bai Pirate Gang has sailed and raged in the peripheral worlds of the Imperium and developed to what it is today all because of speed and professionalism. What I've been training with are the best soldiers, who use the latest warships that have the highest speed, fiercest firepower, and best equipment.

"However, we often captured a lot of obsolete, heavy starships when we looted the Immortal Cultivators' ports or attacked other pirate gangs. I would never take those burdens away, but it seemed a shame to just abandon them.

"As it happens, I've just done a few big jobs, and I have a lot of such old and awkward starships as well as weapons that are no better than garbage in my hands. If you do not despise them, I'll offer those gears to you for you to develop your fleet of Cultivators.

"In the meantime, I can send a batch of tutors to you. They are all seasoned space pirates who have the most battle experiences. They will teach you how to establish and command a real fleet.

"What I'm saying is that the Big Bai Pirate Gang and the fleet of Cultivators can play different roles in cooperation, one in the light and the other in the dark.

"Specifically speaking, I have a lot of intelligence regarding the pirate gangs that are on a job, but if I loot them too often, I may be hated by the people of my trade. So, it will be better if you attack them when they have just done a great business, with their arsenal empty and their warehouses full!

"Or rather, it wouldn't matter if I intercept their trophies myself as long as you take the blame afterward. All in all, we will simply observe our agreement. I will have the latest warships, whereas the other starships will be yours. The rest of the assets will be divided evenly. Consider it a favor from me to support the development of the Cultivators in the Imperium.

"One other thing. On the vast resource planets and in the mines and farms at the periphery of the Imperium, tremendous precious intelligence and human resources are hidden. I'm hoping to work on those aspects, establish a few bases, and transform most people at the edge of the Imperium into my sympathizers and supporters, or at least people who are willing to do business with me.

"But it won't be appropriate for me to do that. Taller trees catch more wind. What exactly does a filthy space pirate want to do when he is winning the people's support? But it is most suitable for your 'Starlight' to do that because you have been doing that since the beginning. Most of the ordinary people have heard your name before and rather trust you.

"All in all, our two parties can pretend to be at loggerheads or even fight each other hard, but there is in fact a lot of room for cooperation and complementation between us. If we play our roles well, even if the regime of the Imperium is not changed, we can at least significantly expand our living space. How about it? Are you willing to do that?"

Now that Boss Bai had put it in such a way, what else could the desperate survivors of Starlight say? Of course they would do it!

What happened later was exactly what Boss Bai predicted.

At first, he offered a batch of outdated starships, which were still usable, to the survivors of Starlight. He then sent experienced space pirates to help train the soldiers of Starlight, teaching Starlight how to ambush, move, and loot other people when they were in trouble almost hand in hand. He also worked together with Starlight to swallow quite a few infamous pirate gangs, allowing Starlight to upgrade their gears and develop rapidly.

After that, with the mainframe crystal processors with high computational abilities, "Fist King" Lei Zonglie's combat and commanding abilities soared. He was even stronger than Boss Bai in terms of hacking and cyberwarfare. The two parties truly became an inseparable whole.

Today, they had conquered the Blue Sky Market together and swallowed twenty-eight pirate gangs and one regular fleet when they were terribly outnumbered, announcing that they were a force at the edge of the Imperium not to be underestimated.

Everything! Everything was truly like a dream!

"Liu Li, snap on my forehead and see if we are dreaming. I don't want to find myself suffering on the Land of Sins after I wake up later!"

Han Te said to Liu Li with a smile.

Liu Li curled her finger and snapped at Han Te's forehead so hard that Han Te jumped to his feet and then lowered his back while covering his head. Even his tears were almost running out.

While wiping his tears, he burst into laughter. "It hurts. This is not a dream. We've truly won. If we can conquer the Blue Sky Market today, maybe it won't take long before we return to the Martial Meritocrats Sector with an overwhelming fleet and smash the Land of Sins into pieces!"

The young man from the Land of Sins couldn't forget his disastrous homeland.

Of course, what he couldn't forget more was the little sister before him who had escaped from the Land of Sins together with him.

Chapter 2458: Big Sisters and Little Sister

The two partners who had been born and grown up together on the Land of Sins went on separate ways after they came to the boundless space.

The blood of ambitions and restlessness had always been flowing inside Han Te's veins. He was very curious about the space pirates' unexpected, unrestrained, and somewhat mysterious and romantic life. When he heard that the fleet of the Cultivators was in need of a liaison officer in the Big Bai Pirate Gang to coordinate the cooperation, he volunteered to take the complicated and arduous task.

After he came to the Big Bai Pirate Gang, not only had he done an excellent job in his post, he also gained access to a lot of other posts, until he encountered the job of "artilleryman" as if it were the fate of life.

The "artilleryman" was much more than controlling the heavy cannons on the starships to attack. The "fire control officer", a senior profession to the post, was responsible for the distribution of firepower and the rhythm of attack of a starship or even an entire fleet.

Even the most commonly-seen mass-produced attack starship had almost a hundred cannons large and small. For a coalition fleet made of hundreds of starships, their firepower combined could be astronomical.

How to configure the complicated firepower, how to adjust the shooting angle, how to change the rhythm of attack, whether to bombard stormily or to barrage unhurriedly, whether to focus on one point or to shoot the entire area… The answers to the sophisticated questions often lay in the hands of the fire control officer.

In most of the deep-sky fleets, the fire control officer was the second or third person only below the highest commander and was equaled to the chief of staff of the fleet.
