The Fist King was silent for a moment before he replied, "I have guessed your identity, but it is not my business. After all, I'm not on the Immortal Cultivators' side. Your identity is none of my concerns."

"Oh?"

Boss Bai smiled warmly. "Why don't you tell me about it?"

"You and Li Yao obviously have known each other for a long time, and both of you are in the Divinity Transformation Stage. Even a regular expert in the Divinity Transformation Stage can only be raised by an enormous force that is famous nationwide, let alone two, both with a Colossus. It is impossible that such a force is never heard of before!"

The Fist King said, "Your flagship may deceive common starship specialists with the modifications and disguises, but it cannot trick me. The style is obviously the opposite of the mainstream of the Imperium.

"Also, as a ferocious space pirate, you took in the Cultivators of Starlight without any hesitation, at the risk of becoming an enemy of all the Immortal Cultivators in the Imperium. It was utterly unreasonable, or at least not just because of 'friendship' and 'compassion'.

"In conclusion, you must be from a certain powerful force outside of the Imperium, of course not the Alliance of the Holy Covenant, but a vigorous country of Cultivators.

"As for the reason why you came to the Imperium, you are here as a spy, special agent, or vanguard, aren't you?"

"What a good guess."

Boss Bai burst into laughter. "Look, don't we have even more reasons to fight each other until one of us dies in order to keep our own secrets safe?"

"Wait!"

The Fist King said in a hurry, "I don't care who you are at all. All I want to do is to carry out my survival and strengthening policies. I never intend to inform the authorities of the Imperium. Why do we have to fight at such a critical moment? Do you not know that a life-and-death battle will significantly lower our odds of survival, and we may die together? Isn't survival the most important thing?"

"You are such a chatterbox."

Boss Bai extended his little finger and picked his ear. "Fist King, if you truly want to be a human, let me tell you one thing—for a human, survival is never the most important thing. Never!"

Hardly had he concluded his sentence when Boss Bai laughed crazily and waved his hands, throwing the debris of starships weighing dozens of tons in the midair at the Fist King overwhelmingly.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

The garbage hit where the Fist King was standing like burning shooting stars, but the Fist King was quick enough to jump to the sky by stepping on the remnants before he landed on Boss Bai's back.

"Boss Bai, you're out of your mind!"

The Fist King's logic judgments could not capture or infer what Boss Bai was thinking at all.

"Wow!"

Boss Bai's eyes almost popped out. "It's such an obvious fact that Zuo Tianying and Li Wuji discovered a long time ago. You didn't know it just now, did you?"

He slashed his hand out. As the spiritual energy surged out, countless tiny pieces were attached to Boss Bai's arm into an extensible sharp blade more than ten meters long. It then split apart while it was spinning, covering the critical parts all over the Fist King's body.

The Fist King moved in the storm of blades as agilely as a swallow that was hiding every raindrop. He was even able to activate his Cosmos Rings unhurriedly in the shadow of the blades and retrieved almost thirty spiritual puppets from there!

When the storm of blades died down, what stood before Boss Bai were thirty identical spiritual puppets. There was no telling which one of them was the real Fist King. Or maybe all of them were the Fist King!

"Such a meaningless battle will only have a lose-lose result. I do not want to fight at all."

Surrounding Boss Bai, the thirty spiritual puppets let out the same indifferent and rational voice, but the rationality in the voice was fading. "But if you insist on a life-and-death battle, I have no choice but to satisfy you. At the very least, I will only lose, but you will die, Boss Bai!"

Chapter 2466: I Want to Be Your Bro

Hardly had he concluded his sentence when thirty spiritual puppets attacked Boss Bai at the same time. Some of the spiritual puppets had sharp blades ejecting out of their arms, some condensed solid shields in front of their companions, and the spiritual puppets that were thoroughly protected opened their shoulders, breastplates, and abdomens, revealing the densest launch systems that were glowing!

The waves of attacks in the perfect rhythm were like the scourge of infinite tides. The tornado of destruction was boosted to the maximum since the second it was born, consuming Boss Bai completely.

Boss Bai's wild laughter, however, was louder and louder in the metal torrents. "Yes, yes, that's it! It's been such a long time since I last enjoyed the ecstasy on the brink of death!"

BOOM!

A couple of spiritual puppets that were close to Boss Bai were blown away by his violent spiritual energy. One of the spiritual puppets was even grabbed by Boss Bai by the head. As the most powerful electric arcs penetrated its iron skull from the gaps, it was cramping hard before all the chips and rune arrays inside its body were overloaded and burnt up, turning it into a twisted shell with black smoke and white flames popping up.

"Is this the best you can do?"

Boss Bai tossed the burning, forsaken puppet away and walked toward the other twenty-nine spiritual puppets, his baggy eyes and his rising lips almost kissing. "You are insulting me and raising my fury, Fist King!"

The Fist King was silent for a moment. Then, the twenty-nine spiritual puppets were suddenly hastened into twenty-nine metal hurricanes. The blurred shadows they left behind condensed such immense spiritual waves that they were almost surrounding Boss Bai like an army of hundreds of spiritual puppets.

"This is more like it."

Boss Bai closed his fingers and unleashed thousands of electric arcs, cutting the gap of the army of puppets randomly. Weirdly, although he did not aim at the Fist King's bodies and only slashed at the air between the spiritual puppets, countless puppets were squeaking and slowing down.

"Ha, this is your weakness."

Boss Bai smiled. "I don't know what life form you are exactly, but beings like you seem to attach more importance to the transmission of information than to the cold shells.

"Attacking one of the shells in your gigantic army is meaningless, but as long as your network is disrupted and the information transmission among your different 'units' is cut off, your vigor and combat ability will be greatly reduced, and you may even be… destroyed!"

The moment he said "destroyed", four powerful waves that were invisible to the naked eye suddenly spread out in the forsaken port. One piece of giant magical equipment was activated in every corner of the port. It was a huge magnet powered by crystals, which was supposed to attract and ship massive metal containers but had turned into traps that looked like swamps for metals after Boss Bai's modification!

The twenty-nine spiritual puppets that made up the Fist King's body were more than 90% metal. Although they had all been demagnetized, the special magnets for port were still too powerful and had too high an attraction force, and the Fist King was greatly affected.

For a moment, a lot of spiritual puppets were cramping as if drunk because of the interferences of the magnetic fields. The other spiritual puppets were still acting normally, but at the same time, they were also slowing down. Boss Bai took the opportunity to attack and destroy more than ten spiritual puppets in a row.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

The Fist King finally determined the coordinates of the four giant magnetic units. The four crystal cannons opened fire nonstop, wrecking all of them in one second.

But the moment the magnetic fields disappeared, Boss Bai retrieved a set of new magical equipment and armor from his Cosmos Ring. It was not a crystal suit controlled by a crystal processor, but the ancient magical equipment and armor from the Ancient Sages Sector that were crude and solely controlled by the human brain.

"Fist King, do you know why I insist on fighting you?"

After putting on the ancient armor, and when dozens of vintage flying swords hovered around him, Boss Bao looked like a reincarnation of an ancient god of war. He stabbed the spiritual puppets on his opposite side, leaving thousands of holes on them like a storm. "I've been in the Imperium for a long time, and yet what I encountered at the peripheral worlds were all guys like 'Zuo Tianying' or 'Li Wuji'. Damn it. They were utterly unqualified to be my opponents!

"It's been a long time since I last found an opponent that is worth fighting with all my strength. I am very 'hungry'. Insanely hungry! Do you know the feeling? Have you ever tasted the feeling? If you do, stop reserving anything and feed the real you to my mouth!"

While roaring, Boss Bai chopped all the other spiritual puppets into parts as if they were harmless vegetables with the ancient sabers and swords unstoppably.

Since he went on a rampage, the Fist King had been quiet. He did not say anything even when the last spiritual puppet was twisted and torn into pieces by Boss Bai, almost like…

He had vanished.

However, while Boss Bai ravaged the spiritual puppets that could not fight back at all, a hundred invisible ripples spread out in the sky.

When the last spiritual puppet on the ground collapsed, the ripples in the sky lost their camouflage and turned out to be a hundred heavy battle puppets that looked even more advanced and aggressive. Scorching crystal cannons were on their shoulders, and their two hundred iron hands that were straightly aimed at Boss Bai had dazzling balls of light that were being condensed.

"Your brain has been overloaded."

The Fist King's cold voice came out from the broadcast rune arrays of the hundred heavy battle puppets at the same time. "You probably need a reboot."

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

The hundred battle puppets unleashed a firepower that was almost equal to a heavy arsenal warship. Hundreds of tiny pillars of light congregated into a waterfall of destruction and fell from the sky, drowning, swallowing, and pressing Boss Bai on the ground. The pillars of light dug into the ground for more than ten meters, almost pushing Boss Bai all the way through the entire port.

What was even more incredible was the Fist King's delicate control over the dispersion of spiritual energy. The shocking destruction inside the forsaken port could barely be felt hundreds of meters away from the place. Even if part of the sound and visual effects spread out, it would only be like a regular "practice" between two experts.

The glowing pillars of light lasted a whole minute.

The ground in the spaceport was melted with a giant hole that was about five meters in diameter and immeasurably deep, a result when the Fist King tried to control his damage. The edge and the inside of the hole were both scorching dark red, and the melted iron was dripping like mud.

Boss Bai's spiritual waves and life information couldn't be detected anymore.

The Fist King, however, daren't be easy. He still powered all the heavy battle puppets to lock onto the hole on the ground tightly once they descended inch by inch.

Very soon, chuckles echoed below the hole. A colorful, ragged silky shirt, stuck to a cable tube, was raised out of the hole and waved as a white flag.

"I surrender. So incredible and awesome! I surrender!"

Boss Bai laughed crazily inside the cave. "I've finally warmed up my old bones and enjoyed myself today. Let's stop fighting. If we fight on, our subordinates will notice what is going on here, and it will truly be 'lose-lose'!"

The Fist King was stunned. The hundred spiritual puppets hummed at the same time, "Boss Bai, what are you up to exactly?

"You are concealing your traces so neatly that even I can barely lock onto you.

"In the high-temperature attack just now, you were even capable enough of protecting your clothes from being burnt into ashes. Your clothes are made of the most regular silk that is easiest to burn, aren't they? But they were not burnt up, which suggested that you did not suffer any fatal injury at all.

"You started the battle, and you are the one surrendering right now. What do you want exactly?"

"Don't be angry, and don't be rash. Speaking of which, beings like you should be incapable of being angry or rash, and you can keep absolute rationality all the time, right?"

Like a blackened mouse, Boss Bai crawled out of the hole that had a temperature of tens of thousands of degrees. He lay on the ground and stretched out all his limbs. Watching the slowly descending heavy puppets in the sky, he spurted out a mouthful of smoke and grinned. "Like I said just now, it's been a long time since I last found an opponent who was worth fighting with all my strength. People like 'Zuo Tianying' or 'Li Wuji' are not nearly gratifying. I was almost crazy after holding myself back for such a long time, and I thought that you were the only person that I could practice with!

"Besides, I think you are a good man. Really. Fist King, you are truly a good man. I'm sincerely hoping to get along with you, not just to be friends but also to be bros. Since we are to be bros, we naturally need to know each other better. For people like you and me, is there a better way to increase our knowledge of each other than a gratifying battle?"

Controlling the hundred spiritual puppets to thud on the ground heavily, the Fist King said coldly, "So, you did not want to kill me but only meant to practice with me?"

"I'm a space pirate. A space pirate never does anything that promises no returns. Like you said, it would be 'lose-lose' if we fought a life-and-death battle. Why would I kill you?"

Lying on the ground comfortably, Boss Bai looked at the guy with a smile. "Besides, am I capable of killing you? Are those hundred heavy battle puppets your real body? Can I kill you by destroying them?

"Or maybe, is your real self hiding inside a certain starship in the fleet of the Cultivators, not necessarily the flagship but perhaps an unattractive rapid warship? If I truly reveal the maliciousness that you cannot resist, you can turn into a starship in the blink of an eye and broke out of the siege, running away immediately. Am I right?"

The Fist King pondered for a moment, and the heavy puppets bowed at Boss Bai at the same time. He then controlled one of the puppets to support Boss Bai to get back on his feet.

There was a hint of admiration in the Fist King's cold voice. "I thought that someone like Li Yao already deserved to be called a monster. I didn't know until today that Boss Bai is the real monster. When Li Yao is compared to you—well, although he saved my life before, I can't lie—it's like the difference between an earthworm and a boa."

Chapter 2467: What's Space Pirate? What's Craziness?

Boss Bai chuckled and simply said without being modest, "Are you indirectly complimenting yourself? Even a monster like me is not confident to completely destroy you and has to 'surrender' to you and regard you as a bro!"

The Fist King hesitated and said, "Are you not scared of me, Boss Bai?"

Boss Bai sniffed. "Do I need to be scared of you?"

"Not that 'scared', but 'highly vigilant'."

Thinking for a moment, the Fist King said, "According to my calculation, if my real identity is exposed, a lot of people will be highly wary and scared of me and try their best to eliminate me. It's difficult for normal people to accept the unknown beings like me, but Boss Bai doesn't care about it at all and is still talking to me lightheartedly. Why?"

"Only the weak are scared of the unknown; the strong will only open their arms to the unknown, either embracing it or conquering it."

Staring at the Fist King, Boss Bai said, "There are bound to be all kinds of weird life forms in the vast universe, and the cooperation, competition, symbiosis and destruction among the life forms are natural, too. Faced with such a cruel and dangerous universe, the most sensible choice is to build up oneself through all possibilities, instead of eliminating the signs of threats in fear every day—there are simply too many signs of threats. How can you eliminate all of them?

"All in all, the real cause of destruction can only be your weakness, not the formidability of your enemy. That's what I've believed in since I embarked on the journey of a space pirate.

"Besides, it is possible that a terrible 'flood' on the other side of the universe has destroyed countless civilizations including the wizardly civilization. Who knows whether or not the torrent of destruction will attack the world of Cultivation one day?

"In that case, it will be the doom of our world. When the doom comes, we will all be animals fleeing from the dark forest in panic. Isn't it hilarious if we are still hostile and scared of each other?"

The Fist King was in deep thought. "That does make sense…"

Sparks of hope shined in Boss Bai's eyes as he extended his hand to the Fist King. "Then, would you like to cooperate with me?"

The Fist King said, "We have been cooperating, to resist the Imperium."

"The Imperium? Hahahaha, the Imperium!"

Boss Bai's laughter was even crazier. "Do you think that a tiny Imperium, or the tiny three thousand Sectors, can trap Boss Bai and be my ultimate prey?"

The Fist King was stunned. "The three thousand Sectors are the maximum of the known universe. Even the vast universe is not enough to be Boss Bai's battlefield?"

"No, no, far from enough!"

The sparks in Boss Bai's eyes gradually burnt into the most glamorous fire. Opening his thin arms at the artificial sky that seemed to be bleeding because of the starships hovering there, he moaned hungrily, "Did you know, Fist King, that I was rather disappointed when I just came to the center of the cosmos even though I was faced with the terrifying enemies such as the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance?

"At that time, I thought that I had seen through the entire cosmos, and there was nothing worth plundering and conquering apart from the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance. The best I could do was to explore the mysteries of the primeval age afterward.

"There was a while when I was confused and lethargic about everything. My ambition was to be the greatest space pirate in history, but even the greatest space pirate couldn't have done any greater job than stealing a country, which had been done by countless men of the time such as Blackstar the Great. If I were to do the same, it would be like biting the bones that had been bitten a hundred times by the predecessors. Would there be any fun in it at all? And would I be remembered by this civilization or this universe billions of years later?

"Hehe, looting the royal palace of the Imperium of True Human Beings, robbing the Covenant Alliance of their ultimate treasures, and exploring the mausoleums of the Supreme Emperor and even Pangu and Nuwa. They did sound awesome, but getting to the bottom of it, were the items in the royal palace and the mausoleums the wealth of the creatures in our world in the first place? The wealth was here all the time. Even if it fell to my hands, it would merely be changing a place, and nothing would be added at all.

"Showing off in one's own world and looting everything, would that be any different from a wastrel who stole and lost all the savings of his parents? Would that be expected of the 'greatest space pirate' at all?"

The Fist King sounded more or less surprised. "Then, what is 'the greatest space pirate' in Boss Bai's mind?"

"Only the worthless wastrels would boss around in their own home and steal the savings of their ancestors. The real capable guys should naturally march outside and steal other family's things!"

Boss Bai smiled. "The existence of the wizardly civilization was like the most brilliant axe that chopped my head, opening my eyes and allowing me to see a new world!

"Wandering around the three thousand Sectors and looting the Cultivators and the Immortal Cultivators is just a child's play. The real great space pirates should pilot thousands of warships, break the barrier of void and desolation, and attack the worlds where the wizardly civilization and other alien civilizations are at!

"Well, although the wizardly civilization that I discovered was probably long destroyed, who knows whether or not other wizardly civilizations or even more bizarre civilizations are on the other side of the infinite universe?

"Driving the warships of our world, commanding millions of warriors, and boosting billions of flying swords, we will arrive before the wizards, magicians, envoys of gods and destroy their altars, burn their temples, seduce their saintesses, slay their beasts, and bring the treasures of the wizardly world back to the Cultivation world. That is what the space pirates from the Cultivation world should do, right?"

Even when the hundred crystal processors of the Fist King were calculating the same time, he still couldn't figure out Boss Bai's ambition. "Yes…"

"No. Even that is not enough. Not enough at all!"

Boss Bai picked up the "white flag" that was stuck to the cable tube a moment ago and put it on his body again, but he kept it unbuttoned and simply allowed the hot steam to rise and blow the ragged shirt backward in fluttering sounds, revealing his skinny chest and his back that was as straight as a spear.

He opened his fingers and extended them at the sky, as if he were piercing through the boundary of the three-dimensional universe and looting the infinite treasures in the four-dimensional space. With a daydreaming expression, he roared crazily, "Even if we eat up the wizardly civilization, we will still be restrained in the swamp of the three-dimensional universe. Since I am the greatest space pirate, is it really something to rob the three-dimensional carbon-based lives? Who knows whether or not we can find a way to break into the four-dimensional universe and pillage those four-dimensional lives?

"Hehe, gods from the four-dimensional space, the supreme existences that dominate the progress of the civilization of mankind… Damn it, I feel like throwing up whenever I hear it. One day, I will let the scumbags who call themselves gods know that the people in the Cultivation world are different and that nothing can possibly crawl over our head and blatantly manipulate our fate. Nothing! Nothing! Nothing!"

Pa!

One of the spiritual puppets that made up the Fist King's body couldn't handle the complicated calculation that exceeded the maximum and collapsed.

"Have you really lost your mind, Boss Bai?"

The Fist King's remaining puppets exclaimed at the same time, "The advancement and formidability of the high-dimensional lives are beyond the imagination of the low-dimensional lives. You cannot threaten the high-dimensional lives at all!"

"Don't underestimate ourselves, Fist King. Life is too amazing. Formidability and weakness, advanced and underdeveloped, they cannot be gauged by the same standard."

Boss Bai laughed both crazily and solemnly, "Viruses are primitive and weak compared with humans, aren't they? But human beings haven't annihilated all the viruses when our civilization is so developed. Even in the most thriving days of the Pangu civilization, countless individuals still died miserably without a cure after contracting a virus!

"They are almost like rats. Rats seem weak, but in the hundred thousand years of history of mankind, too many creatures on too many planets have been destroyed by pestilence and its variations!

"Viruses can kill human beings. Why can't human beings, the insignificant viruses, ants, and viruses from the three-dimensional universe kill the seemingly more advanced and strong beings in the higher-dimensional universes?

"My ultimate dream is to be such a 'universal mouse' that will gnaw at the barrier between different universes and crawl to infinite worlds through the holes. The lives in the four-dimensional universe, the tyrants in the five-dimensional universe, the experts in the six-dimensional universe, or the fiends in the seven-dimensional universe… As long as they exist, I will rob them and steal their everything, making them shudder in fear the moment they hear the name of the tiny rats and viruses from the three-dimensional universe and crying for help whenever they see the flag of the Big Bai Pirate Gang!

"It is not important yet how exactly my dream is going to be fulfilled. All in all, no matter you are from which dimension, I'll just roll my sleeves and fight you. That's what the greatest space pirate in the universe should do!"

Pa! Pa!

Another two of the Fist King's clones were burnt up.

"Your body is obviously too skinny for the job."

Boss Bai sniffed. "If you want to work together with me, you'll have to practice more."

"Me?"

All of the Fist King's clones pointed at their iron noses at the same time. "I'm going to… roll up my sleeves and fight with you?"

"Yes, the greatest purpose for which I am still lingering in the Imperium is to establish a fleet that is enough to launch an expedition to the other side of the grand universe or even to the four-dimensional universe."

Boss Bai said, "That's why I was willing to tell you my secrets and check if you were a 'Gaoist—man in the shell'. If you are and you join my cross-universe army of raiders, it will be possible for us to break the initial obstacle, which is the infinite black wall that lies beyond the three thousand Sectors.

"Believe me, we will certainly find the wizardly civilization and other three-dimensional carbon-based lives, or even the scumbags from the four-dimension and other higher dimensions. Then, we will rob all of them!"

Chapter 2468: It Sounds Flawless...

Boss Bai's body was so small, yet the dominance unleashed from his body and the ambitions that could melt the sun were so wild that even the Fist King's ninety-nine iron clones were forced to step back because of the scorching air from Boss Bai.

"Boss Bai, I have sensed that your brainwaves are in an extremely unstable state."

The Fist King said, "It's almost like you are suffering mental derangement every second, but you somehow get the derangement under control, or rather, you are used to the derangement. Is it the source of your strength and why you are significantly above the regular Divinity Transformation Stage? It's truly incredible and crazy."

"Maybe. Maybe I've been deranged since a long time ago, and I will continue to be deranged in the future."

Boss Bai took a deep breath and enjoyed his craziness. "If I were not insane, I could've stayed at home living a comfortable life in the company of my family and even the worship of the people, but for a real space pirate, would there be any difference between such a boring life and death?

"Now that I've made the step and come to the center of the cosmos, it's naturally the craziest dream that I will pursue after and the most invincible enemy that I will fight. I will explore places that nobody would, dare, or could explore billions of years previously and billions of years later!

"Fist King, join me. I'm told that your goal is to be the strongest in the universe, but there are universes above the universe. Even if you become the dominator of three thousand Sectors, you would still have to flee in panic under the destructive torrent of the doom. Could that be called 'strongest' at all?

"Come with me and conquer the higher-dimensions and reduce those scumbags into nothingness. That will be the real 'strongest in the universe'!"

Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep!

The ninety-nine iron clones of the Fist King were all caught in extreme thinking with noises and smoke popping nonstop. Thinking for a long time, he finally said in a daze, "Your proposal sounds fair enough, but Li Yao said…"

"Forget whatever Li Yao said. The cowardly, indecisive, shortsighted elementary school student knows nothing. Whoever teams up with him will only be killed because of him."

Boss Bai interrupted the Fist King and stepped forward. Hugging one of the Fist King's iron clones warmly, he said, "It will be much more promising if you hang around with Boss Bai more. Just look at the Blue Sky Market and so many starships that we have just earned. Don't you agree?"

The Fist King was confused by Boss Bai's sudden hugging. Thinking carefully for a long time, he still hesitated and asked, "Are you really not bothered that I am… not a human?"

"Human? Hahahaha, you are not a human, but who's a real human?"

Boss Bai laughed and hugged the Fist King's iron body even more tightly. "Ever since the Cultivators awaken their spiritual roots and accelerate their evolution, they have been developing from 'humans' to 'superhumans' or even 'nonhumans'. The more they train themselves, the further away they will be from the narrow concept of 'human'.

"Are you a human if you can blow up a mountain?

"Are you a human if you can unleash electricity from your pores or resist the heat of thousands of degrees with the body of flesh and blood?

"Are you a human if your soul can leave your body and travel thousands of kilometers away in the blink of an eye?

"Since we are bros, it doesn't hurt to tell you another little secret. Say, after a Cultivator died, his soul split into five hundred mice. The five hundred mice killed and swallowed each other, but they also mated and reproduced. After a hundred years of branching and fusing, the final remaining souls were melted with the soul of another Cultivator, giving birth to a brand-new 'human'. Then, is that a human, a ghost who possessed a body, or a rat that has gained wisdom? Hahahaha. Who can tell?"

The Fist King was deeply confused. He realized that his computational ability was never enough before Boss Bai. "A human transformed from five hundred mice, well…"

"So, I do not care if anyone is a human, nor do I care about other people's opinions and definitions on what's right and what's wrong. Who's qualified to decide those things on behalf of you and me?"

Looking at the Fist King in the eyes, Boss Bai said, "Your background is not important. The other people's opinions, comments, and definitions on you are bulls*it too. Whether or not you are a human, and who you are, it all depends on what you do right now and in every second to come. You define yourself with everything you do in your entire life, and you will find the real answer the day you embrace death and destruction in the end. That's the only time you can reach a conclusion."

After a brief silence, the Fist King said, "I seem to understand it now."

"No, you don't, neither do I."

Letting go of the Fist King, Boss Bai said casually against the sea wind with his hands in his back. "What is a human, and who am I going to become… Such questions can never be answered with a few words. Every time we think we understand them, we are merely stepping into the greater unknown, to be faced with even more confusion and choices.

"Maybe only by training, fighting, and questioning continuously and examining what we have done in the past in retrospect at the last moment of our lives can we know whether or not we are a real human, and what kind of person we are. Haha. Perhaps that is the significance of Cultivation.

"Fist King, come and join hands with me. Let's find our outcome, embrace survival or destruction, and define whether or not you are a human with your iron fists!"

"I seem to have been convinced by you."

The Fist King's previously indifferent voice was mixed with emotional waves.

"Hahahaha. I knew that you would be the perfect comrade for me!"

Boss Bai laughed crazily. "If a fleet is commanded by the two of us together, what world can't we conquer, what treasures can't we loot, and what enemy can't we defeat?"

"But that's all for the future."

The Fist King said, "You are perhaps a passionate daydreamer, but I am a doer who pays more attention to the details and the actual work. The wizardly world and the four-dimensional universe are too far away. If we set our midterm goal to be establishing the strongest fleet in the Cultivation world and cross the 'black wall' that lies beyond the three thousand Sectors, then, our 'minor goal' in the short term should be to crush the fleets of the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance in order to win their super warships, tremendous resources, and of course, the most distinguished and ambitious talents.

"If the 'minor goal' is dissected, the chaotic situation in the Imperium is a problem that we have to address immediately.

"Despite our commanding ability, our force is too weak and underdeveloped right now. Neither the reformists nor the four families are enemies that can be dealt with easily. We need to make thorough plans."

"That's not a big deal."

Boss Bai immediately changed from a frantic daydreamer to a heartless schemer. He sneered, "Li Wuji is a spineless dog and will surely bend to our will. Then, we will be able to wear the skin of the National Protection Army for a while longer. When the reformists and the four families fight one another in the 'battle of Seven Seas', we will take advantage of the mess.

"Even if the war between the reformists and the four families ends soon, it still won't be a big deal. I don't think that the Covenant Alliance has vanished so easily. There's bound to be a war between the Covenant Alliance and the Imperium. Then, we will do the same and swallow more crushed fleets to build up ourselves!

"We can talk about the subsequent details later. What matters most right now is to combine the resources in the Blue Sky Market. We are truly having indigestion after devouring twenty-eight pirate gangs and Li Wuji's entire fleet. All the starships are damaged and will not recover their combat abilities without one to two months of maintenance. It is safe to say that this is our weakest moment, like a boa that had just swallowed an elephant. We can barely move right now.

"But it doesn't matter. With Li Wuji in our hands, as long as he sends a report of victory back to his family, we will simply stay and rest under the name of the National Protection Army. Nobody knows the truth, and nobody will disturb us. We will be back to ourselves in one month and ten times stronger than before!

"I've taken all the details into consideration. Every procedure is seamless and flawless. There can't be anything wrong. Fist King, don't you agree?"

"Yes."

The Fist King said, "I have also run a meticulous analysis with my most powerful array of crystal processors. The four families have entrusted the battle of the Blue Sky Market to Li Wuji, and the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors is busy defending the Seven Seas Grand Market, their nest. So, as long as Li Wuji bends, there will be absolutely no problem. Nobody will come to disturb our digestion and upgrade."

"That's good to know."

Having been approved by the Fist King, Boss Bai nodded his head confidently and laughed again.

Right then, his wrist crystal processor buzzed lightly. He checked what it was about, only to discover that one of his subordinates was calling him in panic.

"Did I not tell you that I was going to have a meeting with the Fist King and that we were not to be disturbed whatever you might hear?"

Boss Bai frowned. Looking at his subordinate's twisted face, he asked suspiciously, "What's wrong? Did the captives have a riot?"

"No… Not exactly."

His subordinate shrieked, "We just received a distress call from the edge of the Blue Sky Space Zone. It-It's quite weird!"

"A weird distress call?"

Boss Bai and the Fist King were slightly stunned and looked at each other in bewilderment. He pondered for a moment and said, "Forward it to my portable crystal processor. What can possibly intimidate you into this?"

His subordinate swallowed hard and immediately forwarded the signal that had been processed.

Half a second later, a voice, which sounded as twisted as an earthworm and like a hen whose feathers were set on fire, echoed from Boss Bai's portal crystal processor.

"Boss Bai, help! Boss Bai, help! Boss Bai, help! Boss Bai, help! Boss Bai, help!"

Chapter 2469: He's Coming Straight at Us!

Boss Bai's heart had been indestructible and utterly unshaken during the soul-stirring conquest of the Blue Sky Market when he was terribly outnumbered. He did not doubt himself at all even in the most perilous moment.

But right now, a bottomless crack seemed to have been dug into his heart by the cry for help, and it was shaking violently.

As if he had just taken ten kilograms of rat poison, Boss Bai's face suddenly turned pale.

He was not as flabbergasted even when the Fist King opened fire at him vehemently with the hundred iron clones a moment ago.

His head tilted; every wrinkle on Boss Bai's face was cramping nervously while he listened to the signal carefully with disbelief beaming out of his eyes.

"Boss Bai, help! Boss Bai, help! Boss Bai, help!"

The signal was more and more high-pitched. The guy seemed to be coming at him desperately while being chased by something.

Pa!

Boss Bai turned off the signal quickly. After a slight cough, he glanced at the Fist King and then at his subordinate on the light beam, before he said calmly, "Don't panic. It's just a cosmic noise."

"Cosmic noise?"

His subordinate was rather suspicious. "Boss…"

"Exactly, cosmic noise."

Taking a deep breath, Boss Bai said, "The high-energy celestial bodies in space release radiations and spiritual waves every second. When the radiations, high-energy particles, and spiritual tides hit our communication magical equipment, they will congregate into weird cosmic noises. Sometimes, and coincidentally, the noises may sound like the voices of a human. It is nothing to be surprised at.

"So, it's just the meaningless noise from space. Just ignore it and eliminate the noise from our scanning spectrum."

His subordinate scratched his head. "Boss, that doesn't sound right."

"Yes, Boss Bai, I don't think it's noise either. It sounds somewhat familiar. Let me compare it to the data in my voiceprint library."

After a brief silence, the Fist King said, "Found it. It seems to be… Li Yao's voice."

"Li Yao?"

Boss Bai blinked quickly in confusion. "Which Li Yao?"

The Fist King: "… The Li Yao who introduced me to your place, and whom we just talked about just now. You said that he was like an earthworm and an elementary school student."

"Did I?"

Boss Bai turned on the distress call and listened carefully again before he finally reached the conclusion. "So, it does sound a lot like Li Yao's voice. Then, it's all the more reason why we can't respond! Listen, start radio silence immediately, and let the Blue Sky Market enter the highest defensive state. In the meantime, speed up the transference of captives and assets. Make sure that we can get out of this place immediately under the worst circumstances. It doesn't matter if we are leaving abundant assets behind. Speed! Speed is all that matters!

"As for the distress call… Don't answer it! Don't answer it! Don't answer it!"

"Well…"

The Fist King and his subordinate were both confused. "Why? Is Li Yao's distress call so dreadful?"

"Of course it is, because it is probably not Li Yao."

Boss Bai said with all solemnity, "It may be a scheme of the Immortal Cultivators, who are trying to deceive us with Li Yao's voice. If we really respond to it, chances are that the Immortal Cultivators will notice the anomaly and catch all of us. So, don't answer it. Don't answer it at all. Just pretend that we have never received the distress call and we are not even in the Blue Sky Market!"

"But it won't help."

Frowning, his subordinate stammered, "Boss, after receiving the distress call, we remotely scanned the edge of the Blue Sky Space Zone where the signal came from and detected a rather massive fleet. Right. The fleet was fervidly chasing a few unarmed, ragged carriers. The distress calls were sent from the carriers straight at the Blue Sky Market, and the carriers are fleeing toward the Blue Sky Market precisely too. They-they show no sign that they are changing their course at all. It's almost like they are certain that Boss is here, and they are determined to find you!

"We have analyzed the speed of the hunters and the runners as well as the distance between them. Even if we do not reply to the distress call, the runners are highly likely to reach the Blue Sky Market before they enter the firing range of the hunters, which will probably happen in twelve hours.

"In other words, in twelve hours at most, a whole fleet of hunters will arrive at the Blue Sky Market. Whether or not they will have destroyed the carriers of the runners, their starships are certainly in need of maintenance and supplies after such a long pursuit. In the several lightyears around, the Blue Sky Market is the best port for supplies. They will certainly be anchored in this place. That… That will be terrible!"

Hearing what his subordinate said, Boss Bai took a deep, deep breath.

"Well…"

In a trance, he said, "Assuming—just assuming—that we abandon everything in the Blue Sky Market and the starships we have just captured, will it be possible for us to escape before the mysterious fleet and the damn carriers arrive?"

"It's barely possible, boss."

His subordinate cried, "In order to swallow the twenty-eight pirate gangs and Li Wuji's fleet, our starships suffered heavy losses and are in dire need of a good rest before they take off again. If we embark on a long journey right now, we will be dead for sure without reliable supplies and maintenance ports!

"Also, we've been planning for such a long time and fought bloodily to achieve what we have achieved. How can we abandon all of it?"

"Yes, it's truly impossible…"

Boss Bai held himself back, but he finally couldn't hold it anymore. His face turned from pale to blue and then from blue to red, before he stomped on the ground so hard that almost the entire forsaken port was razed to the ground. "Li Yao, I'll kill you! I'll certainly kill you!"

He rushed to the command center in fury and, having no choice, asked his subordinate to send out the alarm for the highest battle preparation.

The Fist King also asked the Cultivators' fleet to be braced for a new round of battle remotely.

The whole Blue Sky Market was immediately caught in a mess.

The warriors of the Big Bai Pirate Gang were humans, and their nerves were not made of iron. Every human had weaknesses and needed to rest. Having just secured a shocking triumph, both the space pirates from the federation and the Cultivators of the Imperium were both excited and exhausted. Who could've thought that a new enemy would come at such a critical moment?

Wu—Wu—Wu—

The earsplitting alarms, echoing above the ocean of joy, poured down like a bucket of cold water.

"What's going on? A new enemy? A new enemy!"

Liu Li's eyes were swollen from crying. She was wiping her tears and looking for the Fist King to accuse Han Te when she suddenly heard the alarms. She was so shocked that she stood where she was, overwhelmed by the panic in the port for a moment, but very soon, she became firm again and went to the command center in a hurry. "Master Fist King! Master Fist King!"

"A*shole! What's going on?"

Deep inside the Blue Sky Market, at the end of an alley that was full of red lights, Han Te screamed too, and he ran out of the house; his clothes unorganized.

Quite a few of his buttons were missing, and his pants were only half zipped. There were the marks of multiple lipsticks in different hues on his face too.

The fury that was burning through the sky surrounded the young man who had made up his mind to be the "Best Banger in the Universe". His bloodshot eyes unleashed the brilliance that was almost equal to the main cannons of a starship. "Who has lived long enough and decided to disrupt Master Han's business? Let me give you a taste of Master Han's giant cannons!"

He ran to his starship crazily.

Countless other space pirates and Cultivators were returning to their battle posts in a hurry like Liu Li and Han Te. The Blue Sky Market that had fallen quiet before long was on fire again. The captives of the twenty-eight pirate gangs and Li Wuji's fleet were seething too, gradually going out of control.

On their way back to the command center, Boss Bai and the Fist King learned more about the hunters—very unoptimistic information.

The hunters were the regular army of the Imperium. They were the forty-seventh fleet in the sequence of the regular army, commonly known as "Flying Rainbow Fleet", which was a coalition fleet made of the elites of quite a few Sectors.

Of course, the Flying Rainbow Fleet was not a top-tier fleet in the four Kurfürst families or an expedition troop that had been tested by battles for decades in the frontline like Lei Chenghu's Astounding Thunder Fleet. It was merely a regional defensive force made of the few weak, barren Sectors at the edge of the Imperium.

Under normal circumstances, such coalition forces were between "garrisons" and "deep-sky fleets" in terms of strength. They did not have the ability to fight for a long time across space zones, and they were not a tricky enemy.

They were the main opponents for the space pirates, who often played the catch-me-if-you-can game with them. Although they were much larger than the space pirates in number, their speed and efficiency were not always satisfactory because of the complicated command system and their low fighting will. It was often the case that the space pirates looted and escaped, and yet they could not catch the tail of the space pirates. They had always been mocked by the space pirates as "the slow and stupid soldiers".

But today, the situation had changed.

The four families and the reformists were fighting over the hegemony at the edge of the Imperium so passionately that they were even recruiting the space pirates without caring about the cost. The local armed forces of the barren worlds were naturally also the targets that the two parties fought over.

The Flying Rainbow Fleet had been more inclined to the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the reformists in the beginning because most worlds at the edge of the Imperium were deeply associated with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. They were naturally disposed to the reformists.

But the four families turned out to be more powerful and wealthy. There was no telling what promises they had made, but the few worlds behind the Flying Rainbow Fleet had taken their side and announced their support for the four families and the old Council of Elders publicly.

Naturally, the equipment of the Flying Rainbow Fleet had also been upgraded, and their combat ability had been improved. They were no longer the slow and stupid fish that they used to be.

Chapter 2470: The Disgraced Lord

That being said, the most important factor in a war was still people and not the magical equipment. The four families could offer the secondhand warships that had been outdated in their own fleets to the local armed forces like the Flying Rainbow Fleet, but it was impossible for them to create well-trained soldiers out of nothing and help the Flying Rainbow Fleet to complete the transformation.

Therefore, despite the support of the four families, the Flying Rainbow Fleet was still a random, undistinguished, undetermined, and unintegrated second-rate troop that was better at exploiting the people and the weak than defeating the strong enemies.

The Big Bai Pirate Gang in the past wouldn't have been scared when they ran into the Flying Rainbow Fleet. They could always escape even if they could not defeat the enemy.

But today, having just swallowed a whole Blue Sky Market, the Big Bai Pirate Gang was caught in a subtle situation because of the Flying Rainbow Fleet's unexpected arrival.

The awkwardness was almost like when a boa was unable to move after just devouring an elephant, a fat, strong, and stupid boar suddenly appeared on its way…

The Flying Rainbow Fleet naturally knew that the coordinates they were jumping to belonged to the edge of the Blue Sky Space Zone, which was the territory of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors.

They were now hostile to the Blue Sky Market now that they had joined the four families.

However, they seemed to have received the intelligence that Li Wuji of the four families was about to conquer the Blue Sky Market.

Li Wuji's rank in the four families was certainly much higher than the commander of the Flying Rainbow Fleet who had only joined them recently. Therefore, the commander dared not trespass but sent a communication request to the Blue Sky Market, hoping to find out whether it was an ally or an enemy that was occupying the place.

Boss Bai had come to the command center, but he was still unaware of the Flying Rainbow Fleet's purpose. Although the mother port of the Flying Rainbow Fleet was not far away from the Blue Sky Space Zone, the two parties barely had any interaction before. Those secondary troops always considered it a top priority to preserve their strength. They wouldn't have marched at such a heavily-guarded place as the Blue Sky Market without enough temptation.

In the meantime, the conquest of the Blue Sky Market was Li Wuji's responsibility. Boss Bai did not feel that the Flying Rainbow Fleet, as a dog bought over by the four families, had the courage to fight over food with its master.

Pondering for a moment, he commanded his subordinates, "Is Marquess Yongchun full now? If he is, invite him over for a meeting. Right, clean him from inside to outside with high-pressure water guns and sanitize him with fire before bringing him here."

When Li Wuji, Marquess Yongchun, was brought over, he indeed appeared to be very full.

At the very least, he had been gagging since the moment he appeared before Boss Bai and the Fist King, and he did not stop at all in the first five minutes.

His eyes were bloodshot, his cheeks deeply hollowed, as if there were two holes on his face. His lips and his hands were all cramping nervously. He had been away for only several hours, but it almost looked like he had been imprisoned in a dungeon for more than ten years.

The humiliation just now was no better than being abused by his own gender physically for ten days and ten nights. His mind had been completely broken, and he did not dare to look at Boss Bai in the eyes at all.

"Unlock my barriers."

Boss Bai spoke to his subordinate.

"Boss, about that—"

His subordinates were all stunned. Even though Li Wuji had been heavily wounded, he had the Cultivation of the Divinity Transformation Stage after all and was merely suppressed by the barriers on his forehead, the back of his head, and his spine. If the barriers were unlocked, what if Li Wuji suddenly burst out and decided to die with his enemy?

"It's alright. Just unlock my lord's barriers and you may leave."

Boss Bai smiled and said, "I can tell that my lord has thought it through and will not kill himself."

Since Boss Bai put it that way, his subordinates stopped arguing but drew the nails that were carved with complicated rune arrays and connected through crystal wires out of Li Wuji's body.

After every nail was plucked, Li Wuji would shudder, with a cluster of bloody mist that was slightly stinky erupting out of his body.

After eighty-one barriers were plucked, his subordinates hurried to exit the room and close the door for him. Right now, Boss Bai, the Fist King, and Li Wuji were left alone.

"My lord, have you thought it through?"

His hands behind his back, Boss Bai stood before Li Wuji and looked at Li Wuji who was out of shape. "Can we talk about cooperation now?"

"I-I…"

Li Wuji shuddered nonstop. With the flames of both dread and hatred beaming out of his eyes, he mumbled, "I'll kill you! I'll kill you for such great humiliation! I'll kill you!"

"Haha, great!"

Boss Bai opened his arms and exposed the vulnerable spots on his chest and his abdomen to Li Wuji. "Whatever you want to do to me, my lord, you can do it now. Even if you can't defeat me, you can at least die a hero's death and deserve the title of a marquess of the Imperium. Come on, come on, let's do it!"

Holding his iron arms, the Fist King observed coldly, showing no sign that he was going to offer Boss Bai any help. He even stepped backward, implying that he would not intervene in the duel between the two of them.

Dumbfounded, Li Wuji shivered for a long time. The combat ability of the Divinity Transformation Stage was recovering, and the enemy who had greatly humiliated him was right before him, undefended. But he simply couldn't summon any strength to smack the disgusting and hideous face.

"Why are you not moving?"

Boss Bai's voice suddenly grew sharp. His right hand turned into a flash of lightning and slapped Li Wuji in the face heavily, blowing Li Wuji to the ground. The lord's previously hollowed face was immediately swollen like a bun, his saliva, snot, and blood spurting out at the same time. "Just do it, you coward, pussy, and boneless dog!"

"Ah…"

Crouching like a shrimp, Li Wuji wailed desperately. There was no telling whether it was because of pain or because he was angry at his extreme inability.

"Now that my lord refuses to attack, I imagine you must've thought it through."

Boss Bai rubbed his hand that was aching because of the slap just now on his leg while he said casually, "In terms of level and combat ability, you are far from me and Fellow Cultivator Lei. You will be dead for sure if you challenge either me or the Fist King!

"Even if you luckily survive and escape back to the four families, so what? Having been deceived and lost the entire fleet, you will only be defamed and mocked by other nobles!

"Your only hope, and the only way to keep your reputation, position, and power, or even to make one step forward, is to play the trick of cooperating with the enemy. First of all, you will help me and Fellow Cultivator Li to deceive your family and the Imperium so that we can grow and expand under the name of the 'National Protection Army' into a giant beast that nobody dares to mess with.

"Then, Fellow Cultivator Lei and I will naturally devote all the resources and help my lord to rise in the four families through whatever means possible to reach the critical places.

"Hehe, there's no need to feel that you are a 'traitor'. It doesn't hurt to tell you now. Our strength is far beyond your imagination. We have too many spies in the four families and even the reformists. Many nobles you saw every day were actually our people. You are now only following their example.

"How about it, my lord, have you thought it through? We have an unexpected situation right now. I don't have the time to waste on you. Let's make it quick. If you nod your head, you will be a dog before me but still the awesome Marquess Yongchun after you're back to the territory of the four families, with the hope of moving one step further in the near future.

"Or let's be more frank. Revenge is a dish best served cold. My lord, even if you want to seek revenge for your humiliation today, you need to return to your family alive and control even greater power first before you make a comeback, don't you?

"You are still not agreeing? Fine, as long as you slightly shake your head, I'll humiliate you no more but give you a quick death, sending you to meet Blackstar the Great!"

Boss Bai intimidated him brutally.

Li Wuji struggled even harder. There was no telling how many rounds he debated with himself, and dirty tears even ran out of his eyes, but he finally nodded his head helplessly and disgracefully.

The nod seemed to have used up the strength of his life. He collapsed on the ground and moaned like a dying dog.

"My lord, you are truly a smart man. I'm sure you will rise higher in the leadership of the Imperium!"

Boss Bai took a long breath in relief and smiled. Taking out a delicate handkerchief from his pocket, he squatted and helped Li Wuji to wipe the snot and blood on his face. "Look, I still remember my lord's quirk and have the handkerchief prepared for you. Hurry and clean up yourself, and then contact the commander of the Flying Rainbow Fleet!"

Now that Li Wuji had been bent, the communication between the Blue Sky Market and the Flying Rainbow Fleet was much easier.

Never would the commander of the Flying Rainbow Fleet dream that Li Wuji, who had come to attack the Blue Sky Market with a great army, had "defected" in only twenty-four hours. Naturally, he confessed his purpose "dutifully" through the remote communication device.

What the commander said was naturally not to be entirely believed, but Boss Bai was an experienced general, and the Fist King boasted remarkable analysis and deduction abilities. They were able to figure out the general picture of the whole thing.

As it turned out, the Flying Rainbow Fleet had been wandering outside of their defense area and, under the excuse of a long-distance drill, carrying out a rather old but dishonorable military mission of the Imperium—enlisting cannon fodder.

The fierce battle in the Seven Seas Grand Market was about to happen. Naturally, almost all the worlds and fleets at the edge of the Imperium were to be involved.

The four families had drawn the space pirates and the local armed forces to their side at a fortune and offered them all kinds of gears and titles because they wanted the local forces to lead the charge as the vanguard.

Neither the space pirates nor the local armed forces like the Flying Rainbow Fleet were stupid. They accepted the titles and offerings of the four families, swearing that their loyalty would be unquestionable. But whether or not they would really fight for the four families dutifully in a battle… it remained to be seen!

It was impossible for them to die for the four families, but they at least needed to pretend to be fearless. So, all they needed to do was to capture random people, kick them into the obsolete starships, and force them to lead the charge in the battle.

After all, since even the space pirates had turned into the National Protection Army, the rank and the maximal number of the Flying Rainbow Fleet had been more than doubled. The vacancies were allowed to be filled by their own enlistment. So, what was there to worry about? Just start capturing!

Chapter 2471: The Star of the Show Has Finally Come!

At first, the Flying Rainbow Fleet conducted the enlistment in its own defense area according to the tradition. Naturally, it was a familiar job for them. Without meeting any resistance, they tied up tremendous young and old people and sent them to starships.

After their names were added to the register of soldiers, they became the "elites" who were devoted to the country and willing to die for His Majesty and the four Kurfürsten. The subsidy and compensation for every "elite" could be applied from the Council of Elders and were not small at all. It was a bargain in any way.

However, they could not capture too many people from their own territory. After all, the high-intensity work in the mines, the farms, and the magical equipment factories had to be ensured, and the economic systems in the few Sectors needed to be kept running at least at the lowest efficiency too. They certainly could not kill the goose that laid golden eggs.

Therefore, after stuffing a few ragged starships, the Flying Rainbow Fleet shifted their attention to the neighboring Sectors, as well as the resource planets and space fortresses beyond the Sectors and the merchant teams that were passing by.

Among all of them, the passing merchant teams were their favorite targets. Their logic was simple. In such chaotic days, the real dutiful merchants were all resting at home obediently, and those who were bold enough to travel around must be desperadoes who were deeply associated with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, which meant that they were undoubtedly the accomplices of the "illegitimate Reform Committee of the Council of Elders".

Then, wasn't it reasonable, merciful, and humane for the Flying Rainbow Fleet to capture such merchants, confiscate their starships and goods, and enlist everybody in the business team so that they could "correct their mistakes by making contributions"?

Just like that, the Flying Rainbow Fleet jumped to a neighboring Sector, which had been in a bad relationship with it, in secret. Taking advantage of the fact that the local Sector Master was out and the place was weakly defended—speaking of which, the local Sector Master had probably gone to the Flying Rainbow Fleet's territory for "enlistment" in secret with his own troops—the Flying Rainbow Fleet swept across a few local resource planets even more brutally than space pirates, taking away all persons and assets.

Then, they set up ambushes on the few busy business routes nearby, and the moment they detected the spiritual waves of the merchant teams, they would immediately lean close with the advantage of the high speed and the fierce firepower, demanding to board on the starships for inspection. So much as they discovered anything that was remotely associated with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, even if it was only a roll of toilet paper with the logo of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, they would immediately declare that the strangers were the rebelling reformists and that their assets would be confiscated and they would be enlisted.

The operation "to clear the rebels and the bandits" lasted a few months. The hundred obsolete starships that had been used by the Flying Rainbow Fleet were all crammed with crying, hopeless "soldiers". Many carriers of the "rebels" had been captured and added to their own fleet. The operation was certainly a success!

Supposedly, they could return to their hometown for a rest after the successful operation. When the "battle of Seven Seas" began, the newly-drafted soldiers would be escorted to the frontline to be killed, while the veterans of the Flying Rainbow Fleet would cover them in the rear. By the time the newly-drafted soldiers were mostly killed, they would announce that the Flying Rainbow Fleet had suffered heavy losses and were almost entirely wiped out and that they had to withdraw from the battlefield despite their infinite devotion to His Majesty and the four Kurfürsten. Such moves were already clichés. Basically, all the warlords and local armed forces at the edge of the Imperium had been doing that.

However, when the Flying Rainbow Fleet was about to return, they accidentally detected a new starship jumping to their nearby.

Compared with their trophies in the past few months, the tiny starship was only a shrimp that could barely fill the gap of their teeth.

But now that they had seen it, it wouldn't hurt to draft the new starship.

With that in mind, the few main warships of the Flying Rainbow Fleet moved toward the starship lazily. After a brief engagement, the tiny starship's spiritual shield and power units were soon blown up, and it had to send out the signal of surrender.

Up to this moment, everything was normal.

However, what happened later was far beyond the expectation of all the soldiers in the Flying Rainbow Fleet.

Or rather, including the commanders of all levels, 99% of the people had no idea what happened until this moment.

All in all, later that day, the flagship of the Flying Rainbow Fleet had an astonishing explosion. Too many Immortal Cultivators had been wounded or blown up.

What was even weirder was that tremendous Immortal Cultivators had gone missing. Those who went missing were all the direct bloodlines of the leadership of the Flying Rainbow Fleet. They were the sons of the chief of staff and the fire control officer. Two of the highest commander's grandsons had disappeared uncannily too!

In the meantime, the few carriers that had theoretically been controlled by the Flying Rainbow Fleet somehow got away from the fleet taking advantage of the chaos. Those carriers were fully loaded with the cannon fodder that the Flying Rainbow Fleet had captured to die for themselves!

The leaders of the Flying Rainbow Fleet were almost fuming in fury. They naturally chased after the runners passionately.

By logic, the leaders of the Flying Rainbow Fleet shouldn't have been as unvigilant.

But on one hand, their important persons had gone missing and were probably on the few carriers that had wandered off uncannily, and on the other hand, the enemy did not strike them as formidable from the beginning to the end. The few running fleets were also now stopping and now moving, damaged by the barrage. They were under the impression that they could settle the battle with a crazy bombardment.

After jumping to the edge of the Blue Sky Space Zone, the Flying Rainbow Fleet, knowing that it was probably occupied by the enemy, was careful enough to send a message to the Blue Sky Market to check the situation.

After they learned that the business center had been conquered by Li Wuji, they were overjoyed and requested Li Wuji to attack together with them and capture the few uncanny carriers.

"Boss Bai, what do you think?"

After basically figuring out what had happened, the Fist King asked.

"What can I possibly think?"

Closing his eyes and rubbing his nose, Boss Bai gnashed his teeth, "It must've been that wavering Li Yao who accidentally discovered that the Flying Rainbow Fleet was capturing innocent people as cannon fodder. He became a hero who saved people from a disaster again!

"A*shole, if you like being a hero, at least learn to wipe your own a*s! Boss Bai is the greatest space pirate in the universe who will explore the wizardly worlds and the four-dimension someday, not someone who wipes your a*s for you every day!"

"Boss—"

One of his subordinates brought over the latest scanning result. "According to our accurate calculation, the enemy's possible time of arrival has been significantly advanced. If they do not change their course, they will run into the Blue Sky Market in about seven hours."

Boss Bai: "… What's the configuration of the enemy's army?"

His subordinate replied, "Now that the enemy knows that Li Wuji's fleet has conquered the Blue Sky Market, they do not hide their spiritual waves anymore. Based on the exhaust flames of the starships, the Flying Rainbow Fleet has got at least ten secondhand main-force warships from the four families. In terms of speed, firepower, or duration, it is more than 30% stronger than the previous Flying Rainbow Fleet.

"Of course, we do not know whether or not their personnel has been improved. So, it remains to be seen how much their combat ability has been actually improved.

"But according to the data on paper, and since our army is exhausted after a fierce battle, it will be an arduous battle. Even if our army wins, it will be a brutal victory with heavy losses."

"Arduous battle… Brutal victory…"

Staring at the light spots that were getting closer and closer to the Blue Sky Market, Boss Bai's eyes were filled with little red spots from which blood was almost shooting out. In the end, he took a deep breath and waved his hands. "All the starships that can move, prepare to attack. We will pretend to cooperate with the enemy but we will, in fact, surround the Flying Rainbow Fleet from their flanks and their rear!"

"S-Surround them?"

Stunned, his subordinate thought that he heard it wrong. However weak the Flying Rainbow Fleet was, they were a regular army after all. The Big Bai Pirate Gang might be capable of swallowing them after a month of rest, but in today's awkward situation, it wouldn't be bad at all if they could defeat or crush the enemy. If they were to surround the enemy, wouldn't the previously outnumbered troop be even more dispersed?

Now that they were seriously short of hands and everybody was exhausted, they were still going to disperse and surround the enemy. It was almost the greatest joke. How could their boss make such a mistake?

"Yes, we will surround them."

Boss Bai's eyes suddenly grew firm. "Then, we will eat the Flying Rainbow Fleet without letting go of a single escape capsule!"

"It's impossible!"

His subordinate blurted out, "It's already the greatest miracle to have swallowed one elephant. How can we possibly swallow two elephants in a row in only twenty-four hours."

"Yes, we can, as long as—"

Boss Bai narrowed his eyes and mumbled, "As long as Li Yao still had the slightest humanity and is not simply crying for help!"

…

At the edge of the Blue Sky Space Zone, a starship that was riddled with holes and blackened by fire was fleeing in panic. Sparks were spluttering everywhere, while intense smoke was popping up.

"Boss Bai, help! Boss Bai, help! Boss Bai, help!"

Wearing a silk flowery shirt and a pair of beach pants, with blue-and-white slippers on his feet, Li Yao held a cup of juice of tropical fruits, shrieking while sipping the juice.

Long Yangjun stood far away from him, with her hands behind her back, and stared at the infinite sea of stars outside of the crystal window, as if she did not know him at all.

Li Jialing, on the other hand, looked at him in bewilderment.

Xiaoming and Wenwen were sitting in a corner. They were biting their lips and twisting their clothes now and then, their big watery eyes filled with confusion, as if they were wondering why they had such a father.

"Rest assured, everything is under control."

Li Yao shouted for a while before he turned around and smiled at Li Jialing. "According to my accurate speculation, the new master of this place should be my life-and-death brother Boss Bai, who is a man of integrity and brotherhood. After receiving my cry for hell, he will certainly reinforce me without caring about anything else. Then, everything will be fine!"

"He's just a space pirate—"

Li Jialing had been holding himself long enough and couldn't do it anymore. "How much integrity and brotherhood can he possibly have?"

"Well…"

Thinking for a moment and sipping the juice again, Li Yao made a gesture and said, "He has as much integrity and brotherhood as I do!"

Chapter 2472: Li Yao's Plan

Li Jialing: "… Why am I getting more and more scared as you talk, Brother Yao? Speaking of which, even if we are asking for help from this Boss Bai, there is no need to put on such tawdry clothes, is there?"

Li Jialing pointed at Li Yao's red-and-green clothes and his blue-and-white flip-floppers.

"What do you know? This is exactly a garment of Boss Bai's style!"

Li Yao said disapprovingly, "I learned this style from Boss Bai exactly as a way to show my admiration for him. The moment he sees this same-style battle robe, he will certainly recall how we fought together years ago, and he won't refuse to help us at all."

Li Jialing bit his teeth hard as he tried to hold himself back. "I only feel that this is such a disgrace. We were never like this when we were faced with Blackstar the Great a few days back. At that time, you rode on the 'Gold Vulture' magnificently. Isn't it a shame to be so… humble before a space pirate?"

"A shame? Do you really think that you are something and your honor is valuable?"

Li Yao sniffed and drank up the juice in the cup. His eyes glowing with calmness and determination, he said solemnly, "We Cultivators can shed blood or even die for what we believe in. Do we need such a thing as honor?"

"Cough, cough. Cough, cough, cough, cough."

Long Yangjun coughed lightly not far away and moved quietly, getting as far away from Li Yao as possible.

Xiaoming and Wenwen couldn't hear it anymore and went to the cabins in the rear to look after the soldiers that had just been rescued—or the people who were soon to be cannon fodder.

Blushing, Li Jialing was lost for words. After a long daze, he finally managed to ask, "Brother, I'm just confused. Although the Flying Rainbow Fleet has a hundred times more starships than we do, it is just a mediocre fleet without a Colossus, isn't it? Our carrier may not have any combat ability, but with the Colossi of you and Sister Long, wouldn't it be more than easy for you to destroy the enemy's flagship?

"I don't believe that the local troop at the edge of the Imperium can be so determined that they will still fight the Colossi when their flagship has been decapitated by the Colossi. It's absolutely impossible! Even the best fleet of the Imperium hardly chooses to attack the enemy's Colossus if they do not have any Colossus themselves.

"So, why do we have to ask Boss Bai for the favor?"

Li Yao sighed. Licking the juice on his lips, he said, "This question is very simple. First of all, it is indeed possible for Long Yangjun and me to crush the Flying Rainbow Fleet's battle formation or even destroy the enemy's flagship and a few main warships.

"But it's certainly impossible to keep the entire Flying Rainbow Fleet here with only two Colossus. Such local troops from the edge of the Imperium are all specialists at preserving themselves. They may be not good at fighting hard battles, but they are the best experts when it comes to shifting sides or running away! Perhaps the moment they detect the spiritual flames spreading out of the two Colossi, they will immediately escape at their highest speed. How many of the starships can Long Yangjun and I catch even if we have three heads and six arms?

"Once most of the starships of the Flying Rainbow Fleet jump away, they will certainly spread out the news that two mysterious Colossi appeared in this area. Soon enough, all the forces will learn the traces of Long Yangjun and me, including Blackstar the Great.

"Then, it will be easy for him to figure out our intention and our association with Boss Bai. He may even infer that our operation in the next step must be concerned with the Seven Seas Grand Market, the nest of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors.

"It is the last thing that I want Blackstar the Great to know. In the game between us and Blackstar the Great, our greatest advantage is that the enemy is in the light while we are in the dark. The more secret and stealthy we are, the better. It will be best if we are gone from Blackstar the Great's eyes forever and emerge from his back at the most awkward moment for him!

"Secondly, and more importantly—

"We attacked the Flying Rainbow Fleet at the risk of exposing ourselves in order to rescue the ordinary people who had been captured by the Flying Rainbow Fleet as cannon fodder.

"If we hadn't done anything, the innocent miners, farmers, and small dealers who struggled to survive would've been chained to the starships and forced to march at the most dangerous battlefield by the damn Immortal Cultivators of the Flying Rainbow Fleet, getting killed for nothing!

"Right now, we have only saved part of the innocents. There are still more 'soldiers' who are scattered on the starships of the Flying Rainbow Fleet. We can barely rescue them with only our strength. If Long Yangjun and I really destroy the flagship of the Flying Rainbow Fleet, the remaining fleets will only disperse and bring the captives back to their nest. Then, we can't chase after them planet after planet, can we?

"So, if we want to rescue all the innocents at once, we must tread lightly. We can only show the Flying Rainbow Fleet that we are weak and attract them to Boss Bai's territory so that we can surround them with the Big Bai Pirate Gang and possibly the fleet of the Cultivators, trying to not let go of any starship.

"By the time the last retreat of the Flying Rainbow Fleet is blocked, it will be the showtime for the 'Arsonist' and the 'Super Awesome Upgraded Heavenly Crystal'. Hehe. My purpose is not so simple as to crush the Flying Rainbow Fleet; I'm going to swallow them from the skin to the bones. Not even a screwhead is going to get away from my hands!"

"That explains a lot!"

Li Jialing was a smart person himself. After Li Yao pointed out the critical factors, he immediately understood everything. His anger and confusion a moment ago turned into admiration again.

Thinking for a moment, he glanced at Long Yangjun not far away and said in a low voice, "However, Brother Yao, you may want to stop calling Sister Long's Colossus 'Super Awesome Upgraded Heavenly Crystal'. She seems pissed about the name."

"What's wrong with the name? How magnificent it sounds! It's much cooler than simply 'Heavenly Crystal'!"

Li Yao frowned. "Her Colossus has been utterly transformed and is nothing like the previous 'Heavenly Crystal'. Besides, only a Colossus with such an intimidating name is qualified to team up with my 'Arsonist', right?"

"…"

Li Jialing was silent for a moment before he quickly changed the topic. "Right, I'm also confused about one thing. How did you know that the Blue Sky Space Zone is already Boss Bai's territory? His Big Bai Pirate Gang couldn't have expanded fast enough to swallow the entire Blue Sky Market, could it? Also, according to the secret messages among the four families that Xiaoming and Wenwen intercepted after hacking the mainframe crystal processor of the Flying Rainbow Fleet, Boss Bai had surrendered to the four families and joined Marquess Yongchun, Li Wuji. Even though the Blue Sky Market was not within the control of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and the reformists, it should be the territory of the four families led by Li Wuji, and Boss Bai and the other twenty or so pirate gangs should be hamstringing each other. He wouldn't be able to help us even if he wanted to.

"Then, wouldn't we suffer attacks from both sides if we jumped to this place?

"So my question is, why did you think that the Blue Sky Market's new master was Boss Bai, and even 'Fist King' Lei Zonglie and the Cultivators' fleet were here?"

"Because he is Boss Bai!"

Li Yao smiled and placed his legs on the desk. Looking at his toes that were twisting merrily inside the slippers, he said with a smile, "The moment Xiaoming and Wenwen got the intelligence that Boss Bai had surrendered to the four families, I knew that there must be a scheme in it.

"Boss Bai is a man who pursues absolute freedom. Since the moment he… broke the old boundary, nobody would ever be qualified to be his master. Even the most formidable gods wearing the supreme crowns cannot bend his knees or tell him what to do!

"The four families? Marquess Yongchun, Li Wuji? Hehe. Those small fries are not even qualified to command Boss Bai's leg hair!

"So, I was very certain that Boss Bai succumbed to Li Wuji because he had dug a gigantic trap for Li Wuji to jump in—a trap that was probably not just for Li Wuji but also for the dozens of pirate gangs that were gathered in the Blue Sky Market.

"Considering Boss Bai's ambitions and the background that the 'battle of Seven Seas' was about to begin, it was obvious what Boss Bai was trying to do. He was going to swallow the elephant and finish the most dangerous leap, making the Big Bai Pirate Gang rise at the border of the Imperium and become a powerful force that could possibly shake the situation of the Imperium!

"Such a scheme could barely be accomplished by the Big Bai Pirate Gang alone, but didn't the intelligence that we receive earlier suggest that the fleet of the Cultivators commanded by 'Fist King' Lei Zonglie had also risen unstoppably at the edge of the Imperium? Do you think that it is a coincidence that the ranges of activities of the Cultivators' fleet and the Big Bai Pirate Gang are almost the same and can barely be separated?
