"The rumors had it that the Cultivators' fleet had been in frictions with the Big Bang Pirate Gang, but since you spent some time in the Martial Meritocrats Sector too, you should know why the Fist King and the rest of them were here. They were introduced by me to join Boss Bai and must've been cooperating with Boss Bai in secret. Otherwise, they wouldn't have grown so fast at all. Now that they had teamed up to trap Li Wuji and the space pirates, could their enemy possibly survive them?

"Rest assured. If my guess is correct, the entire Blue Sky Market must be under the control of Boss Bai. Also, his capabilities have significantly expanded after swallowing so many pirate gangs and all the starships of Li Wuji's fleet together with the Fist King. Since he is a man who is willing to die for his brother anytime, he will certainly attack with all his available forces and help me surround the Flying Rainbow Fleet the moment I cough and drop him a hint!"

Chapter 2473: No Way In Hell!

"Is that so?"

Li Jialing was still rather suspicious. "If he is as trustworthy as Brother Yao claimed, why didn't you just activate the direct communication with the Blue Sky Market and explain everything to him, instead of shouting the humiliating distress calls? Are you sure that 'Boss Bai, help' is enough to convey all the information?"

"It can't be helped. This starship was stolen by us. Also, it's been so many years since I last met Boss Bai. I'm really not confident that I can turn on a secret communication channel to talk to Boss Bai with the devices on board. So, I can only broadcast via public channels."

Li Yao explained, "In order to attract the Flying Rainbow Fleet to bite the hook, we are too close to the Flying Rainbow Fleet. Any information sent to the Blue Sky Market in public may be received by the Flying Rainbow Fleet.

"If I confess my whole plan in the communication, only to be learned by the Flying Rainbow Fleet, they will certainly turn around and escape however stupid they are, won't they?

"So, I can only send my information and my request in such a subtle way. Even if the Flying Rainbow Fleet catches my voice, they can't figure out my real intention."

"That explains a lot, but—"

Li Jialing seemed to have understood it, but he was then mired in greater confusion. "The cry for help is so ambiguous. Are you sure that Boss Bai can understand your meaning, figure out the current situation, and help us surround the Flying Rainbow Fleet, Brother Yao?"

"Yes, he certainly can."

Through the empty juice cup, Li Yao observed the bizarre, twisted world and mumbled, "He is Boss Bai, a strengthened Boss Bai who has been enhanced by a certain Yan person. He is almost a half teacher for me. I'm sure he understands me.

"In any case, I cannot watch the Immortal Cultivators capturing so many innocents as cannon fodder, covering the old, the weak, the women, and the children in the combat suits of the Imperium, and getting them killed in the meaningless war.

"However slim the chances are, I will wager on it and let the shameless animals who captured innocents to fight in their place pay!"

…

In the meantime, above the Blue Sky Market, the Big Bai Pirate Gang, and the Cultivators' fleet, under the cover of dozens of Li Wuji's starships that could still fly, had taken off from the port, moving faster and faster to the boundless space.

"Infinite Fire", the Big Bai Fleet's previous flagship, had died together with Black Swirl, the flagship of the Black Wind Fleet, in the battle to protect the federation. After that, the Big Bai Pirate Gang had jumped to the center of the cosmos on a comprehensive supply starship. After a series of sneak-in, deception, seizure, maintenance, modification, and upgrade, the Big Bai Pirate Gang had a brand-new flagship, which had insanely melted the power units of almost ten rapid warships, making it the fastest flagship at the center of the cosmos.

Boss Bai had named his new flagship as—"Alloy Virus".

The Fist King had found the name perplexing, not knowing what deeper meaning it had.

After Boss Bai revealed his whole ambitions today, he finally understood the meaning of "Alloy Virus". Boss Bai was probably hoping that the flagship could reach the higher dimensions and pose as much a threat to the incredible existence there as viruses had posed to mankind, wasn't he?

As for the flagship of the Cultivators' fleet, it was much simpler.

The Cultivators who escaped from the Martial Meritocrats Sector were all gathered around Gao Kangda's Great Mountain.

Gao Kangda was the captain of a mining starship in the beginning. Despite his resolution and his devotion, he was an excellent organizer of logistics but not a distinguished fleet commander.

After the Fist King's abilities were gradually awakened, allowing him to absorb and even "copy" a lot of tactics and skills to his database from Boss Bai, he naturally took over most of the commandership from Gao Kangda. Even the flagship had been turned into a mothership of space shuttles modified from a comprehensive supply starship. Such massive starships did not have a high combat ability themselves, but they had the thickest armor and the greatest inner space, making it possible to accommodate tremendous crystal suits, space shuttles, Colossi, and sometimes even maintenance factories with full facilities. They were the most reliable mobile bases. With the Fist King's amazing abilities, it could carry out more than 300% of its battle performance.

The Fist King had named the flagship "Iron Fist", a rather plain name.

At this moment, "Alloy Virus" and "Iron Fist", commanding their respective fleets, approached the Flying Rainbow Fleet from two sides.

The Flying Rainbow Fleet was still ignorant of the upcoming destructive strike.

Just now, they had confirmed with Li Wuji about the soul signature and the battle cooperation code. There was nothing suspicious.

Furthermore, it was they who invited Li Wuji's fleet to attack the enemy together. Nothing could possibly be wrong.

Well, the only thing that could go wrong was this Marquess Yongchun might burst into a fury after discovering that they had captured so many civilians and pretended that they were soldiers. But that was for the future. There would always be ways to satisfy Marquess Yongchun.

But it did not mean that Boss Bai and the Fist King had nothing to worry about now.

It was because they were faced with an extremely troublesome mission, which was to surround the Flying Rainbow Fleet.

A siege in space was utterly different from a siege on the ground.

A siege on a ground battlefield was two-dimensional. All one needed to do was to surround the enemy's front, rear, left, and right. Theoretically, as long as one had an army four to five times greater than the enemy's, it would be possible to complete the siege.

But the battlefield in space was three-dimensional. It was a vast space similar to a sphere, without obstacles such as mountains or rivers. Surrounding it was highly difficult, and escaping was more than convenient.

Although the Big Bai Pirate Gang had just swallowed twenty-eight pirate gangs as well as all of Li Wuji's fleet, their flagships were all ragged and riddled with holes, not even having a 50% combat ability.

It was needless to say that most of the captives might be having other plans and could not be completely trusted.

In such a subtle situation, dispersing the army and surrounding the enemy recklessly would only make the circle of siege extremely thin. Chances were that the Flying Rainbow Fleet could leave a few holes on the siege after some minor stabs.

Once the siege was broken apart and the enemy launched a counterattack, there was even the risk that the army could be annihilated.

The risk was so high that no wonder even Xiao Lishui, the chief of staff of the Big Bai Pirate Gang who had been a space pirate for hundreds of years, was gloomy and suggested to Boss Bai hesitatingly, "Boss, are we really going to… swallow the Flying Rainbow Fleet?"

"Or what?"

Staring at the data on the menu that was erupting like a flood and changing a hundred times per second, Boss Bai said coldly, "In order to combine the starships and the personnel of the twenty-eight pirate gangs and Li Wuji's fleet and to swallow the resources in the Blue Sky Market, we need at least a month.

"During the month of our upgrade, nobody outside is to know the truth of the Blue Sky Market or spread out what happened in the Blue Sky Space Zone.

"The Flying Rainbow Fleet is so close to the Blue Sky Market that they will demand to enter the port for supplies and maintenance in any case. By then, do you really think they will ignore what happened in the Blue Sky Market? If we do not annihilate all of them, should anything leak out, it will be the main forces of the four families that come here.

"Hehe, we've been planning for an entire year for today. Now that we have swallowed the elephant, it is impossible for us to give it away. Since escaping is not an option, we can only fight now!

"Besides, hehehehe, the batch of new warships that the Flying Rainbow Fleet got is not bad. It is a shame that they are used by those idiots. It's not Boss Bai's style to refuse the meat that has been fed to my mouth!"

"However—"

Xiao Lishui sighed and raised the critical question. "With our combat ability right now, it is impossible to annihilate the Flying Rainbow Fleet however we infer the battle! Boss, take a look at the results of the dozens of battle simulations. If we condense the troop into a charge formation, it will be likely that we can defeat or even crush the Flying Rainbow Fleet; but if the troop is dispersed on the entire surface of the sphere while we try to surround and swallow all of enemy's starships, it will be difficult. Highly difficult.

"It's too risky to swallow the enemy in one bite. Boss, be careful not to be tripped by the gutter!"

"Have you taken Li Yao into consideration?"

His eyes half narrowed, Boss Bai said, "According to the Fist King and Li Yao's performance with the reformists, he is now steadily in the Divinity Transformation Stage, with a rather tough Colossus, the 'Gold Vulture'."

"Yes, we have. We added Li Yao's combat ability to the simulations."

Xiao Lishui said, "It's true that the Colossi can be incredible when it comes to attacking strongholds and decapitating, but they are not invincible. Their fatal weaknesses are fuel, ammunitions, and longevity of fighting. However strong Li Yao is, it hasn't been long since he entered the Divinity Transformation Stage after all. He is in the beginning level or the middle level of the Divinity Transformation Stage at best. How could he blow up a few starships in a row?

"A space battlefield can be tens of thousands of kilometers in diameter. Should we collapse on one side, it may be barely possible for Li Yao to reinforce in time however spectacularly he fights on the other side. So, Boss, you have to think carefully!"

"Huh…"

Crossing his fingers under his nose, Boss Bai thought hard, with profound brilliance beaming out of his eyes.

He replayed Li Yao's cries for help.

"Boss Bai, help! Boss Bai, help! Boss Bai, help!"

Li Yao's earsplitting scream echoed in the bridge again, making every member of the bridge team pale.

But this time, Boss Bai chuckled in a low voice.

"Little a*shole, Old Xiao is right, it's been no more than one year since you advanced into the Divinity Transformation Stage, hasn't it? I really can't figure out what kind of 'miracle' you can bring to us this time!"

Boss Bai mumbled but bulged his eyes immediately like the main guns of a starship being fired. He rose abruptly and waved his hands, declaring resolutely, "Send my order out. We will no longer pretend to be an ally of the scumbags of the Flying Rainbow Fleet. Go full speed ahead from the flanks and surround the enemy according to Plan D. In the meantime, launch enough interferential bombs to prevent the enemy from jumping away or sending out any distress call.

"The Blue Sky Market is mine. Now that you've come to hell, there is no need for you to leave now!"

Chapter 2474: A Welcome Barrage!

The only outcome of trespassing on the Big Bai Pirate Gang's territory was death!

Boss Bai's roars were the most intense excitants for the space pirates from the federation. Even though they had run out of their strength and spiritual energy in the previous battle, when the horns of a new battle were blown again, they were still unleashing the most fervent enthusiasm, as if they were devoting their lives to the furnaces of the starships.

The starships that looked like arrows suddenly accelerated, leaving trails behind that looked like sharp claws, while they went to the flanks and the rear of the Flying Rainbow Fleet.

The distance between the two parties was so close that the Big Bai Pirate Gang and the Cultivators' fleet were unable to continue their disguise. Even the most inert commander could tell that their transformation, which looked like a blooming, man-eating flower, was not meant for the few carriers; they were planning to swallow the Flying Rainbow Fleet that was almost their size!

"What… are they doing?"

On the flagship of the Flying Rainbow Fleet, the leadership made of dozens of warlords and local bigshots from the peripheral worlds of the Imperium had been gloating under the illusion that the carrier Li Yao was at would be captured in no time. They were even thinking about how they should relax and enjoy themselves in the Blue Sky Market after the battle.

Little did they anticipate that "Li Wuji's fleet" on their opposite side would suddenly transform into a highly-malicious battle formation of siege and annihilation!

They tried to transform, stay away, or even retreat, but there was no time.

The Flying Rainbow Fleet and the Big Bai Pirate Gang had been moving toward each other in the beginning, like two crystal trains running on the same rail at the highest speed that would collide very soon.

In such a gargantuan fleet, a safety distance must be guaranteed between every two starships in case of the conflicts of the spiritual shields or the exhaust flames. It was like a piece of precise but delicate artwork, and turning the fleet around quickly was certainly not an easy job. Also, turning around against the enemy's fire was suicide.

The Flying Rainbow Fleet had no choice but to keep their speed and charge into the siege of the Big Bai Pirate Gang and the Cultivators' fleet.

For a moment, the leaders of the Flying Rainbow Fleet were indeed overwhelmed.

Naturally, they did not know the drastic changes in the Blue Sky Market, or who their enemy was. They thought that Li Wuji had somehow lost his mind and decided to swallow them. It was anything but unusual for the four families' troops to swallow the miscellaneous troops from the edge of the Imperium.

However, after they calmed themselves down and scanned the exhaust flames on the opposite side, thereby determining the number and combat ability of the Big Bai Pirate Gang and the fleet of Cultivators, they were amused and more or less relieved.

It was too far-fetched.

Judging from the lackluster and messy exhaust flames, it was not hard to tell that the hostile starships were either of low levels or just been through a fierce battle. They were heavily damaged but not repaired yet, which significantly reduced their combat ability.

Such a small army had changed into such a ferocious battle formation, trying to surround them from all directions and swallow all of their starships? It was too far-fetched!

"Damn it. What is Li Wuji up to exactly? Does he think he can blatantly exploit the magnates from the edge of the Imperium because he is a marquess and a powerful person in the Li family?"

"We have pledged loyalty to the Council of Elders sincerely and have our own supporters in the Council of Elders. Li Wuji has chosen the wrong target!"

"Even if he is dissatisfied with us drafting civilians as soldiers, there is no need for a battle. Which family in the entire Imperium does not fight a war in such a way? Is he really trying to accuse us with such a lousy excuse and swallow us to increase his own strength?"

"Hehe. Those grand nobles are always like this. They think that they are the best and everything is theirs. But why?"

"Let's crush Li Wuji's fleet first and report the matter to the four families. A great war is coming. Let's see how he can be the 'Marquess Yongchun' after he makes such a horrible mistake!"

Thinking that they had seen through the weakness of Li Wuji and the siege, the leadership of the Flying Rainbow Fleet reached the consensus to move forward at full speed and rip the enemy's battle formation from the front.

By common sense, it was the absolutely correct strategy.

No matter whether the enemy was Li Wuji's fleet or the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors', their weakness was apparent. Whatever their purpose was, it would only fall out after their siege was shattered.

The Flying Rainbow Fleet that was moving at full speed did not have the time to stop and release the remote communication units or unfold the antennae that could be dozens of kilometers long, which meant that they could not send any signal to an external world lightyears away. Faced with a hostile army whose combat ability had been compromised and which was in a wrong battle formation, it was unnecessary for them to ask for help or send alarms.

Besides, the fierce firepower from the Big Bai Pirate Gang and the Cultivators' fleet had exploded around the Flying Rainbow Fleet. The first round of fire was not very destructive, but the surging spiritual waves and space ripples it spread out prevented the Flying Rainbow Fleet from jumping and blocked their super-remote communication functions.

"They are scared that we may run away?"

The leaders of the Flying Rainbow Fleet were amused. "Those great nobles truly have infinite confidence. They think that they can trap us with such a thin siege.

"Forward! Forward! Forward! Pierce out from the enemy's front that is the weakest spot. It should be them who are running!"

The two fleets, one as round as a shield and the other as sharp as a dagger, approached each other rapidly. The most dazzling spears of light protruded from their front ends, and a short but brutal space battle was about to begin!

…

"Do you see it? Have you seen it?"

On the ragged carrier, faced with the starry light that was more and more brilliant, Li Yao danced in wild joy. "What did I say? Check it out! Just like what I said—even a man as formidable as Boss Bai has to show some respect for Vulture Li Yao!

"The Flying Rainbow Fleet has been stalled by Boss Bai. Those idiots are not even trying to escape. Haha. They are asking to be killed!

"Boss Bai has released the most intense interferential firepower at the earliest chance, stopping the Flying Rainbow Fleet from jumping away or sending out distress calls. It seems that we are truly appreciative of each other, and he understood the profound content hidden in 'Boss Bai, help' easily!

"The two parties are engaged. The Flying Rainbow Fleet has been attracted tightly without any hope of getting away. Now, it's our showtime to return what we have suffered in the past days to those scumbags!"

Li Yao licked his lips, which were blistering because of his burning fighting will, with a wildest and exhilarant smile.

"But…"

Li Jialing said next to him hesitatingly, "Why do I feel that the violent fire from the Big Bai Pirate Gang is aimed at us?"

"Huh?"

Li Yao's pupils constricted violently. Deep inside his eyes, the brilliant barrage from the dozens of arsenal warships of the Big Bai Pirate Gang on his opposite side expanded to the maximum in only one moment, almost setting his eyeballs on fire.

As it turned out, thousands of pillars of light were rushing at the ragged carrier Li Yao was in straightforwardly.

Behind the pillars of light were the magnetic tides that were slower, and behind the magnetic tides were the crystal bombs that were even slower. It seemed that Boss Bai was going to kill him in the bombardment!

Li Yao's back was soaked in a cold sweat. "Seriously?"

The overwhelming barrage almost drowned Li Yao's ragged carrier, but it brushed past him with an error of several kilometers or even several hundred meters, dispersing and shooting at the pursuers of the Flying Rainbow Fleet behind him, blowing them into a mess.

In a space battle, a distance of several kilometers was really shorter than the width of a hair. The barrage demonstrated Boss Bai's incredible control of firepower. But still, it seemed unnecessary to make the barrage so close to Li Yao's starship.

"Well…"

Li Jialing was silent for a while. "I don't know if it is my illusion, but why do I feel that Boss Bai seems very dissatisfied about us and does not welcome us, and he was venting his fury through a barrage just now?

"Right, I was right. He does not know that Sister Long, Xiaoming, Wenwen, and I are here. So, he is only dissatisfied with and does not welcome Brother Yao, right?"

"You… You must be kidding."

Li Yao swallowed hard and wiped the cold sweat on his forehead. "Boss Bai and I are the closest friends. This is just the way space pirates greet each other. The more intimidating a brother is, the fiercer barrage you will use to welcome him. That was just salvo! You must know what salvo is, don't you?"

"Is that so?"

Li Jialing narrowed his eyes in great suspicion. "But when the overwhelming barrage almost drowned us a moment ago, Brother Yao obviously shrank your neck and was sweating hard with a gloomy face. Was it really a normal reaction when you were faced with a salvo?"

Li Yao: "… We are in the middle of an emergency. Let's seize every second instead of talking about the irrelevant stuff. Now, it's time for us to make a grand entrance with the most spectacular sound and visual effects! Long Yangjun, are you ready?"

"I'm always ready."

Long Yangjun, who had been leaning against the wall with her eyebrows furrowed, as if she were trying to hold something back, said casually, "But regarding the current situation, in order not to let any remnant of the Flying Rainbow Fleet run loose, I suddenly have a new idea. Why don't we split apart?"

"Split apart?"

Li Yao was slightly dazed. "How are we going to split?"

"I, Li Jialing, Xiaoming, and Wenwen will fight on one side."

Long Yangjun said, "And you will fight on the other side. In such a way, you will have nobody to hinder you when you are on a killing spree, and your unparalleled, terrifying combat ability can be fully unleashed."

"Huh?"

Li Yao blinked. "Is that so?"

"Yes, of course."

Long Yangjun nodded her head. "And I believe Li Jialing, Xiaoming, and Wenwen all agree with such a plan."

Chapter 2475: Countless Stars, Countless Heroes!

"Is that so?"

Li Yao waved his hands randomly. "It doesn't matter now. Let's just march out of here. My bloodthirsty saber has been starved for a long time!"

Against the aftermath of the barrage, he rushed to the back cabin.

The carrier was not in a complicated structure. After leaving the bridge and passing a long pathway, Li Yao reached the large warehouse, which was supposed to store goods.

But at this moment, it was the stink of fear that flowed out of the warehouse when Li Yao pushed the thick alloy gate. The warehouse was fully loaded with the civilians who were in bloody, ragged clothes, with horror and sorrow on their faces. It was like an iron can crammed with rotten meat.

Xiaoming and Wenwen were trying to comfort the people who had luckily survived death, but the two of them were only kids no more than ten on the surface. How could they be persuasive with their cute cheeks and their young voices?

The moment they saw Li Yao, everybody was like someone being drowned who saw a lifebuoy. Their eyes gleaming with hope, they extended their hands at Li Yao.

"Master Li, the fire outside is so fierce. What happened? Did the pursuers catch up?"

"Please help us, Master Li. We really don't want to fight. We haven't received any military training and do not know how to fight at all!"

"Master Li, a lot of our families are still imprisoned in the starships of the Flying Rainbow Fleet. Are they still fine? They…"

The adults, the seniors, the children, the women, the sick, and the tortured were all weeping in misery and devastation.

"Dad…"

Xiaoming and Wenwen were somewhat at a loss too. Looking back at Li Yao, they frowned, as if they did not understand why the war in the human world had to be fought in such an absurd and shameless way.

Li Yao took a deep, deep breath to hold back his fury, particularly when he saw that some children who were no more than ten years old had been captured by the Immortal Cultivators to be sent to the battlefield.

Every time he saw such a scene, he felt that all the tranquilization skills that he had practiced in the past hundred years were all useless, and he seemed to be back to the young man who did whatever he wanted to do in the past, blowing the scum into real "scum" without caring about anything!

"As I said yesterday, I am not a master. I am absolutely different from those 'masters' of the Imperium."

Li Yao said slowly, "You may call me 'Ah Yao' or 'Brother Yao'. The only master of anyone can only be themselves.

"The barrages in the next second may be a bit fierce. While our carrier probably won't be hit, the spiritual waves may affect our power units and make the carrier shake violently. So, I'm here to remind you to enshroud yourselves and fix yourselves to the wall with the protective gels. Remember, do not expose any body part to the air, particularly not the head. It will be bad if you hit your head and bleed!

"As for the rest, I'm going to take care of it!

"I assure you as a Cultivator that you will all be fine. Your families that have been captured by the Immortal Cultivators will be fine too. I will rescue all of them and make the animals who have tarnished the dignity of the strong, the honor of soldiers, and the boundaries of mankind pay a price a hundred times greater!"

For a moment, golden and bloody lights were flying out of Li Yao's eyes at the same time!

"Brother Li Yao…"

Astounded by Li Yao's daunting vibe, everybody fell silent.

But a little girl with a headful of messy hair and a pale face stepped forward and said, biting her lips, "Grandma said that you are the most, most, most awesome hero, so you can certainly drive the bad guys away and save my mom. Is that so? I miss my mom. I want my mom…"

The little girl began to sob.

A lot of other children widened their eyes and looked at Li Yao in expectation. Just like the little girl, they were forced to be separated from their parents.

It was in such a way that the Flying Rainbow Fleet coerced the civilians to become moths that would dart at the fire "willingly". The Immortal Cultivators had captured the underage children and locked them on an independent starship, threatening their parents that if they did not charge in the front, the starship that was loaded with their children would.

In such a way, the troop of cannon fodder would have "high morale". The civilians whose children had been held hostages would go to die obediently without a gun pointed to their heads.

Before finding Boss Bai, Li Yao was too outnumbered to rescue everyone. Naturally, he got the carrier where the children were locked away first. So, there were particularly plenty of children on board.

It was rather weird that, although Li Yao had never been fond of children in the beginning, especially not the mischievous and unpredictable ones, after he discovered that he became a father and had two super mischievous and super unpredictable children that were Xiaoming and Wenwen, he somehow found the other children much more agreeable than before, and he often felt the urge of sacrificing everything to protect and fight for them.

"Little Lingdang…"

Li Yao fell on one of his knees and tried to hold back his killing intent and put on a kindly expression while raising his lips that were still cramping because of fury.

Because the girl had the same name as Ding Lingdang, she left a particularly deep impression on Li Yao. Last night, he had chatted with the girl and comforted her for a long time before she finally fell asleep.

It was also the reason why "Little Lingdang" had the courage to speak to him when he looked all aggressive and murderous.

"Didn't Brother Li Yao tell you a lot of stories about the heroes in the universe yesterday?"

Li Yao held Little Lingdang's shoulders and delivered a stream of tranquilizing spiritual energy, warming up Little Lingdang's body like a warm flow. "About the most, most, most awesome hero, I'm certainly not one. Even if I am a hero, I'm just the most, most, most small and insignificant one. Here, just as small as this finger."

Li Yao shook his little finger with a rather comical look on his face, making the worried little girl giggle. A lot of other children behind her laughed too.

Li Yao laughed at himself. Coughing for a moment, he said, "Let me tell you, there are a lot of superheroes who are way more awesome than me, like Boss Bai or the Fist King. They are also good guys with a strong sense of justice and integrity, and they like to help the people in need best!

"Naturally, it is not enough to save your parents or to terminate this war with only my strength, but together with the strengths of those superheroes and good guys, it will be more or less enough.

"Listen. Can you hear the thunderous noises when the spiritual waves are blowing at the shell of our starship? That's the heroes like Boss Bai and the Fist King who have received Brother Yao's cry for help, learned what happened here, and therefore come to save you and your parents without caring about anything!"

"So…"

Little Lingdang was moved. In a trance, she mumbled, "Brother Yao didn't lie to us. There are real heroes. There are so many of them!"

"That's right."

Li Yao nodded his head heavily and glanced around at all the little kids. "Trust Brother Li Yao. No matter how dark the world is in your eyes, there is light, there is justice, there are heroes, and there will forever be in the future!

"There are as many stars in the sky as there are heroes in the universe. Sometimes, the light of the stars is shadowed by the dark clouds, but in the end…

"The dark clouds will be ripped apart by us!"

"Ah…"

Li Yao's words made all the little kids forget the dread of the bombardment and the pain of being separated from their families for a moment. In their trance, they seemed to have entered a new world, one where the clouds were torn into pieces, the darkness faded away, and the heroes shined as brilliantly as stars!

"So, Little Lingdang, stop thinking, and just go to the jelly gel with your friends for a nice sleep."

Li Yao smiled. "When you wake up, your parents will be right before you."

"Okay!"

Little Lingdang nodded her head heavily. "I believe in Brother Li Yao. Thank you, Brother Li Yao. Brother Li Yao is the best!"

"Hehe, you don't need to thank me. It's mainly Boss Bai that you need to thank."

Scratching his messy hair, Li Yao said, "Right, you haven't forgotten what I taught you yesterday, about what you should do after we meet Boss Bai, have you?"

"Of course not, Brother Li Yao said that Boss Bai is a kind and lovely grandpa who loves children best."

Little Lingdang said innocently, "So, after the bad guys are kicked away, Brother Li Yao will bring us to meet Boss Bai. The moment we see him, we will run to him and cling to his thigh and call him 'Grandpa Bai' in the sweetest voice. Then, he will be greatly touched. When he is touched, he will offer a lot of resources to us, or even give us some starships for us to live in with our mom, dad, grandfather, and grandmother temporarily. We will be able to hide somewhere unknown and rebuild our home after the war is over!"

"Exactly, Brother Li Yao has entrusted the matter to you, Little Lingdang. Remember to practice more with your friends."

Rubbing the little girl's head, Li Yao said, "Remember, you must cling to his thigh tightly and do not let him go. Also, you must learn the rhetoric that I taught you by your heart, well, namely the things about how he is a superhero and how he is a man of integrity and compassion, so that you can speak them aloud in public occasions when there are a lot of people. Trust me. Brother Yao is such a good man. How can I possibly deceive you? Boss Bai will certainly be moved to tears by what you do, and then he will satisfy all your wishes whatever they are!"

"Okay!"

All the children nodded their heads solemnly, eager to carry out their task.

Some of them were already practicing how to cling to Boss Bai's thigh and how to adulate him as the greatest man in the entire universe.

"Let's go!"

The daunting vibe was back to Li Yao. He waved his hands in excitement at Xiaoming and Wenwen, who had been dumbfounded by what he did. "It's time to blow up the Flying Rainbow Fleet and reunite with Boss Bai!"

Chapter 2476: Are You Prepared to Die?

Inside the maintenance warehouse of the ragged carrier, Li Yao was finishing the last preparations on the crystal suit and the Colossus and setting up the most appropriate magical equipment.

Xiaoming and Wenwen were also testing the space shuttles and the spiritual puppets that were suitable for the battle in space. In terms of combat style, they were a lot like the Fist King.

"Dad, Xiaoming and I have been thinking for a long time, but we still want to ask—"

While testing, Wenwen asked carefully, "Is it really decent to deceive the children no more than ten years old? Or maybe, have you decided to embark on the journey of becoming a supervillain, so it doesn't matter however shameless you are?"

"What are you talking about? What's 'deceive' and 'shameless' about?"

Li Yao said matter-of-factly, "That was just 'necessary modesty'. In front of those kids, I naturally could only say that I was a minor, insignificant hero. Should I tell them the truth that I'm a super invincible hero who saves the world as frequently as they drink water every day? That would've sounded too arrogant!"

Xiaoming: "… Nobody is talking about that! We were talking about how you described Boss Bai as a good guy, a hero, a man of honor and integrity who always helped those in need, and the craps like that!"

"Well, about that—"

Li Yao chuckled and said, "That was not entirely a lie. In fact, there are not so many congenital good guys or bad guys. What matters more is the environment and whether or not somebody leads them in the front and pushes them from behind.

"Boss Bai was perhaps not a good guy in the past, but if I push him in the back or even kick him in his bottom heavily, I may be able to kick him to the side of the good guys, right?

"Do you know that being a good guy can be addictive in many cases? It may be uncomfortable in the beginning, but before you know it, you'll be addicted and can no longer get rid of it. Hahahaha!

"Right, let's talk about that later. Just now, Long Yangjun proposed to split apart. What do you say? Are you going with me or with her?"

Xiaoming and Wenwen looked at each other thoughtfully.

"We'll go with Aunt Long."

Wenwen said gently, "This is our first official appearance in a space battlefield of human beings after all. The first impression is too important. We need to try to leave a… good impression on most human beings."

"Fine, you can go with her. Just be careful!"

Li Yao filled his crystal suit with fuel and put it on. Then, he suddenly realized that something was wrong. "Wait. What do you mean by 'a good impression'? Why do you think that you can leave a good impression if you go with Long Yangjun instead of me? What's the meaning of that?"

He was going to pursue further, only to discover that the kids had been carried away by more than ten spiritual puppets in a hurry.

"Dad, we are running out of time. Whatever you want to say, let's talk later!"

Crawling into the space shuttles that had been installed with super crystal processors and the spiritual powers of the highest level, making themselves enough to be the hotspots of the Spiritual Nexus in space, the kids flew out of the carrier with countless spiritual puppets around and joined Long Yangjun.

"What unlovely kids."

Li Yao mumbled and absorbed the Colossus into his Cosmos Ring. Exiting the carrier through the double airtight gates, he entered the heated space battlefield too. "The situation doesn't look good. I can only talk to Boss Bai after I finish the battle in three minutes!"

At this moment, the situation was indeed unfavorable for the federal space pirates and the Cultivators.

The Big Bai Pirate Gang and the Cultivators' fleet had just been through a fierce battle. They did not have the time to refill the lost fuel and ammunitions, and the damage on the starships was not repaired either. It was not hard to imagine how loose and weak their siege was under such circumstances.

However mediocre the Flying Rainbow Fleet was, it was a local armed force at the edge of the Imperium that had just been upgraded with a batch of powerful starships after all. It had its own tusks and claws.

They had condensed their starships into the tightest battle formation. Their firepower density in the area was multiple times higher than that of the Big Bai Pirate Gang. Soon after the battle began, the Big Bai Pirate Gang's siege was about to be ripped apart.

"Boss, this is not going to work out. Let's change the battle formation!"

On Alloy Virus, Xiao Lishui, the dutiful chief of staff, proposed again, "If we fight on like this, our circle will be completely broken and collapsed by the Flying Rainbow Fleet in less than half an hour!

"The enemy is even scanning the center of our information streams and about to lock onto our flagship!

"With their dense firepower right now, should they attack our flagship, it would be possible that even Alloy Virus would be blown up!"

"What's the hurry?"

Even though the flagship started to take the hit from the enemy, Boss Bai was still as calm as ever. He crossed his hands below his nose and said, "Just wait for a while longer."

"What are we waiting for?"

Xiao Lishui was anxious. "There won't be enough time if we don't transform now!"

"We wait for—"

Boss Bai glanced at Xiao Lishui and stared at the few seemingly plain light spots on the light beam that represented the ragged carriers, while he gnashed his teeth and said, "—Li Yao to finish his trick. In any case, this is exactly like the last time. We can only trust that this a*shole… can bring a new miracle!"

"Ahhhhhhhhhh!"

Eight hundred kilometers from Alloy Virus, on "Stainless Mouse", a super arsenal warship of the Big Bai Pirate Gang with the greatest firepower, Han Te was also distributing firepower in a hurry, unleashing the destructive force together with his roars. "Too many! Too many! Too many! Why are there so many hostile warships in our front? It's impossible to take down all of them!

"Why did Boss Bai make such a weird battle formation? Did he think that he could blow up so many hostile warships because the future 'Best Banger' is helping him?

"You are truly… overestimating me, Boss Bai!

"Yes, of course, that must be it. Hehehehe. Since Boss Bai has such high hopes in me, I will certainly not let him down. Come on. Let the storm be more violent. Even if there were ten times more hostile warships, I'm going to blow you up one after another into smithereens!"

On the other side of the battlefield, on "Iron Fist", the flagship of Starlight, the Fist King's computational ability had been fully carried out too. The array of a hundred super crystal processors that made up his main brain was screeching and squeaking nonstop, with smoke popping up.

Liu Li controlled the ventilation and cooling rune arrays to lower the temperature of the Fist King's brain while she exclaimed in shock.

"Master Fist King, you can't do that!

"Stop the calculation to increase the precision of shooting. If you continue, the No. 17 crystal processor on your left side will explode in twenty seconds, and another 0.9% of your computational ability will be lost!

"What are you doing exactly? You are not sparing anything for further changes now that you have devoted all your computational ability since the moment the battle began. What are we going to do later?"

Seeing that the Fist King did not cherish his brain at all, Liu Li was so angry that she was almost batting his iron skull.

"Don't worry, Miss Liu Li."

The Fist King was standing next to Liu Li in a common spiritual puppet as the main vehicle for him to move around. From the broadcast rune arrays, a low and warm voice spread out. "The reinforcements are about to come. It doesn't matter even if all the computational ability runs out as long as we persist until that moment. Then, we will just watch him play."

"Huh?"

Liu Li was stunned. Looking at the burning space displayed on the virtual light beam, she asked, "What reinforcements can possibly resist a whole charging fleet?"

At the center of the battlefield, centered at the flagship, the Flying Rainbow Fleet had indeed accelerated to the highest speed and was about to crash into the area that had the densest information traffic and where Alloy Virus was at.

It appeared that the sparse firepower around Alloy Virus could barely resist the torrent of the Flying Rainbow Fleet and would likely be separated from the other starships around.

On the bridge of the Flying Rainbow Fleet, people were already talking and laughing.

"Those nobles are just pretentious phonies who do not know war at all. They will be blown apart in such a weird battle formation very quickly!"

"They must've fought the garrison of the Blue Sky Market just now, which is why their starships are damaged and their spiritual shields are not in the best shape. Why do they want to swallow their 'ally' under such circumstances? What are the nobles thinking really?"

"It doesn't matter. After their siege is broken and they are separated, we will know what they are up to. Our defense is justified in this conflict, and the victory will be easy. It won't matter even if the case is reported to the four Kurfürsten!"

"Let's go! Let's go! Let's go!"

But only three seconds later, the merry atmosphere was disrupted by a stammering exclamation.

"R-Report, an extremely-intense high-energy reaction has been detected right… right on our fleet's way!"

…

The infinite brilliant stars in the vast space were eclipsed by the fire of destruction that human beings set with their wisdom and hatred.

Right ahead of the marching Flying Rainbow Fleet, where the destructive fire was densest, Li Yao floated in the sweltering vacuum while wearing only the thin crystal suit. He watched the hundreds of enlarging warships that were coming at him like shooting stars from not far away.

"Let's confirm one last time—"

Li Yao asked Xiaoming and Wenwen in the communication channel. "According to the intelligence after you hacked the flagship of the Flying Rainbow Fleet, there are no captives on the flagship, right?"

"No."

Xiaoming and Wenwen said, "The flagship is of paramount importance and has limited space. Naturally, the unimportant captives would not be kept in such a critical place."

"That's good."

Li Yao said, "On the other hand, you are mostly done with the hacking, cracking, and duplicating work of the battle network of the Flying Rainbow Fleet, right? I don't need you to make a second 'command center of the Flying Rainbow Fleet'. As long as you can disrupt the enemy's information transmission after I succeed, it will be enough.

"To put it more simply, once I chop off the head of the Flying Rainbow Fleet, I do not want them to grow a second one. Can you do that?"

"Rest assured, Dad."

Xiaoming and Wenwen said lightheartedly, "We've been dealing with the Flying Rainbow Fleet for such a long time exactly because we are compromising their battle network and planting all the 'viruses' and 'bombs', haven't we? It's all gone. There won't be a problem at all!"

"Understood!"

Faced with the main forces of the Flying Rainbow Fleet that were rushing close, Li Yao straightened his arms and opened his hands, while the Cosmos Ring in which the Colossus was stored emitted a weird and bloody brilliance. "Then… prepare to die now!"

Chapter 2477: One Attack Is Enough!

The weird and blood brilliance spread out like ripples that had been dyed by Li Yao's fighting will, turning into a network of nerves and veins in space.

Then, based on the network, the various magical equipment units and limbs of the Colossus broke the boundary of the three-dimensional space and appeared, like a giant mixed with wrath and discontent that had crawled out of the invisible Styx!

The Colossus "Arsonist" arrived at the battlefield where the fire was densest, right before the hundred main warships of the Flying Rainbow Fleet that were rushing forward!

Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep!

The high-energy reaction that felt like a supernova outbreak left a burning, enlarging figure of a human on the scanning magical equipment of the starships of the two parties. The rhythming waves formed crests that rose to the sky and the bottomless troughs, teaching everyone the meaning of "horror" in the most straightforward way.

Some of the Immortal Cultivators above the Nascent Soul Stage even heard the thirsty roars of the Arsonist with their keen senses.

…

"What kind of strength is this?"

Han Te, Liu Li, the leadership of the Flying Rainbow Fleet, and the common soldiers of the two parties were all overwhelmed by the unexpected high-energy reaction and exclaimed in disbelief.

It was not because they had not considered the possibility that a Colossus would show up on the battlefield, but even for the Colossi, this Colossus that had crawled out of the Styx with brighter and brighter flames had shattered all the boundaries of common sense the moment it appeared.

The high-energy reaction was truly unbelievably powerful!

…

"Is this… Li Yao?"

The Fist King quickly analyzed the spiritual waves released by the Arsonist; his cold voice filled with intense confusion. "His Colossus seems to be different from the one with which he sabotaged 'Manjusaka, the City in the Sky' or the 'Gold Vulture' that appeared with the reformists. It is now almost too powerful to be calculated.

"How is it possible? What kind of odds could've possibly caused—"

…

"Haha. Li Yao, Li Yao!"

The Fist King had been in touch with Boss Bai through remote communication. Naturally, his confusion was heard by Boss Bai. While laughing crazily, Boss Bai said, "Odds are for the weak. The real experts, like I told you earlier, are bold enough to roll up their sleeves and do it as long as there is a one-billionth chance!

"Li Yao, there's no reason why you can't figure out our status. Since you have distracted so many starships of the Flying Rainbow Fleet to my place, I'm sure you have a way to settle all of them? Come on. Let's see how much you—the jerk, the lunatic, and the monster—have expanded since you came to the center of the cosmos!"

…

"Why? This isn't Li Wuji's Colossus, 'Lotus Buddha'. We do not have such a Colossus in our database at all!"

"Where does this maniac come from? Is he really going to die with us?"

"Get out of the way, you crazy person!"

The bridge of the Flying Rainbow Fleet was filled with screams too.

The leaders of the Flying Rainbow Fleet were, naturally, aware that Li Wuji boasted a Colossus, but Colossi were not absolutely invincible. Now that they were pushing forward while barraging in such a dense battle formation, even Li Wuji with his "Lotus Buddha" had to get out of their way.

Otherwise, even if Li Wuji could take down a few main warships of the Flying Rainbow Fleet, he would have to pay a gory price, causing irreversible wounds to himself as well as his Colossus.

The commander of the Flying Rainbow Fleet could not come up with a single possibility why Li Wuji would play such a dangerous game where nobody was a winner with them.

Little did they expect that standing in the way of the rushing Flying Rainbow Fleet was not "Lotus Buddha", but a mysterious Colossus whose spiritual waves were… countless times more powerful than the Lotus Buddha!

Hearing the tides of spiritual energy from Li Yao's heart blasting their bridge nonstop, and looking at the almost exploding data fed back by the scanning magical equipment, how could they not be intimidated?

But the whole fleet had been accelerated to the highest speed, like a bull whose fury had been entirely raised. There was no time to decelerate or change the direction now.

Besides, at such a close distance, the Colossi were much more agile than the starships. They had nowhere to run to at all but could only crash into it while wishing that the enemy was merely disrupting their scanning magical equipment with certain weird techniques, resulting in the wrong data.

"Impossible. It's absolutely impossible. If such an energy reaction is true, he will be one of the best experts of the Imperium. Why would such an expert appear in such an insignificant battlefield or have the time to bother with our trivial matter?"

"Illusions! Illusions! All are illusions! You can't trick me!"

On the bridge of the flagship of the Flying Rainbow Fleet, the helpless howls congregated into a rolling torrent.

…

If the two parties did not change their route, the main warships of the Flying Rainbow Fleet, centered at the flagship, would crash into Li Yao's "Arsonist" in ten seconds.

As a matter of fact, overwhelming light and barrages, which seemed to have filled up the entire universe, had been shot at the Arsonist.

Li Yao inhaled and exhaled, feeling that thousands of his brain cells were blooming in every corner of his brain. In a trance, he entered a very self-contradictory new world.

On one hand, after Xiaoming and Wenwen's treatment and upgrade, and since part of the secrets of Earth had been unlocked, his understanding about the universe reached a whole new level, allowing him to view the universe more calmly, rationally, and objectively.

The heated space battlefield, and the raging barrages ahead of him, all turned into the purest lines, odds, and numbers, causing not the slightest disturbance.

On the other hand, the crimes that the Immortal Cultivators had committed on the ordinary people—the slaughter game on the Land of Sins, the dark exploitation below the ground of the capital, and how the Immortal Cultivators were capturing the ordinary people, chaining them up and threatening them with their children to fight a war against their wishes.

Such ugliness and evilness raised the deepest and purest fury inside Li Yao's heart, making the expert who was vaguely beyond the Divinity Transformation Stage so angry that he was like an eighteen-year-old man, with actual fire flowing out of his every pore.

"Don't the scumbags like you call yourselves the elites of the Imperium and the experts of mankind? Can't you scramble for power and wealth on your own instead of conducting them in such cruel and detestable ways?

"You say that the resources are limited, you say humanity is full of darkness, but why can't you summon your courage and wisdom to explore the infinite worlds beyond the three thousand Sectors and search for new resources when the universe is such a vast place? Even if you can't find any, even if it's impossible to cross the almost boundless black wall, the civilization of mankind will at least end splendidly!

"You do not have the courage to be pioneers and can only hide in your home and bully the people who are weaker than you. What 'experts' are you? Cowards! You are the most sordid, shameless, and hideous cowards!

"Hehe. You cowards are not qualified to enjoy everything at the center of the cosmos at all. Let me send you vile maggots to hell!"

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Faced with the overwhelming barrages and light, the Arsonist instantly turned into an unpredictable phantom that penetrated through the Flying Rainbow Fleet's battle formation and appeared right before the flagship.

Above the right arm that had been raised high, gold daggers as thin as paper were spurting out of the Cosmos Rings. When the sharp blades emerged at the vortex of spiritual energy, they were like a gold fountain—no, a gold volcano!

The gold volcano unleashed infinite feathers that formed a saber a hundred meters long, and the saber's burning flames were further boosted to thousands of meters away, clearly visible in the entire battlefield.

The Arsonist raised the saber high and charged at the flagship of the Flying Rainbow Fleet, with light that was even more brilliant than a full-speed starship jetting out of its back. In the most fearless and magnificent gesture, it became the sole focus of everyone on the battlefield.

The two parties were both rushing at the highest speed. Nothing could possibly stop their collision.

"What-What the—"

Both the common warriors who did not know what was going on, such as Han Te and Liu Li, and the leaderships who knew part of the truth and Li Yao's intention, like Boss Bai and the Fist King, were greatly surprised. The regular way for a Colossus to fight a starship was to dig into it and sabotage the bridge, the arsenal, and the power units. But judging from Li Yao's gesture, he did not intend to dig in at all. He was trying to destroy the starship from the outside in a head-on clash!

"How is it possible!"

Devastating screams burst out on the bridge of the flagship of the Flying Rainbow Fleet. "What kind of weirdo is this? Have you really lost your mind?"

In the Immortal Cultivators' cries of disbelief, the thousand meters of spiritual energy simmering between the Arsonist's hands had been condensed into half-solid gold by Li Yao's fury. Not bothering to look for any weakness or flaw, he simply slashed it at the head of the Flying Rainbow Fleet's flagship, where the spiritual shield and the armor were thickest.

Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi!

The spiritual energy almost a thousand meters long was a destructive, unstoppable torrent, which tore apart the spiritual shield of the Flying Rainbow Fleet's flagship after only half a second's impasse, before leaving the deepest gorge on the super-alloy armor. It was partly because of Li Yao's own sharpness, but more important, it was because the two parties' momentums had both been transformed into the most destructive damage. It was almost like Li Yao raised the saber and kept still while the Flying Rainbow Fleet's flagship crashed it at the highest speed on its own. The only result was that—

Shua!

The furious auras of the saber that had been extended to thousands of meters long directly pierced through the flagship, making a shocking, melted damage appear in the middle part of the flagship, which completely separated the flagship into two parts.

Under the watch of countless people who had been stunned, Li Yao had chopped off the head of the Flying Rainbow Fleet's flagship!

Chapter 2478: Unstoppable and Invincible!

The vast sea of stars was reduced to a small pond at the moment because the dominating vibe of the Arsonist that was as violent as a fiend was utterly unbearable.

The astounding attack did not only slash the flagship of the Flying Rainbow Fleet but also the hearts of all the onlookers, whose pupils and pores were constricting at the same time. The people of lower levels who were not firm enough were even covering their chests and exclaiming uncontrollably.

Even the stars seemed to be shaking and dimming because of Li Yao's slash!

The Flying Rainbow Fleet's flagship was like an executed criminal who had been cut into two pieces from the right shoulder blade. The front part and the rear part had been separated, revealing an astonishing transversal surface that was almost one kilometer in diameter. The cutting part made of the cabins, which had been exposed to the vacuum, was like a complicated maze whose cover had been opened. The scene was even more dazzling and intimidating than a direct explosion of the warship.

"After only one attack… such a gigantic warship was beheaded?"

"How is it possible? Even if it is a Colossus, it can't be so unbelievable!"

"What kind of Colossus is this? Who's using this… godlike Colossus? How did he do it? That's unreasonable! Didn't he slash from the front? Why has the warship been 'beheaded' instead of being cut into two parts from the middle?"

Confusion, shock, and fear were surging in everyone's heart, even completely swallowing their thinking and depriving them of their normal reaction.

Only the super experts such as Boss Bai and the Fist King, when replaying Li Yao's flabbergasting attack just now that had been captured from various perspectives at a much lower speed, could figure out what exactly Li Yao had done.

The attack seemed simple, untechnical, and purely about violence and brutality, but it actually involved very sophisticated knowledge of spiritual energy, structure studies, material science, and even magnetic fields.

The attack of the Arsonist from the front did not just damage the spiritual shield of the Flying Rainbow Fleet's flagship, but it also released special waves by the quakes of the spiritual energy of a rather high frequency, thereby raising the "resonation" of the spiritual shield on the Flying Rainbow Fleet's flagship.

Such "resonation" significantly reduced the hardness of the spiritual shield, intensified the inner stress of the starship's armor, and more importantly, greatly reduced the speed of the starship's head. It was like a gigantic hand pressing the head of the starship and lowering its speed to one-tenth of the original instantly.

The problem was that the enormous starship had very powerful inertia. When the head of the starship was stopped, the body and the rear were still keeping the highest speed to rush forward.

As a result, the latter half of the Flying Rainbow Fleet's flagship turned into a ten-thousand-ton hammer that crashed into and squeezed the front, leading to countless tiny fractures and cracks on the overall structure of the starship.

The area where the fractures and cracks were most focused was exactly one third to the front of the starship, the place that was equaled to the "neck".

It was not until this moment that the saber auras of the "Arsonist" finally burst out and slashed with a slant downward on the place where the fractures and cracks were densest and where the structure hardness was completely gone, chopping off the head of the gigantic iron beast.

If the replay was viewed from different perspectives, it could be seen that Li Yao's almost perfect slash had a very large arc, which resulted in the obvious deviation when the Flying Rainbow Fleet's flagship brushed past him. The helical force raised in the process boosted the damage of the attack to the maximum.

One attack in the head could've pierced through the bridge and also destroyed the starship, but it was certainly impossible to cut a flagship almost ten kilometers long into two halves.

In terms of the visual blast and the heart-exploding shock, it would've been far worse than "beheading"!

"Li Yao, you…"

After witnessing and analyzing the scene that almost looked like a "miracle", Boss Bai heaved a long sigh and thought for a long time before he mumbled, "I don't know how I should comment on you now!"

"Where exactly do you people come from?"

The Fist King also raised doubts coldly in the communication channel. "Why can you always ignore 'probability', 'common sense', and 'extremity'?"

The whole battlefield seemed to have been frozen after the shocking and unstoppable saber beheaded the Flying Rainbow Fleet's flagship. The two parts of the flagship, under the protection of the spiritual shield that had not entirely vanished, were actually still intact. It could even be seen that many dumbfounded Immortal Cultivators were observing the tangible universe as if they were in a dream.

But the ice lasted only half a second before it was broken by destructive explosions.

The contradictory inertia of the Flying Rainbow Fleet's flagship still existed. The rear part from which the power units were still spurting at full speed crashed into the front part that was pressed by Li Yao heavily. This time, the two halves of the starship whose framework had entirely collapsed were squeezed together. In the next second, fireballs that absorbed everything burst out, and the whole starship exploded in no more than three seconds. Not a single Immortal Cultivator or an escape capsule managed to get out.

The fire caused by the explosions condensed into a magnificent palace, of which Li Yao slowly walked out with the Arsonist, stepping on the void and covered in the wings of light. The remaining souls of countless Immortal Cultivators were hovering around him, screaming miserably and struggling hard, only to be burnt up by him with spiritual energy mercilessly. The scene stunned the warriors of the Big Bai Pirate Gang and the Cultivators' fleet and frightened the Immortal Cultivators of the Flying Rainbow Fleet.

Huchi… Huchi… Huchi… Huchi… Huchi…

Li Yao's own spiritual energy and soul power had been exploited to the maximum in order to perform the glamorous attack of beheading the starship in one move. It was barely even possible to keep his lungs steady. He breathed heavily.

Everybody had his limits. Neither a Divinity Transformation Stage expert nor a Colossus was absolutely invincible. Even such a monster as Li Yao could only perform one of those attacks at a time. Then, he would need to rest for at least half an hour and refill abundant spiritual energy and fuel before he performed another such attack.

But for Li Yao today, one attack was enough.

"Kids…"

Spectacular brilliance surged out of Li Yao's eyes, and he put on a brilliant smile. He waved the light saber that was glowing unpredictably and pointed at the shuddering Flying Rainbow Fleet. "Help your father sweep across the Flying Rainbow Fleet!"

Xiaoming and Wenwen took action.

The infiltration and hacking in the past few days allowed them to master the features and weaknesses of the battle network of the Flying Rainbow Fleet.

When the flagship was still out there, the battle network was heavily defended and probably unassailable for their young brains.

However, after Li Yao "beheaded" the flagship, a shocking, irrecoverable hole appeared at the center of the battle network of the Flying Rainbow Fleet.

Even if another starship, which had carried enough super crystal processors, was going to make up for the hole and replace the flagship as the "second flagship", it would take them at least half to one minute to reset the whole command system and reconnect all the starships.

It was exactly during the crucial half-minute that Xiaoming and Wenwen crawled into the battle network of the Flying Rainbow Fleet. They disrupted the enemy during the generation of the "second starship". At the same time, they tossed "information bombs" that contained all kinds of garbage data and viruses to the battle network crazily. They even simulated the information features of the "second flagship", pretending that they were the new flagship of the Flying Rainbow Fleet.

For a moment, all the remaining starships of the Flying Rainbow Fleet were mired in the swirl of wrong data and commands. They were completely lost and had no idea what to do.

"Huh?"

On "Iron Fist", the flagship of the Cultivators' fleet, the Fist King keenly sensed the anomaly in the data stream and information waves. All the crystal cameras over his body were glittering. "My new… own kind? The universe is a vast place, and I'm indeed not unique!

"Then, welcome to be here, and allow me to join you!"

Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi!

From the special launch cabins of Iron Fist, countless unmanned space shuttles that looked like wild bees flew out. Those space shuttles did not carry too much firepower but boasted remarkable abilities in cyberwarfare. They were the Fist King's trump cards and most powerful weapons!

On "Alloy Virus", however, Boss Bai's attention was focused on the other side of the battlefield, where another Colossus had shown up, lunged to the area where the starships of the Flying Rainbow Fleet were densest, and were on an unstoppable killing spree. While it was not as dazzling as Li Yao's Colossus, the efficiency of its slaughter was not any lower.

That was Long Yangjun's "Heavenly Crystal".

"An expert who is new to the Divinity Transformation Stage but has reached the limits of the Divinity Transformation Stage, if not beyond?"

Boss Bai rubbed his chin hard. "Li Yao, how many cards do you have exactly, and where did you find such tough helpers? A*shole, the two Colossi of yours would have collapsed the Flying Rainbow Fleet on your own, couldn't you? You were truly shameless to transfer the trouble to me. That's really…"

He cursed for a while and rose abruptly, looking at Xiao Lishui, his chief of staff who had been stunned by what happened.

Still in a trance, Xiao Lishui said, "Boss…"

Boss Bai grinned. "What are you waiting for, Old Xiao? The Flying Rainbow Fleet has lost their commander, and their fighting will have been completely shattered. They are now no better than lambs waiting to be butchered.

"In the next second, you will command the fleet and shrink the circle of siege. Send a message to them openly and ask them to surrender. Let's try to finish the battle in one hour!"

While talking, Boss Bai turned around and walked out of the bridge.

"What about you?"

Xiao Lishui blurted out, "Boss, where are you going?"

"I—"

Boss Bai looked back at Li Yao's and Long Yangjun's Colossi on the light beam before he sniffed and said, "The Blue Sky Space Zone is our territory. Can we let that jerk Li Yao show off on our territory without doing anything? I have to go outside and warm up myself a little bit no matter what!

"I hope that the Flying Rainbow Fleet won't surrender too soon. Huh. However much he has grown, Li Yao must be out of strength after that attack, right? Scumbags of the Flying Rainbow Fleet, hurry up and attack him. Even I can barely refrain from hitting him after seeing his cocky appearance.

"Li Yao, just wait for it. I'm coming!"

Chapter 2479: Successful Reunion

Boss Bai's wish did not come true after all.

The Immortal Cultivators of the Flying Rainbow Fleet were too terrified by Li Yao's attack to even escape. They were all rigidified where they were, barely able to stand on their feet. How could they have the courage to attack the godlike "Arsonist"?

Also, the corrosion of Xiaoming, Wenwen, and the Fist King had completely minced their whole command and coordination system, turning every starship into a lone and isolated island in the boundless sea of stars. They were no different from coffins floating in the vacuum.

Boss Bai's and Long Yangjun's Colossi, the "Kleptomaniac" and the "Heavenly Crystal", were the last two straws that broke the back of the camel.

If there were only one Colossus, it might've been possible for them to beat it back with dense barrages, but faced with three Colossi, the Flying Rainbow Fleet, which had been reduced into chaos after losing the commander, couldn't resist at all.

Now that neither fight nor flight was an option, all the remaining starships of the Flying Rainbow Fleet let out pale brilliance that was gradually condensed into a circle, like a bubble released by a dead fish. It was the general semaphore for surrender in the Imperium.

Eventually, in only half an hour, the brief skirmish was already over. One might even say that it was ended by Li Yao's one attack!

The Big Bai Pirate Gang and the Cultivators' fleet suffered far fewer losses than anticipated, and they gained unexpected trophies. Besides the tremendous starships of the Flying Rainbow Fleet, the most refreshing news was that Li Yao was here!

Everything Li Yao did in the Land of Sins, particularly the astounding performance during the destruction of "Manjusaka, the City in the Sky", had been spread throughout the Imperium via the ubiquitous Spiritual Nexus.

Although many people were unaware of his name, even the people in the darkest mines on the most remote resource planets were singing over the super Cultivator. Some even worshipped him, regarding him as the legendary Cultivator who had resisted the Imperium for a thousand years.

The central members of the Cultivators' fleet were all rescued by Li Yao in person from the Martial Meritocrats Sector. Naturally, they knew part of the truth and were full of gratitude and admiration for Li Yao.

It was needless to mention the leaders of the Big Bai Pirate Gang, who, as natives of the federation, certainly knew the "founding father of the federation, dominator of three Sectors". Besides, the picture where Li Yao emerged out of nowhere leading a dozen Colossi during the battle to resist the Black Wind Fleet was still fresh for them!

"Li Yao! Li Yao! Li Yao!"

His name and his awe-inspiring attack just now almost boosted the morale of the Big Bai Pirate Gang and the Cultivators' fleet by ten times. It was no exaggeration to say that they were bold enough to march at the best fleet of the Immortal Cultivators as long as Li Yao was leading them!

The celebrational atmosphere for the double triumphs reached the highest point when Alloy Virus, Iron Fist, Stainless Mouse, and the ragged carrier that Li Yao boarded on arrived at the spaceport of the Blue Sky Market. The whole port exploded because of Li Yao's arrival.

Naturally, Li Yao's style, which was almost identical to Boss Bai's, raised minor confusion.

But very soon, their confusion was broken by too many kids who ran past Li Yao with bouquets and balloons in their hands and jumped at Boss Bai.

"Grandpa Bai, thank you for saving us and our parents!"

"Grandpa Bai, you are a hero. You and Brother Li Yao are both the most, most, most awesome superheroes!"

"Grandpa Bai! Grandpa Bai! Grandpa Bai!"

Vehemently, the kids clambered on Boss Bai, who had been dumbfounded, if not crept out, with a twisted face. Some of the lovely fellas were even pouting their lips, hoping to stamp their saliva on Boss Bai's face.

"What a touching scene—"

Li Yao smiled at the scene and took a long breath in relief. "Another gamble has been won!"

While the kids ran at Boss Bai, Li Yao naturally had his own adoring public—Han Te and Liu Li, the two young people who had gradually grown up.

"Grandpa Yao!"

"Grandpa Yao!"

Han Te and Liu Li jumped at Li Yao, one from the left and the other from the right, but Han Te was faster and hugged Li Yao tightly.

"You're finally back, Grandpa Yao!"

Thrilled, Han Te was almost in tears. "I was told that you went through a lot of astonishing things when you were with the Immortal Cultivators in the capital. Liu Li and I were very worried about you, fearing that something might happen to you.

"But now that I think of it, haha, it was absolutely impossible. How could anything have happened to someone as awesome as Grandpa Yao?

"However, how exactly did you perform the attack, Grandpa Yao? It was too dazzling and unbelievable, wasn't it? Can you tell me how to do it? Aya, it suddenly occurs to me that practicing saber is not a bad idea at all. Practicing saber or practicing cannon-shooting, how should I choose? Speaking of which, a talented genius like me must be capable of mastering them both, am I not?"

Li Yao listened to Han Te with a smile. It was great to know that the young people who luckily escaped from the Land of Sins had reached Boss Bai's place safe and sound.

However, what was wrong with Liu Li? Why was she staying back and looked rather pensive?

"Liu Li—"

Li Yao waved at the girl and said in curiosity, "Is there anything wrong? Are you not happy to see me?"

"Of course I am very happy to see Grandpa Yao. It's just that I do not want to see the bummer Han Te!"

Pouting her lips, Liu Li accused him in front of Li Yao, her eyes red. "Grandpa Yao, Han Te has been mean to me!"

"Huh?"

Blinking quickly, Li Yao looked at Liu Li and then at Han Te, before he became grave. "Han Te, you and Liu Li are having an adventure outside of your hometown together. You should support each other. How can you be mean to her?"

"Grandpa Yao, I am not!"

Han Te raised his hands and complained, "I was never mean to Liu Li. I hadn't seen her for a long time until today. Hardly had we started talking when she burst into fury and stormed off. How could I have the time to be mean to her?"

"Is that so?"

Her arms akimbo, Liu Li glared at Han Te. "You didn't have time with me, but you had all the time with those big sisters of yours, didn't you?"

"You are being completely unreasonable!"

Han Te turned to Li Yao. "Grandpa Yao, please tell us who is right and who is wrong…"

Scratching his head, Li Yao knew what was going, but there was little he could do. "Han Te, if you have any super scheme, world-ending crisis, or if you need to save the universe, you can come to consult me. But regarding this… about the relationship of young men and young women, hehehehe, there is barely anything that Grandpa Yao is capable of. While Grandpa Yao is a married man, it was my wife who pursued me years back, so I am actually short of experience in that regard…"

…

On the other side of the port, the Fist King was also searching in the crowd with dozens of spiritual puppets.

Although Xiaoming and Wenwen looked no different from the kids that Li Yao saved, their unique brainwaves allowed the Fist King to distinguish them from the crowd easily.

A cold iron puppet, confining the excitement of finding its own kind, appeared before the two kids and released a friendly wave to them.

"Greetings, I am the Fist King. We already met each other in the space battlefield just now."

"Hello, Master Fist King. I am Xiaoming, I am Wenwen, we are Li Yao's children and the children of mankind."

After a brief communication, the kids crawled on the broad shoulder of the iron puppet, one on the left and the other on the right, before they walked to the depths of the port.

Those unique beings seemed to have a lot of information and data that they needed to share with each other.

…

Not far away, Long Yangjun roamed in the port casually and observed the warships that were as magnificent as mountains with her hands in her back.

Most of the warships still had the insignias of Li Wuji's fleet, the Flying Rainbow Fleet, or the twenty-eight pirate gangs painted on their heads, but they were like animals that had been obediently domesticated and followed the orders of the federal space pirates and the Cultivators of the Imperium.

After so many starships were gathered in the Blue Sky Market for a month of maintenance and upgrade, with the commandership of Boss Bai, the excellent warriors like Li Yao and Long Yangjun, and the chain of command as well as the cyberwarfare abilities that had been comprehensively strengthened by Xiaoming and Wenwen, Long Yangjun had no doubt that such a luxury coalition would be qualified to challenge any elite troop of the Imperium.

Although it was not enough to resist the reformists or the four families singlehandedly, it would certainly be enough to be an important leverage in the game of the two parties.

Not a long time ago, when they marched at the army of the Immortal Cultivators in the furious flames of the Great Iron Factory deep below the capital, she didn't foresee that such drastic changes would happen so soon.

"Li Yao…"

Long Yangjun combed her long hair and said with a smile, "One truly does not need to worry that their life may not be spectacular enough when they are with such an interesting guy like you!

"Is Blackstar the Great the ultimate enemy of the game? What wonders are coming next?"

…

The festivity in the port endured more than three hours, before the Cultivators, the federal space pirates, and the people who were just saved gradually dispersed and were relocated to appropriate places.

The panoramic image of the space battle just now, particularly the glamorous scene where Li Yao beheaded the flagship of the Flying Rainbow Fleet in one attack, was also displayed and replayed before the captives, destroying their last resistance.

The captives who were secretly planning to rise, after learning that the Big Bai Pirate Gang had swallowed the entire Flying Rainbow Fleet when they were exhausted and incomplete, were greatly appalled by Boss Bai's arts of commanding and Li Yao's astonishing combat ability.

Strength was the highest moral in the world of the Immortal Cultivators. Since Li Yao and Boss Bai were the strongest experts they had ever seen, there was not a second choice except to follow and pledge loyalty to them.

It took more than half a day to anchor the fleet, regroup the soldiers, settle the civilians, and lock up the captives. Finally, Boss Bai, the Fist King, Long Yangjun, Li Yao, and the rest of them were able to take a rest and sat down to talk about everything that had happened.

Chapter 2480: Have You Ever Heard of “Witchcraft”?

"The bottom line is, let's forget for now how Fellow Cultivator Long and I came to the Imperium, why I joined the reformists and worked for Queen Li Linghai, or the details like why I am wearing a battle robe that is in exactly the same style as Boss Bai's—"

Deep inside the Blue Sky Market, in the secret office that belonged to Zuo Tianying in the beginning, Li Yao spoke to Boss Bai and the Fist King passionately, "The most important thing is—listen up, the most important thing is that Blackstar the Great is back to life! He is now residing inside Queen Li Linghai's body! Right now, they have occupied the entire capital, and the great scheme they're up to inside the capital may eventually turn the center of the cosmos upside down!"

Boss Bai listened expressionlessly. His eyebrows did not shake at all even when he heard the news that Blackstar the Great had been revived. In the end, he simply said casually, "Okay."

"Okay?"

His eyes popping out, Li Yao looked at Boss Bai in disbelief. "Hey, hey, hey, Boss Bai, what's your reaction? I'm serious and not kidding. Wuying Qi, Blackstar the Great and the ancestor of the Immortal Cultivators, has been revived and may resume his peak combat ability anytime! Why are you not surprised at all?"

"There's nothing to be surprised at."

Boss Bai looked at Li Yao and said, "Since 'Vulture Li Yao' was involved, it was only natural that infinite schemes and trouble were caused. It appears to me that 'revival of Blackstar the Great' is already the slightest damage that you can possibly cause, isn't it? To be honest, I'm already grateful since you haven't triggered the super events such as 'revival of the Mad Armageddon'."

"What's that supposed to mean?"

Li Yao felt wronged. "It's not like I brought Wuying Qi back to life…"

"I'm more interested in the two kids here than in the revival of Blackstar the Great."

Interrupting Li Yao's self-defense, Boss Bai turned to Xiaoming and Wenwen curiously and grinned at the kids as a greeting.

Boss Bai jumped to the center of the cosmos immediately after the battle to resist the Black Wind Fleet in the federation without any delay. So, although he had heard Long Yangjun's name before, he did not know that Long Yangjun activated the Nuwa warship in the Ancient Sages Sector, looted an escape capsule, and broke all the defenses before she escaped.
