"Look, Long Yangjun, at your last moment in the Star Glory Federation, you seized an escape capsule of the Nuwa warship by force and left after activating a certain unknown teleportation technique. Too many experts of the federation watched your departure. A lot of them, including Ding Lingdang, Jin Xinyue, and people of the national security system, came to ask me about you. That was a lot of pressure, but I didn't tell them much. However, those experts and specialists were no fools. Even if I didn't say anything, they must've deduced many things from the traces.

"It's impossible that the federation will forever be out of contact with the Imperium. Once the federation intervened in the war between the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance in a certain way, the intelligence about you will certainly be sent over. How are you going to hide by then? Will you have even more intense conflicts with the federation, the Imperium, and the Covenant Alliance who have grasped incomplete intelligence?

"On many occasions, conflicts boil down to misunderstandings, and misunderstandings come from mistrust and secret-keeping. If you do not say who you are, the specialists of the federation will be obliged to make the worst guesses. Will that be nice?"

Long Yangjun sniffed and bit her lips, her eyes filled with the childishness of Pangu and Nuwa. "Do you think that I'm scared of having conflicts with the federation?"

"Yes, I know that you are not. I don't doubt that you are scared of nothing. But is it fun? Is it really fun?"

Li Yao opened his hands. "Keeping your own little secrets forever, wandering off the society, staying away from your own kind, not trusting anyone, and never being really devoted to anything—is it really fun and gratifying to live such a life until death?"

"Human beings are not my own kind!"

Long Yangjun gnashed her teeth and said in a low voice, "You seem to have forgotten it again. I'm an alien."

"No, you can be a human being, a real human being, as long as you want to!"

Li Yao said in a hurry, "Genetically speaking, human beings are not significantly different from the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan. As for the soul, who knows what it actually is? Also, as you said just now, even the original Pangu or Nuwa Clan used to be made of regular individuals who were made of flesh and blood and capable of loving and hating!

"Also, didn't we run into the poisons in the Pangu laboratory below the Nuwa warship in the Ancient Sages Sector, which could transform human beings into the Pangu Clan? The bodies of Meng Chixin and Wu Suiyun, the Divinity Transformation Stage experts of the Ancient Sages Sector, were changed into those of the Pangu Clan, weren't they?

"So, human beings are fundamentally the same as the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan. They are just the different appearances of the same carbon-based intelligent life. As long as you want, you will be an out-and-out human being. I believe that even if some of the people find you unacceptable, dreadful, or even detestable temporarily, most people will accept you, trust you, and support you given time. At least I can!"

Long Yangjun scorned, "Childish."

"Childish… It's indeed somewhat childish, but you are thinking exactly the same somewhere deep inside your heart, aren't you?"

Li Yao continued staring at Long Yangjun. "You are unwilling to live in loneliness forever as an 'alien'. You crave to meet your own kind, communicate with your own kind, and speak out all your secrets. That's the reason why you talked so much to me just now.

"In the boundless sea of stars, nobody is a lone island. Everybody yearns for communication, sharing, and living together with their own kind. I am not an exception, and neither are you.

"If you are as proud and indifferent as you claim to be, you shouldn't have told anybody anything. No, no, no, you shouldn't have shown it to me in the very beginning! We could've minded our own business and explore our respective secrets and origins. Wouldn't that have been great and safe?"

Long Yangjun's pupils constricted violently, as if her most sensitive concern had been brought up. There was even killing intent as she was embarrassed and infuriated by Li Yao.

"Make no mistake. I'm glad that you regarded me as a friend and were willing to talk to me, which also gave me an opportunity to chat with a friend and relax myself, sharing secrets with each other."

Li Yao did not respond to Long Yangjun's killing intent at all. With his hands open, he said aloud, "Trust me. Publicizing the so-called secrets won't be a big deal. In many cases, you are just scaring yourself.

"Just now, you said that the Pangu civilization sealed themselves because of extreme fear, and they probably had never even seen the real appearance of the Flood. But aren't you the same? Out of the fear for the unknown and the mistrust for other people, you have completely sealed yourself and only show up in public under the false masks such as 'Lead Eunuch Wang Xi', 'Dongfang Mingyue', or 'Serenity Seraph'. Only when you are with me, a monster who is almost as peculiar as yourself, can you slightly let loose of yourself and allow the real Long Yangjun to surface and catch her breath. Is such a life not tiresome and boring? Is it really fun?

"Now, I have already made up my mind to change myself and everything. Are you willing to follow me?

"Just follow up, Long Yangjun! Even Xiaoming and Wenwen, the creatures who were born not long ago and who are much weirder than you, are bold enough to admit their identities after the meticulous calculations. Are you not even as courageous as Xiaoming and Wenwen? You daren't face your real self when you are intrepid enough to fight Blackstar the Great for three hundred rounds?"

His questions that were like a torrent that had just broken the dam obviously collapsed Long Yangjun's confidence. Her eyes were rolling, and her breath was in disorder.

"You do not need to answer me right now. Just tell me the answer tomorrow."

Li Yao went on, "I'm going to find Boss Bai and the Fist King now and ask them to share the most secret intelligence openly too. Then, we will build up the super invincible gold team—the 'partnership of pyromaniacs'! Of course, I'll be the first one to talk, about Earth, about the dreams, and about myself!

"'Vulture Li Yao' couldn't have walked to this day without my trustworthy friends! I believe that my friends won't disappoint me this time. And you, you won't disappoint me, either!

"You must come to the meeting where we will establish the 'partnership of pyromaniacs' and honor our friendship tomorrow. You can speak last if you want. I'll be waiting for you!"

Li Yao ran faster and faster and disappeared before Long Yangjun could say no.

Long Yangjun was left stunned below the artificial moon for a long time. Kicking a pebble below her feet, she murmured, "… What an idiot!"

Chapter 2488: Forum of the Eccentrics

The next day in the morning, the artificial sun was shining, and the air was filled with a pleasant fragrance. When Boss Bai, the Fist King, and Long Yangjun gathered again for the meeting, they discovered that Li Yao had arrived earlier and that the meeting room had been redecorated into a whole different atmosphere.

Flowers and fruits were on the table. Xiaoming and Wenwen were standing at the door, wearing ribbons and greeting them with "welcome, welcome!" and with rigid faces. A red, eye-catching banner was also flying on the top of the meeting room, which said "The Forum of the Advanced Eccentrics of the Pangu Universe".

Boss Bai, the Fist King, and Long Yangjun looked at each other in bewilderment, lost for words. It was not until a long time later that Boss Bai finally spoke on behalf of them, "Li Yao, what the heck is this supposed to mean?"

"This is a theme that I've come up with for the meeting!"

Li Yao greeted them with an affable smile and explained, "The so-called 'Pangu universe' refers to the three thousand Sectors that are surrounded by the infinite black wall. 'Three thousand worlds' is a narrow, ambiguous phrase. Since the Cultivation civilization has three thousand Sectors, it is possible that the wizardly civilization is made of a lot of worlds too. 'Pangu universe', on the other hand, is much more accurate. I suggest we refer to our hometown with this phrase in the future."

"No, the Pangu universe is not a big deal, but what do you mean by 'eccentrics'?"

Boss Bai glared at him. "You are eccentric!"

"Compared to the… regular residents of the Pangu universe, I'm indeed somewhat eccentric."

Li Yao said casually, or rather, shamelessly. "Not only am I eccentric, but everybody here is slightly off the track too. This is the reason why we are gathered here and the focus of our meeting today!

"Come on, let's take a seat and cut to the chase. I talked to you guys in person last night. I believe everybody understands the importance of candor right now.

"First of all, no matter what advanced eccentrics we are, the strength of the individuals is always limited when faced with the overwhelming universe. Even if we bury our secrets in our hearts and somehow become a hundred times stronger, it will still be barely enough to resist the cold, dark universe and to realize our dreams on our own.

"I need the help of each and every one of you, and I believe that there will always be a time when you need my help.

"Besides, we are all smart guys with remarkable computational abilities. Even if a certain someone doesn't tell, can't other people guess it out? Here, hasn't Boss Bai guessed the Fist King's identity, and the Fist King guessed that we are from a certain country of Cultivators outside of the Imperium? Thankfully, the two of you were rational enough to not burst into real conflicts, but it is inevitable that you are slightly wary and concerned, isn't it? As time goes by, and when the situation changes, will the wariness and the concerns expand into a real chasm and lead to a real conflict?

"Boss Bai, Long Yangjun, the two of you escaped from the federation one after another. Your identities have been exposed. There is no reason to keep it a secret from the… poor eccentrics here when everybody in the federation knows it, right?

"I'm the same. I have grown from an unknown scavenger in the magical equipment graveyard into a strong, dominating, unparalleled superhero that is admired by everyone. It is only reasonable that you are skeptical about my unbelievable performance, right?"

"Enough."

Boss Bai said coldly, "Boss Bai does not need to hide anything from anybody. Don't think of me as such a cowardly and stealthy person!"

"I agree."

The Fist King also said calmly, "Regarding your proposal, I inferred hundreds of times last night. It is true that exposing my secrets will result in more risk, but taking the returns of the tighter cooperation, the risk will be worth it."

"That's great. Then, let's reintroduce ourselves!"

Li Yao picked up an apple and bit it hard, with the juice splashing everywhere. Looking at the hole on the incomplete apple, he finally made up his mind and said, "I, Vulture Li Yao, as you know, am from Floating Spear City of the Star Glory Federation. Fist King, this used to be the secrets of the few of us, but it is now your secret too. I hope that you can understand our trust for you.

"You must know most of my experiences. However, the secret that I have never told anyone except for my wife is—I've been having a weird dream since childhood, one about Earth and one where 'witchcraft' and 'ABCD' showed up. That dream is perhaps the source of my strength!"

Li Yao briefly described his dream about Earth and concluded, "I don't know what happened exactly. Was a certain 'earthling' teleported to the Star Glory Federation, possessing the body of a dying infant in the federation, or was it only certain… information waves from the Earth that traveled through time and space and got received by an infant in the federation?

"I'll have to ask your advice on the matter."

Everybody listened quietly, not saying anything at this moment but caught in deep thought.

It was not exactly because possession or information transmission was unusual. In a world of Cultivation, bizarre things such as possession, reincarnation, memory transplantation, and memory superimposition were happening on a daily basis. Even Boss Bai was sort of reborn through possession too.

Li Yao had grown into one of the few best experts at the edge of the Pangu universe after only a hundred years naturally because of many fortuitous factors.

However, the so-called "Earth", a center where multiple civilizations gathered, and the "Vulture Plan" to destroy it were too mysterious and thought-provoking.

When the Earth and the Flood were combined, even the Fist King's or Xiaoming and Wenwen's computational abilities were not enough to support the complicated deductions.

"Let's not complicate a simple question. Let's consider this… Earth is to be located at a dimension beyond the Pangu universe. A strong life in the dimension named 'Earthling Li Yao' dissected, compressed, and packed all the information that made up his life before sending it to our Pangu universe. Then, the information was received by your young brain."

Boss Bai pondered for a moment and said, "This high-dimension space named Earth, as a center of civilizations, is very likely to be an asylum where many civilizations hide from the Flood together. So, it will be perfectly reasonable that both Cultivation and witchcraft can be found there."

"No."

The Fist King said, "If Earth is truly an asylum to dodge the Flood, why must it be a 'high-dimensional space'? Why can't it be a 'low-dimensional space'?

"Imagine a two-dimensional life that does not have thickness. As long as it is vertical to our eyes, it will be invisible for us, and it will be in a perfect shelter.

"In other words, when a high-dimensional life observes a low-dimensional life, there will always be blind angles during the observation. Then, assuming that the survivors of the civilizations are determined to avoid the Flood, it will be safer to lower their dimensions and hide in the blind angles of the Flood, won't it?

"That is to say, with the three-dimensional universe we are at as the standard, Earth is very likely to be a world of a lower dimension—maybe a two-dimensional world. Evidence? The evidence is that the earthlings cannot Cultivate or use witchcraft. It is possible that their abilities are restricted when they lower their dimension. They can only reminisce about their abilities through fantasies."

"That still doesn't make sense."

Fascinated, Long Yangjun couldn't help but join the discussion. "If Earth is just a 'two-dimensional asylum', it still can't explain Li Yao's Vulture Plan. Why must he destroy Earth?

"It was mentioned in the weird dreams that countless civilizations were 'confined' to Earth in some sort of infinite loop, which was repeated generation after generation meaninglessly. What does that mean exactly?"

"Wait, just wait a moment."

Li Yao hurried to drag the topic back. "Until we find more intelligence and evidence, we won't reach anything on those impractical topics. Let's go back to our main theme just now. I'm already done. Who's up next?"

"Let me. I've got nothing to hide. Whoever finds me unacceptable is free to duel with me!"

With a sullen face, Boss Bai said, "My name is Bai Xingjian. I am… mixed with the souls of two people, one named Bai Xinghe and the other named Yan Xinjian. No, perhaps there are still rats. The souls of five hundred rats. This is my story…"

Boss Bai introduced his life.

How shameless Yan Xinjian was in the beginning, how painfully he had struggled, how he commanded the army of space pirates to crush the fleet of the Cultivators, and how he met his karma when he tried to advance into the Divinity Transformation Stage, ending his atrocious life under the curse of his wife. Eventually, he became five hundred rats, suffering the half-alive and half-dead torture.

Then, it was about how Bai Xinghe watched Xiao Xuance, "leader of all Cultivators", killed his parents, how he ended up on Spider Den, how he explored the dark, perilous underground fortresses under the coercion of the space pirates, how he rose step by step full of wrath and hatred, and how he turned around and died with the formidable enemy for his son Bai Kaixin.

Eventually, the two unpardonable souls encountered in the darkness below the ground. After years of fermenting and condensing, they finally broke out of the ground and found the body of a newly-dead boy named "Mouse Bai". Reincarnating inside the body, the new-generation Boss Bai began his journey!

"I am certainly not a good guy. By either the standard of the federation or that of the Imperium, I'm a ferocious and monstrous bandit. So, if anyone wants to seek revenge from me or cut my head to claim bounties, it will be perfectly understandable."

Finishing his life story calmly, Boss Bai narrowed his eyes and looked around before he said, "However, whoever wants to kill me must be prepared to be killed by me. Until my dream is realized, I will not die. Nobody and nothing can possibly stop me from marching to the higher dimensions!"

Chapter 2489: Who's the Master of the Universe?

Everybody fell silent after hearing what Boss Bai said.

Except for Li Yao, all of them were rather shocked.

Possession and reincarnation were not rare, but splitting the soul into five hundred pieces, storing them inside the bodies of mice, and luckily escaping after a hundred years of self-brutalization was too incredible!

Long Yangjun couldn't help but glance at Li Yao, finally understanding what he meant by "eccentrics".

"It's my turn next."

The Fist King said, "Lei Zonglie is not my real name. I do not have a name. I… am not a human being, but a machine."

In the calmest tone, the Fist King quietly depicted his struggle and survival, and how he studied on the Land of Sins in the past hundreds of years, including how he spent a long time inside a forsaken library of human beings and gradually awakened his first wisdom there.

He mentioned almost all the things that he never had the opportunity to in his entire life. In the end, he summarized, "I used to think that 'machine' was my entirety. Little did I expect that the wizardly chip that Boss Bai brought over would open up new possibilities for me. The more I studied the wizardly chip, the more I felt that something in my fundamental data was simmering with impatience and about to burst out, like grass sprouting again in the broken ice after a cold winter.

"In the past, I did not know what my real destiny was, or what I was going to do after I became the strongest in the universe, but now, I have come to the understanding that if my origins are indeed from the wizardly civilization, I'm bound to find the relics and the survivors of the wizardly civilization. Perhaps I will need to face the Flood or go to Earth, the mysterious world in Li Yao's dream, together with him."

"We're the same."

Before the Fist King finished, Xiaoming and Wenwen, who had similar life forms to him, shouted, "Our births were probably concerned with Earth too. So, we naturally need to find Earth, look for the origins of our lives, and perfect our life forms. What if all the earthlings look exactly like us? It would be great if we could find six billion partners!"

After everybody confessed their identity, they all focused their eyes on Long Yangjun.

Long Yangjun's countenance was erratic as she lowered her head, deep in thought for a long time. When she accidentally raised her head, what she saw, however, were the encouraging eyes of Xiaoming and Wenwen.

Long Yangjun was slightly dazed. The corners of her lips rose, as if she were laughing at her own timidity. Taking a deep breath, she said slowly, "My name is Long Yangjun. I am an… ancient person."

"We know that."

Boss Bai said, "Being from the Ancient Sages Sector that is covered in the dark nebula, you naturally count as an 'ancient person', which is too rare among us so many 'eccentrics'. Why are you so cautious, Fellow Cultivator Long?"

"No."

Long Yangjun said expressionlessly, "When I said that I was an 'ancient person', I did not mean that I was from the Ancient Sages Sector, but that I was from an earlier time, the primeval age hundreds of thousands of years ago."

Under the eyes of the stunned Boss Bai and the sparking Fist King, Long Yangjun told her story unhurriedly.

Of course, it also included her speculations on the primeval war, the threat of the Flood, and everything else.

Although the experts at the meeting were capable of communicating in a way dozens of times faster than language, it still took Long Yangjun a good ten minutes before she finally explained her past, her memories, and her deductions.

Her words were like the fiercest space storm that had blown Boss Bai and the Fist King into dizziness, who were not back to themselves until a long time later.

By the time they were finally back to themselves, Boss Bai stared at Long Yangjun for one minute and then at Li Yao for another, before he said with a bitter smile, "Vulture Li Yao, you are truly incredible. It truly baffles me why so many… unbelievable monsters have been gathered around you!"

Li Yao grinned and opened his hands, making the gesture of hugging everyone. He said lightheartedly, "Look, don't you feel much more comfortable after telling the secrets in your heart, like a heavy burden that you've been carrying for a thousand years has been put down? Furthermore, the sky did not fall, and nothing went wrong. We now do not need to be wary of each other or pretend to not know each other's secrets when we do know. Such awkward situations can be saved, right?"

Boss Bai scratched his stubble. His eyes were small, but the brilliance of those of a tiger was beaming out. Glancing around, he said slowly, "The 'eccentrics' of different backgrounds like us perhaps do not count as 'normal human beings' in the narrow sense. However, from a different perspective, we are all searching for the ultimate truth through hard work and trying to shape ourselves through the truth. In that case, we are all 'Cultivators' in the broad sense, aren't we?

"Fellow Cultivators, greetings, it's nice to meet you, and it's great to fight side by side with you on the path of Cultivation!"

Boss Bao's summary was exactly what everybody was thinking.

Li Yao, Long Yangjun, the Fist King, Xiaoming, and Wenwen said at the same time, "Fellow Cultivators, greetings."

Everybody laughed.

"Then—"

Dominating vibe surrounded Boss Bai, as if he were a raging nebula himself. "Now that the fellow Cultivators here have all confessed our secrets to each other, let's focus on the more concrete subjects! It has occurred to me that the ultimate goals of all the fellow Cultivators here—searching for Earth, searching for the wizardly world, or searching for the truth of the primeval war—are deeply-associated and indispensable. Only by helping each other can we find the truth and our true selves.

"That is the basis of our teamwork, but we are also faced with a common problem, which is one of life and death that the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan were likely to have been faced with in the past. The problem is, should we march out of the 'Pangu universe' or not?

"If we march out, it's possible that we will be discovered by the Flood. By then, not only will we be killed ourselves, but the Pangu universe will also be destroyed. If we do not march out, we will be suffocated in this place, repeating the tragedy of the Pangu civilization's destruction in the past.

"This is a dilemma. Each option has its own reasons but also extremely high risks. Allow me to give my answer first. We should march out without considering anything!

"Life is itself a gamble with extremely low odds. When the fish march to the seashore in a wild gamble, were they ever scared of the dangers on the continent? The glory of any civilization depends on a hundred percent bravery while fighting for the one percent luck. In the past, the Pangu civilization chose to stay back, and we have seen what happened to them. Should we still copy what they did? Of course not! We should march out and take our chances! Every civilization dies. All that matters is whether the death is splendid or soundless!"

Boss Bai declared passionately.

"I agree with Boss Bai. There are certainly risks. The crucial factors are the probability of risks and the scale of returns."

The Fist King said calmly, "According to Fellow Cultivator Long's speculation, the leadership of the Pangu civilization intended to hide in the Pangu universe for hundreds of thousands of years and restore their feelings, desires, and self-will after the Flood ebbed. It is a very reasonable guess.

"Then, isn't the contemporary age we are at already 'hundreds of thousands of years later'? At this point, it is highly likely that the Flood has ebbed, and the returns to explore the extraterrestrial lands are highest. There can't be a better opportunity than here and now."

Thinking for a moment, Li Yao asked, "It's true that the Flood has probably ebbed, but what do you mean by saying that the returns are highest now? Can you explain?"

"I'm talking about the remains and the living space."

The Fist King said, "In nature, the destruction brought by any flood entails the hope of new life. When the floods rage on the land, they will leave fertile mud while they destroy forests, they will bring the life seeds from elsewhere, which will give birth to new creatures while they swallow the old ferocious beasts. I imagine that the Flood in the universe is the same, right?

"After the Flood swallows the civilizations, countless relics will be left behind for the exploration of the survivors. Once we discover the relics of the wizardly civilization, there will be hope for us to master all the technologies and wisdom of the wizardly civilization or even to find new lives to join us. We will establish a brand-new alliance of carbon-based lives—no, an alliance of multiple-form lives, exactly like the Pangu's alliance in the past.

"After every relic of civilization is found, the grand alliance will be stronger. In the meantime, there can't be too many hostile, malicious civilizations in the universe right now. They have all been swallowed by the Flood, and it is barely possible for a new-generation powerful civilization to be born after only less than a million years.

"In other words, the civilization of human beings in the Pangu universe is likely to be the greatest beneficiary of the previous outbreak of Flood. As long as we have the courage to march out despite the risks, it is possible that we will harvest the entire universe!"

"Well said!"

Boss Bai couldn't help but slap the table. "Outside, it's the infinite treasures and living space that have been left behind after the Flood ebbed. It's a wasteland where everything in the past has been destroyed. Whoever has the courage to explore and conquer it will be the new master of the grand universe. Or rather, by condensing the strength of thousands of civilizations, it will be possible for us to fight the Flood once again someday in the future!

"Fist King, although we are not the same species, I have to say that I truly like you!"

Xiaoming and Wenwen had similar thinking patterns as the Fist King's. After hearing what the Fist King said, the two of them immediately began to calculate. A moment later, they nodded their heads at the same time. "Master Fist King is quite right. If we ever have the chance to march out of the Pangu universe in the thousand years to come, it will truly carry the least risks and the highest returns."

"I have no objection."

Long Yangjun closed her eyes and said, "The tragedy of the Pangu civilization's destruction still flashes in my head. There is no need to walk on the wrong path twice."

Having reached a consensus, everybody looked at Li Yao.

Pondering for a moment, Li Yao smiled and said, "I don't think we need to worry too much about the problem, because somebody has marched out of the Pangu universe ahead of us not a long time ago!"

Chapter 2490: Let's Complete a Minor Goal First...

Li Yao was talking about Wei Qingqing.

The little green bird that seemed weak but was in fact proud and stubborn had been launched out through a special starship with part of the spectral Cultivators, or "Spiriters", in the "Tinder Plan".

The way they explored the universe was similar to that of the "Blood Stripe Virus". Their speed might not be high, and the technologies applied might not be too advanced, but the attrition and consumption on themselves were the lowest. They were likely to breach the black wall in the end!

Although Wei Qingqing's starship was only powered by spiritual energy and was pushing forward through consecutive space jumps regularly, there was still a good chance that they could surpass the black wall with enough time.

Should Wei Qingqing and the other Spiriters unfortunately encountered the Flood after they crossed the black wall, the odds would be quite high that the Flood would figure out the coordinates of the Pangu universe and the existence of the civilization of mankind through them.

Then, it would not matter whether or not Li Yao and the rest of them marched out.

Or rather, it was not important whether or not the Tinder Plan led by Wei Qingqing was reckless. Human beings were a species full of curiosity and desire for explorations after all. Since the civilization of mankind had picked up the tenebrum energy and the artificial intelligence after a hundred thousand years of development, it wouldn't be long before they grasped the abilities to march to the extraterrestrial lands.

The efforts of Li Yao and the rest of them were merely accelerating the arrival of the event.

Once the abilities to march to the extraterrestrial lands were grasped, with the natural instincts of human beings, there would certainly be ambitious, audacious guys who would march out without caring about anything. Even if there were not Wei Qingqing, Li Yao, or Boss Bai, other people would still embark on the quest!

It was impossible to prevent human beings from marching out of the Pangu universe, unless the curiosity, interest, feelings, and free will of all the individuals were erased like what the Pangu civilization did in the past.

The lowest requirement was to establish a country where every individual was closely monitored and all the civil, spontaneous exploration of the universe was banned so that the roads to space could be completely blocked.

Was it possible?

"Look, as a matter of fact, we only have one choice, which is to push forward bravely, to explore, to conquer, and to contact all the civilizations that are threatened, destroyed, and swallowed by the Flood!"

Li Yao concluded the discussion just now and then changed the topic. "On the other hand, marching to the extraterrestrial lands, exploring the Flood, and searching for Earth—those are our distant, ultimate goals. Before we realize our ultimate goals, there is still an easy, short-term minor goal that we need to accomplish, which is to settle the Imperium and Blackstar the Great.

"To be more precise, we have to modify the Imperium of True Human Beings into what we would like it to be and to introduce the Star Glory Federation to the center of the cosmos. In the meantime, we have to resist the attack of the Alliance of the Holy Covenant, thereby achieving a steady, triangular balance of the Imperium, the federation, and the Covenant Alliance.

"This is exactly where my belief lies. While the vast universe is splendid enough, I am a human being after all. If my human patriots in the Pangu universe live in misery every second, will the vast, splendid, and mysterious universe outside be related to them at all? Wouldn't it be too hypocritical and hilarious if I were to watch the ordinary people be brutally exploited by the Immortal Cultivators and do nothing while I preach about how to prevent the civilization of mankind from being destroyed by the Flood?

"Boss Bai, stop glaring just yet. Even if you do not care if the ordinary people are exploited by the Immortal Cultivators, you will need a powerful fleet in order to march to the extraterrestrial lands. Where exactly are you going to find such a fleet without a steady rear?

"You are not really thinking that it is possible to establish a fleet capable of crossing the black wall and reaching the extraterrestrial lands through the clandestine and stealthy methods, are you?"

Lost for words, Boss Bai was deep in thought.

The Fist King said in a dull voice, "Li Yao does have a point. Now that I think of it, the starships have to be navigated by some sort of super artificial intelligence in order to cross the black wall and march to the extraterrestrial lands. Also, they need to be upgraded into a certain new structure to be powered by the tenebrum energy. It is a massive project that requires breakthroughs in thousands of technological frontiers before it can be implemented. The fleet is certainly not something that we can build by robbing a few random fleets.

"Resources. We need astronomical resources, which can only be provided by a powerful and steady country from the back.

"I don't know how powerful your Star Glory Federation is, but since it lies at the border of the Pangu universe, it can't be enough to support the construction of such an 'extraterrestrial expedition army' in terms of scale. Only a country the size of the Imperium or the Covenant Alliance can facilitate the birth of such a fleet.

"Naturally, it's impossible for the Covenant Alliance to work with us, which makes the Imperium of True Human Beings the only option."

"Apart from resources, there's also intelligence."

Long Yangjun said coldly, "If the Pangu civilization was truly and indirectly destroyed by the Flood, tremendous intelligence regarding the Flood must've been left behind. Who knows whether or not such intelligence was passed on to the hands of the human experts during the rise of mankind?

"Is it possible that the conflicts among human experts were actually about certain secrets beyond the Pangu universe, like the Flood?

"For example, the Alliance of the Holy Covenant.

"What is the significance of the Covenant Alliance? Is it really so simple as to erase everybody's feelings and self-will so that they will be the Pangu Clan's obedient tools? If it is really as simple as that, how could the people of the Covenant Alliance never doubt or resist in the past thousand years?

"Is it possible that the Covenant Alliance has actually learned something about the Flood and that they are merely carrying on the last will of the Pangu civilization by continuing to seal themselves, in order to… protect the Pangu universe in such an extreme way?

"Also, there's the rebirth of Blackstar the Great and the secret he is digging below the capital in the Empyreal Terminus Sector. What is it exactly? Why does he think that he will be able to defeat the four Kurfürst families and even the Covenant Alliance as soon as the item is found?

"Furthermore, was the war between the Supreme Emperor, the strongest human being, and the Blood God, his strongest clone, ten thousand years ago really a simple struggle for power? Of course I do not have any evidence, but wouldn't it have been too lowly for people as strong as the Supreme Emperor or the Blood God to fight only for power?

"It must've been a competition of beliefs between the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God. Then, what exactly were the 'beliefs' that they had different opinions on? What complicated contradictions could've possibly turned the Supreme Emperor and his clone against each other?

"Lastly, and perhaps most importantly, what is the 'Book of Revelation' that the Pangu civilization left, and for what reason was it left? The historians in the future generations all believed that it was a seed of their civilization when the Pangu civilization realized their inevitable failure at the last years of the primeval war, but I have a different theory. Was it possible that the Pangu civilization compiled the eight volumes of the Book of Revelation when they realized the threat of the Flood as a preparation to revive themselves after the Flood ebbed?

"If that is the case, the Book of Revelation will be very important for our exploration in the extraterrestrial lands and our understanding of the Flood. Gathering all eight volumes may be too difficult, but it will be safer to launch the expedition after we find at least one or two volumes of it, won't it?"

Long Yangjun's words were reasonable and convincing.

They were also endorsed by the pieces of her memories and were, therefore, closer to the fact than the groundless speculations of the historians.

What exactly was Blackstar the Great up to? What kind of secrets were buried in the mausoleum of the Supreme Emperor? Where was the mysterious Book of Revelation? Was it possible that the ways to cross the black wall and explore the extraterrestrial lands had been engraved on it?

"It seems that—"

Boss Bai chuckled and said, "In order to realize our ultimate gold, we will have to accomplish the minor goal of 'blowing up Blackstar the Great' first."

"Exactly."

Li Yao nodded his head. "Wuying Qi is not blind. It's not like he will watch us establish an enormous expedition fleet without doing anything. We cannot begin the explorations of the next stop without fixing the Imperium and the Covenant Alliance."

"Alright. Then, let's use Wuying Qi's head as a lantern to illuminate the journey to the extraterrestrial lands!'

Boss Bai waved his hands and said, "We were planning to participate in the battle of Seven Seas after all and work on another major heist! As long as we benefit from the battle of Seven Seas and expand a couple of times, we will be one of the major forces at the edge of the Imperium. Who will be able to do anything about us?"

"Exactly. The battle of Seven Seas will be the first major space campaign for the reformists and the four families. Neither party can afford failure. So, there will be a great chance for us to take advantage of the chaos."

With his eyes glowing, Li Yao put on a cunning smile. "However, taking advantage of the chaos is not enough. Since so many top-tier eccentrics of the Pangu universe have been gathered here, I think that we can work on something greater!"

Boss Bai narrowed his eyes. "Li Yao, I knew that you would be up to no good. Tell me about it. What do you want?"

Pondering for a moment, Li Yao said, "I want to make the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors side with us!"

"Have you lost your mind?"

Boss Bai remarked, "The Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors is the greatest endorser behind the reformists and has been supporting them vehemently for decades! An important reason for the reformists' unstoppable rise is the incessant investment from the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors!

"Right now, the reformists are about to succeed, and the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors is going to reap the fruits of victory. Do you think that they will be out of their mind and begin to support you and me instead of the reformists?"

"Just be patient and hear me out."

Crossing his fingers and covering his nose, Li Yao put on a mysterious smile and said calmly, "Of course, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors will continue supporting the reformists without caring about the cost. The two parties are indispensable in the first place.

"But is supporting the reformists the same as supporting Blackstar the Great?

"I hope my fellow Cultivators can reflect on the difference and answer one question for me. Why hasn't Wuying Qi revealed his identity as 'Blackstar the Great' yet? What are his concerns… or fears?"

Chapter 2491: Us, and Us Only!

Li Yao's words cast the experts at the meeting into deep thoughts. Those experts were all monsters with extraordinary computational and deduction abilities. Now that Li Yao had pointed it out, they immediately realized that it was weird.

"That's right. Blackstar the Great does not have any reason to keep such a low profile."

Long Yangjun, who already had a head-on clash with Wuying Qi, was the first to realize the problem. "He might've been laying low during the 'dormancy period' when he was still relatively weak because he feared that the four Kurfürst families would join their hands to suppress or kill him should his identity be exposed. But right now, the reformists have ended on bad terms with the four families. The two parties have declared war on each other. Even the Empyreal Terminus Sector has been taken down by the reformists and the 'Reform Committee of the Council of Elders' has been established. He has the Imperial Guards to defend him and countless experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage at his disposal. What is he still scared of?

"There are a lot of benefits to reveal his identity. In the Imperium of True Human Beings where the greatest virtue is strength, 'Blackstar the Great' is not an identity to be ashamed of. Many people can accept the grandeur return of an expert who has slept for a thousand years. They are even looking forward to such an expert, who will stand out, consolidate the loosened Imperium, and lead them to reclaim their former glory!

"Just look at 'Emperor Shenwu' who was not half as capable as he was described. He was already praised to be 'a second Blackstar the Great' when he only won an expedition temporarily. Right now, we are talking about the reappearance of the authentic founding emperor of the country and the ancestor of all Immortal Cultivators. In terms of legitimacy, his position is stronger than anybody else's.

"I imagine that the morale of his supporters will be boosted to the summit if Wuying Qi reveals his identity, and the morale of the four families will hit the rock bottom. Not only will the Immortal Cultivators of other surnames in their territories waver, but even the lower-level Immortal Cultivators within the four families may be shocked and join Blackstar the Great instead, right?

"It is needless to mention the bottom-level people of the Imperium of True Human Beings, who have mostly grown up listening to the legends of the wise and strong Blackstar the Great. If they learn that their emperor is back to life, how can they not worship and follow him with their lives?

"On the other hand, even if he does not reveal his identity, Queen Li Linghai is still a thorn in the flesh for the four Kurfürst families, and the four families will still try everything possible to kill Li Linghai. It is absolutely impossible for them to reach an agreement.

"From every perspective, Blackstar the Great should reveal his real identity. At the very least, he has absolutely no concerns on the four Kurfürst families' side."

"You're quite right. So, Wuying Qi has been keeping his identity a secret not because he is concerned about his enemy—the four Kurfürst families—but because he is concerned about, if not scared of, his temporary ally—the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors!"

Like a blade that was slowly unsheathed, Li Yao's eyes grew sharp, and his every word was as precise as a scalpel cutting a nidus. "What is the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors? It is a coalition of the new capitals at the edge of the Imperium, or in fact, the whole Imperium. It represents the strength of the billions of medium and small business groups nationwide. What are the appeals of the new capitals and the small businesses? What are the instincts of their survival and development?

"First of all, a unified and open market. The customs and the redundant tariffs in different Sectors should be canceled so that capitals can flow unrestrictedly like blood, instead of only being controlled by the noble families who know nothing except for exploitation and entertainment.

"Second, a social and business environment that is more fair, just, and open.

"Capitals inherently demand fairness and oppose regality. Everybody is equal in front of the hideous money, which is the most basic business logic. If the emperor's one dollar can be used as three dollars, a noble's one dollar can be used as two dollars, and a civilian's one dollar can be used as only half a dollar, no business can ever thrive.

"Thirdly, and most importantly, a healthy, vigorous, and expanding market. Specifically, the new capitals call for better privileges, more spare time, and more rights and freedom for the ordinary people, or the so-called hominoids, in the Imperium. Only in such a way will the hominoids be capable of and interested in consuming the goods presented by the capitals and helping them grow.

"Capitals are, of course, cruel and heartless. It will certainly not devote itself to the liberation of hominoids for no good reason. However, if most hominoids are slaves and puppets, who are not interested in any consumption other than on 'energy pills' or 'slaughter streams', the new capitals will be starved.

"So, allow me to reaffirm my opinion that I came up with after I arrived in the Imperium. The new commercial capitals and their representative, 'Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors', are the most advanced, open-minded forces in the Imperium of True Human Beings that crave for changes the most. So, they are most likely to cooperate with us."

Boss Bai observed, "But the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors is hostile to the Cultivators, and they have already chosen the reformists."

"All the Immortal Cultivators are hostile to the Cultivators. All we can do is to find the least hostile community in them."

Li Yao said, "Also, as I said just now, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors chose the reformists and Queen Li Linghai, not Blackstar the Great.

"While the slogan of the reformists on the surface is 'Honor the Majesty, Settle the Rebels, Renew the Imperium', implying that they want to build a powerful and unified government, their declaration 'Honor the Majesty' is pure bulls*it. Neither the reformists nor their patron, the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, wants an autocratic emperor. Once their reform succeeds, it is very likely that they will enforce a constitutional monarchy, where the emperor is regarded as a lofty, honorable symbol while the real power is concentrated on the 'Reform Committee of the Council of Elders'. It is needless to say that there will be tremendous spokespersons of business groups in the committee to ensure that the future of the Imperium lies in the hands of capitals.

"Why did they choose Li Linghai as the leader of the reformists? Part of the reason was that Li Linghai had a strong army, but more importantly, it was because they knew that Li Linghai was from the Li family and had been the queen of the Imperium for decades. Her identity meant that she couldn't become a dominating emperor or a tyrant. She could only be an obedient spokesperson of capitals!

"That was the foundation of the cooperation of the reformists, or Li Linghai, and the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors.

"But what we are talking about right now is not Li Linghai but Wuying Qi. Is it possible that such a tyrant, despot, and dictator such as Blackstar the Great will be an obedient spokesperson of capitals?

"Impossible, it's absolutely impossible! From what Wuying Qi said during our battle against him, it seems that he has grasped a certain secret technique that guarantees eternal life. Well, an eternal life sounds too unbelievable, but judging from his tone, it wouldn't be a problem for him to live another two to three thousand years.

"It's already bad enough for someone like Wuying Qi to live two to three hundred years, not to mention that he will now live ten times longer! He will certainly focus all power and capitals in his hands, closely monitor all the experts and civilians, and become the most powerful tyrant!

"Under the governance of Blackstar the Great, even the nobles will have no living space at all and can only shudder in fear below his feet, let alone the small businesses and the free capitals? I have never heard of any tyrant that can live in peace with free capitals!

"Yes, the shortsighted owners of the free capitals, by their contributions to the rising of Wuying Qi, can change into aristocratic capitals. But it must be noted that the source of the so-called aristocratic capitals is still in the hands of Blackstar the Great. What does a founding emperor like to do most? Killing the potential threats, of course! Didn't Blackstar the Great kill many contributors to his rise when he came to power years ago? As long as the leaders of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors have the slightest brainwaves that are not corrupted by the short-term benefits, they will not believe him!

"In conclusion, my opinion is that most of the leaders of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors must've been deceived. They are under the illusion that the person whom they have pushed to the frontstage is the 'relatively harmless' Queen Li Linghai. They are confident in controlling Li Linghai or at least in negotiating with her in the Reform Committee of the Council of Elders in the future to share the huge cake that is the Imperium.

"However, never have they dreamed that the person they have pushed forward is a tyrant a hundred times more dreadful than the four Kurfürsten. Besides, the tyrant has both the strength and the legitimacy. Once he is in power, he will not share the cake with anybody else but will simply crumble all the oppositions into the frostings on the cake before he swallows it!

"Right now, the competition between the reformists and the four families is still intense, and Blackstar the Great still needs the strength of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors. Naturally, he would not expose his identity at such a moment! He would wait at least until the backs of the four families are completely broken and the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors cannot jump out of his control. He would only drop his disguise and claim the Imperium again at that time!"

"It does… sound reasonable."

Pondering for a long time, Boss Bai said, "Li Yao, it seems that you have truly preserved your intelligence in daily life and used it in critical moments like right now. So to speak, there is truly an opportunity for us to cooperate with the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors?"

"Not just the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, but also most of the real reformists."

His eyes glittering, Li Yao said carefully, "For the people who truly want to 'renew the Imperium', instead of only fighting for power, the rebirth of Blackstar the Great will be a huge setback, and they will not allow such a thing to happen. Those people are the cooperators that we need to appeal to.

"To put it more simple, Blackstar the Great is a worm lurking within the reformists, intending to steal the fruit of victory after the reformists and the four families have both suffered brutal losses. It is our mission to stop him from realizing that!

"The idea that the Imperium needs renewal is right, but Wuying Qi and Li Linghai in the Imperium are both false reformists, who are taking advantage of the real reformists and will strike them after they succeed! The cause of the reformists is now in perils. Right now, only we can raise the banner and call for a real reform!"

Chapter 2492: The Birth of a Super Alliance!

"Wow!"

Hardly had Li Yao finished when Xiaoming and Wenwen clapped their hands hard. With his eyes full of admiration, Xiaoming shouted, "Why can dad be so sordid when he is sordid and so awesome when he is awesome?"

"Is what Boss Bai said true, and this is some sort of special way to distribute thinking abilities?"

Wenwen speculated, "He reveals only one percent of his wisdom to handle the daily work and communication, but the other ninety-nine percent of wisdom is not hibernating but running in silence, continuing the complicated and long-term deductions. Then, when the critical moments come, he will be able to present the results!"

"This is truly a remarkable ability that both ensures maximal computational ability and utilizes all the spare time of the brain in a reasonable way. Also, the energy consumption is greatly lowered, without any concerns of getting mentally deranged."

Xiaoming was amazed. "For a pure crystal processor, it is absolutely impossible to switch between the two computational states in such a seamless way. Only a human brain is capable of that. Is this where the human brain is more advanced than the crystal processor?"

The two kids looked at each other and declared at the same time, "It seems we need to learn from Dad and pick up such a fantastic way of thinking!"

"Is that really so complicated?"

Slightly frowning, Li Yao said casually, "This is just the most insignificant reflection of my wisdom."

Boss Bai covered his forehead. "… Enough, Li Yao, it's really enough. Let's just talk about the specific moves."

"Specifically, first of all, an elite rapid response fleet with a high combat ability is certainly indispensable."

Li Yao said solemnly, "The Blue Sky Space Zone is now ours, and Li Wuji, Marquess Yongchun, is in our hands too. According to the intelligence, there should still be one to two months until the battle of the Seven Seas Grand Market. During the time, the whole attention of the reformists and the four families will be focused on enlistment and mobilization. As long as Li Wuji delivers appropriate information, I don't think anybody will disturb us while we maintain, upgrade, and train the fleet here.

"I don't know the first thing about the construction of a fleet, but Boss Bai is undoubtedly the connoisseur here. Naturally, you should be fully responsible for it. The key is to let the Fist King, Xiaoming, and Wenwen completely melt into the fleet, in order to build a super fleet that is fastest and has the best cyberwarfare abilities even in the entire center of the cosmos. Eventually, the fleets stronger than us will not be as fast as us, those faster than us will not be as good at camouflages and deceptions as us, and the professional cyberwar troops that are better at camouflages and deceptions than us will not have as many super experts and Colossi for a head-on clash as we do. In that case, our fleet will never fail.

"With the super fleet as our shield, we will be able to initiate communication with the Star Glory Federation.

"It's been a while since I jumped to the Imperium. The federation must be eager to learn the updates on the center of the cosmos. Before, I was either closely monitored by Li Linghai or running desperately. I never had the conditions to build a massive, super-remote base of communication, but right now, the conditions have been met.

"Although the federation, lying at the border of the Pangu universe, is not nearly as strong as the Imperium or the Covenant Alliance, our national power can certainly amount to one of the four families, can't it? When the life-and-death game comes to the most critical moment, the reinforcements from the federation might play a vital role, right?

"The last thing is what I said just now. I'm thinking of finding Jin Yuyan, the director of the connoisseur, in the Seven Seas Grand Market and talk him into joining our side!"

Boss Bai, the Fist King, Long Yangjun, Xiaoming, and Wenwen were all deep in thought.

Knocking the table slowly, Boss Bai said, "There's no reason why Blackstar the Great would ignore the problems that you have thought of. If I were Wuying Qi, I would certainly have infiltrated the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors one way or another. Are you sure that Jin Yuyan is not Wuying Qi's guy, and he is unaware of the truth?"

"It goes without saying that Wuying Qi has tried to infiltrate the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors and partly succeeded, shifting the loyalty of some members of the alliance from 'capitals' to 'His Majesty'."

Li Yao said, "After all, capitals are like water. When they grow fierce and turn into a torrent, they can be most ferocious and destroy everything on their way.

"But when they are weak, they will be the weakest things in the world, even weaker than tofu. For the pettiest benefits, they will not hesitate to sell their parents.

"However, even if most members of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors may sell their organization and join Blackstar the Great, how could Jin Yuyan, as the director of the alliance, betray the foundation of his cause? Even if he does side with Wuying Qi, he will still be a major threat to Wuying Qi and will get removed sooner or later.

"Since he has become the leader of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, his smartness is unquestionable. I don't believe that he will be so greedy and unwise. So, my judgment is that Jin Yuyan is still deceived, and he does not know the truth inside Li Linghai's body. This guy is negotiable."

Boss Bai said coldly, "If your guess is correct, I think that Jin Yuyan is likely to be in some sort of danger because Blackstar the Great certainly would not like such a 'smart guy' controlling the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors."

"That's right."

Li Yao said, "That's all the more reason why I need to go to the Seven Seas Grand Market. After all, a great battle is coming, in which anything can happen. It will be rather troublesome if Jin Yuyan is killed by unknown assassins.

"All in all, this is our plan. Boss Bai will supervise the construction of the fleet, I will establish the super-remote communication base, and after the communication with the federation, Long Yangjun and I will sneak into the Seven Seas Grand Market to persuade Jin Yuyan. We will try to earn the most benefits from the battle and accomplish the minor goal of killing Wuying Qi first before we pursue our ultimate dream of breaking out of the Pangu universe. Any questions?"

Boss Bai: "No."

The Fist King: "No."

Xiaoming and Wenwen: "No."

Long Yangjun: "Yes, some of my primeval memories have just been awakened. It has occurred to me that I'm rather talented in fleet commanding. So, I'm thinking to stay in the space battlefield. The Dominator of Ventilation Tubes alone is enough for the sneaking job. Keep it up! I'm rooting for you!"

"Huh?"

Li Yao blinked quickly and turned to Xiaoming and Wenwen. "Hey kids, why don't you come with your father on this mission?"

"About that…"

Xiaoming and Wenwen looked at each other and hesitated. "Yesterday, we talked about it with Master Fist King and realized that there were a lot of things that we needed to learn from Master Fist King. Also, if the battle network of a super fleet is to be established, it seems that our database will have to open more accesses and interfaces. All-All in all, there are a lot of technical details and problems that need addressing. You wouldn't know what they meant even if we tell you, Dad. So, we really don't have the time."

"That's right."

The Fist King said calmly, "My database is more condensed and professional, storing solely the information about battles, but Xiaoming and Wenwen's database includes a lot more information about the civilization of mankind. Our communication will significantly increase our logic abilities and mental activities. So, I have improved the issue to be the top priority. So, I cannot go with you, Li Yao. But don't misunderstand, I truly want to go with you. Trust me, I'm a machine. Well, a machine doesn't lie."

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao looked at Boss Bai.

Boss Bai had already activated more than ten light beams. He was so focused on the construction of the fleet that he did not notice Li Yao's scorching eyes at all.

The meeting room fell silent in a subtle, awkward atmosphere.

Li Yao looked at everyone, and everyone looked at their fingers.

"… Fine."

Narrowing his eyes, Li Yao gnashed his teeth and said, "Let's discuss the specific arrangements later. All in all, the first meeting of our 'partnership of pyromaniacs' is very successful—"

"Wait, what?"

Boss Bai was suddenly refreshed, and his head craned out of a light beam. "What the heck is the 'partnership of pyromaniacs'? Was this one of the motions? Why didn't I know anything?"

"It's… the name of our team!"

Li Yao said matter-of-factly, "So many sophisticated eccentrics of the Pangu universe have been gathered for the future of mankind. This is such a thrilling and significant issue, and such a luxury and top combination. Naturally, it needs a great name!"

Boss Bai bashed the table and glared. "The most important thing is why this combination is named as 'partnership of pyromaniacs'? Can't it be called 'coalition of kleptomaniacs'?"

"Yes, of course it can, but the name 'partnership of pyromaniacs' is more meaningful and awe-inspiring, because our goal is to bring light and warmth to all the civilizations in the whole universe!"

Li Yao scratched his head hard. Seeing that Boss Bai was apparently not in a good mood, he said in a hurry, "Don't be upset, Boss Bai. How about this, let's both take a step back and meet halfway. The name of the team is still 'partnership of pyromaniacs', but we can use your battle robe as the suit of our team! When the unparalleled experts of 'partnership of pyromaniacs' show up, you, I, the Fist King, Long Yangjun, Xiaoming, Wenwen, and even Li Jialing, Han Te, Liu Li, and the rest of them will all be wearing colorful shirts, beach pants, and blue-and-white flip-flops. Wouldn't that be glamorous and astounding?"

Boss Bai: "…"

Xiaoming: "So, has Dad entered the low operation speed mode again, preserving all his intelligence?"

The Fist King: "I think so. Hehe, those idiotic human beings. Can 'partnership of pyromaniacs' or 'coalition of kleptomaniacs' even count as a name? In my opinion, it should be called the 'invincible alliance of iron dominators'!"

"Huh?"

Wenwen was astounded. "M-Master Fist King, are you serious?"

"Of course."

The Fist King said, "I'm a machine. A machine never jokes. I'm always serious. Is there a problem?"

"… I do have a minor question."

Xiaoming couldn't help but ask, "Master Fist King, how do you feel about the name 'Super Awesome Heavenly Crystal Second Generation'?"

"It's not half bad."

The Fist King analyzed carefully for a moment and said, "It's simple yet intimidating, the suffix 'second generation' points out its model and version, leaving room for future upgrades. The following models can be named as the third generation, fourth generation, fifth generation, and so on without causing any confusion. It's a rarely-seen good name. What's wrong?"

Xiaoming and Wenwen said at the same time, "N-Nothing. Just forget it."

Chapter 2493: Super Cyberwar Cruiser!

While a friendly and joyful discussion was going on inside the meeting room, allowing the participants of the meeting to gain a profound understanding about each other's stance, two fledging, vigorous young men—perhaps the new generation of the partnership of pyromaniacs—were confronting each other coldly.

They were exactly Han Te and Li Jialing.

Appointed as guards of the meeting, they stayed at the door to prevent anybody else from barging in.

However, the two young men were like magnets on the same pole, finding each other utterly intolerable even though it was the first time they met.

Han Te leaned against the wall like a hippie, his lips pouted, while he squinted at the golden hair on Li Jialing's body with disdain all over his face. He was also mumbling something nonstop.

Li Jialing, on the other hand, was holding his arms and almost half asleep, his head lowered, not even looking back at Han Te.

His arrogance completely irritated the disobedient young man from the Land of Sins. Han Te walked around Li Jialing for a few laps and provoked him, "Hey, golden-haired boy, has anybody ever told you that you look very arrogant?"

"Then, has anybody ever told you that—"

Li Jialing replied casually, "—you look very unmannered?"

"Hehe, the worms that gnawed our way out of the Land of Sins like us were all raised by nature. Of course, we are unmannered!"

Han Te gnashed his teeth. "We are certainly not half as unmannered as the sons of the great houses who always enjoy an extravagant life!"

"Extravagant life? Great houses?"

Li Jialing was amused. He shook his head and said unconcernedly, "I know you, Han Te. You are very famous in the Big Bai Pirate Gang—the stud from Stainless Mouse! However, a piece of advice: if you suffer cerebral ischemia for a long time, your brain tissues tend to necrotize, and your IQ will be lowered, although I don't think you have much to lose right now."

It took Han Te a long time before he finally realized what was going on. He immediately burst into a fury with his eyes bloodshot, like a bull whose eyes were covered by a piece of red cloth. He demanded angrily, "Li boy, I know you too. You're from 'Manjusaka, the City in the Sky', aren't you? You once received the most rigorous training there, and the 'materials' for your training were all captured from the Land of Sins, weren't they? Every muscle and every bone on your body is saturated with the blood of the people of the Land of Sins. One day, one day—"

"'One day' is unnecessary—"

Golden flames seemed to be surging in the middle of Li Jialing's golden hair. The last thing he would like to be reminded of was the miserable life when he was in "Manjusaka, the City in the Sky". He couldn't help but burst into a real fury too. "Let's just make it here and today. Although I do not believe I'm the one responsible for the miseries of the Land of Sins, if you have nothing better to do than to vent your fury on me, just bring it on! I'm right here!"

Crack! Crack! Crack! Crack!

Han Te's arms that were made of iron unleashed purple electric arcs too, with the sword balls carved with rune arrays swirling rapidly among them. Staring at Li Jialing, he announced, "Had it not been for Grandpa Yao, I would've taught you a lesson a long time ago!"

"Same for me."

Li Jialing narrowed his eyes and said, "Had it not been for Brother Yao, I assure you that I would've turned you from a stud into a mule. Do you know their difference?"

"A*shole!"

Han Te burst into fury. Then he realized that something was not right. "You are tricking me! I call him 'Grandpa Yao', and yet you call him 'Brother Yao'. Doesn't that mean I'm multiple generations lower than you? Golden-haired boy, it's too cunning of you!"

Li Jialing: "… Idiot."

"You—"

Han Te was about to go on a rampage when he saw a petite person running to the place. Delighted, he retreated his spiritual energy and put on a smile. "Liu Li, are you here for Grandpa Yao or Master Fist King?"

It was exactly Liu Li, the girl who grew up and escaped from the Land of Sins together with him.

Liu Li was still mad at Han Te. Seeing that he was guarding the door, she sniffed and became gloomy, looking at the other direction.

However, she was greatly shocked when she saw the other young man who was guarding the door and couldn't help but look back in Han Te's direction as she exclaimed, "A cat demon!"

Li Jialing finally had enough of it. The golden hair all over his body stood up, making his head twice as large as usual, while he roared, "Who's a cat demon? Are the people from the Land of Sins all as ignorant as you? This is a lion, a magnificent golden lion!"

"Aya!"

Scared, Liu Li hurried to hide behind Han Te, while Han Te shouted and waved his iron arms at Li Jialing. The three young people became a mess.

Of course, it was a rather unharmonious episode of the meeting of the establishment of the partnership of pyromaniacs.

Eventually, a common understanding was achieved inside and outside of the meeting room.

The small team made of the most bizarre-looking, unbelievable, and strong eccentrics of the Pangu universe was eventually named as "Partnership of Pyromaniacs", on the condition that Li Yao would no longer wear Boss Bai's same-type robe in the future, at least not right in front of Boss Bai.

After the team was set up, the construction of the "Coalition Fleet of Pyromaniacs", co-founded by the Big Bai Pirate Gang and the Cultivators' fleet, was finally set on the correct and high-speed track.

The first to come was the maintenance, modification, and upgrade of the starships.

Having captured tremendous starships of the twenty-eight pirate gangs, Li Wuji's fleet, and the Flying Rainbow Fleet, the "Coalition Fleet of Pyromaniacs" almost had infinite possibilities of modification.

As if he were a disloyal son who was selling his father's properties without cherishing, Boss Bai dismantled the power units of abundant starships in the most extravagant way and installed all of them on the few starships that he intended to retain.

In the end, the power units of those starships were mostly three times more than the power units on the starships of the same type, and in extreme cases, they were six times more powerful. It meant that they would have the unparalleled acceleration and the agility that would almost, no, absolutely lacerate the shell and the internal structure of the starships.

Nobody had ever done such a thing before.

Because starships were not the faster, the better. Even if they were pursuing the maximal speed, the goal could not be achieved by simply installing a few more power units.

It also required the redesign of the shape of the starship, perfection of the structure, the most well-trained and fearless crew, and the ancillary artificial intelligence system that could accurately control every rune array in the power units.

As it happened, most of the conditions could be satisfied in the Coalition Fleet of Pyromaniacs.

For the conditions that could not be met, it was Boss Bai's opinion that they could be made up with courage and luck.

"Trust me. Having walked to where we are today, we are definitely the luckiest people in the Pangu universe. So, those starships will absolutely have no faults even if they are twice as fast as their maximal speed, as long as you are not on our starships. Absolutely!"

Boss Bai patted his chest and promised Li Yao.

Next, it was the modification of the Fist King's starship "Iron Fist" and the preparation for Xiaoming and Wenwen's own starships.

While Xiaoming and Wenwen were capable of hacking the Spiritual Nexus freely, this was a material world after all. Their brains were still undeveloped, which meant that their overly powerful abilities were restricted, and if they were to use such abilities, it might cause irreversible brain injuries.

Li Yao, Boss Bai, and the Fist King discussed for a long time. Eventually, they prepared two "Swirl"-level cyberwar cruisers of the Imperium as their special starships.

The starships of such a model did not have fierce firepower, but they carried a very powerful crystal processor system and cyberwarfare system. It was their responsibility to disrupt the enemy's Spiritual Nexus, fabricate signals, and camouflage the network of their own side in wartime. They were the best carriers for information lives.

"Swirl" was not the most advanced model of cyberwar cruisers of the Imperium. As a matter of fact, there were quite a few better ones in Li Wuji's fleet that was newly captured.

However, "Swirl" had the greatest advantage in that the starships of such a model were relatively large in size because of the underdeveloped technologies, which meant that there was great potential for modification. After the abundant useless facilities were removed, there would be room for more mainframe crystal processors.

Eventually, the two Swirl-level cyberwar cruisers were respectively installed with eight mainframe crystal processors that were dismantled from other starships.

According to the general rule of starship upgrade and modification, it was an absurd approach that would result in the redundancy of computational ability and excessive energy consumption. The mainframe crystal processors were the greatest energy consumers on starships. The main guns only consumed spiritual energy when they were fired, but the mainframe crystal processors could consume astronomical spiritual energy every second.

For a normal commander, piling eight mainframe crystal processors on one starship was like installing a hundred cannons on one tower. It was utterly stupid.

But for Xiaoming and Wenwen, there could never be enough crystal processors.

In their words, they were trapped in the flesh and blood like fish living in a small pond previously. By connecting to the eight mainframe crystal processors via the nerval interaction liquids, they felt that they had leaped from a pond into a giant river, and they were now able to swim and soar.

However, eight mainframe crystal processors were also the limits of what their tiny bodies could bear.

For the information lives, every new connected crystal processor was like one more piece of bread for a human being.

One's body could only be nourished and built on by eating bread in a good time so that more bread could be swallowed and digested later. The process of adaptation was necessary.

It was the same for information lives. Connecting hundreds of mainframe crystal processors to Xiaoming and Wenwen's brains and asking them to power the equipment would wear them out.

Li Yao and the Fist King, one being the kids' father and the other being their own kind, had devoted all their knowledge to the modification of the two kids' starships and were very satisfied with the final result.

Looking at the newly-crafted "Super Swirl"-level cyberwar cruisers, Li Yao and the Fist King were almost shedding hot tears as lubricants. The two of them almost couldn't wait to name the new starships.

Xiaoming and Wenwen stopped them without any hesitation, claiming that they should keep a low profile until the conditions matured. So, the two starships were simply named as "Xiaoming" and "Wenwen", and it was unnecessary for Li Yao and the Fist King to waste their time on thinking of more awe-inspiring names.

Chapter 2494: New World, New Dominator!

Besides the most advanced crystal processors and cyberwarfare units, the two "Super Swirl"-level warships naturally still needed crews.

While Xiaoming, Wenwen, and the Fist King could reduce half of the posts on board through the fully-automatic operation system, the agility, independence, and creativity of human beings in the key posts could not be replaced by piles of data.

Li Yao chose the reliable soldiers in the Big Bai Pirate Gang who knew the truth of the federation and who still had family in the federation, as well as those in the Cultivators' fleet who held bloody grudges against the Immortal Cultivators, as the crews of "Xiaoming" and "Wenwen".

Those people either considered the federation as their homeland or had the deepest hatred against the Imperium. Their loyalty was certainly not a problem.

The question was how to explain what Xiaoming and Wenwen were to them.

Li Yao had made up his mind that he would not keep the truth a secret for too long.

Xiaoming, Wenwen, and the Fist King had also run careful deductions. If they did not intend to have full conflicts with the civilization of mankind, resulting in heavy losses on both sides, the sooner they revealed the truth, the better.

If they had mastered a dozen fleets and millions of aggressive battle puppets, only to be exposed by somebody else, it was impossible for the paranoid and sensitive human beings not to overreact.

While they were still fledging, they could offer to publicize the truth and grow up under the watch and guidance of mankind. That was perhaps the fastest way of growth for a "child civilization".

Of course, the situation at the center of the cosmos was still too chaotic. The reformists, Blackstar the Great who was hiding among the reformists, the four Kurfürst families, the Covenant Alliance that was coveting from the darkness, the mysterious mausoleum of the Supreme Emperor, as well as the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God whose life statuses were unknown… There were too many variables.

The troops in the Blue Sky Market were far from united too. Since the battle of the Seven Seas Grand Market was about to begin, all the crew members would only be baffled if the truth was completely revealed. Therefore, after discussing with the Fist King and Boss Bai, Li Yao decided to go one step at a time and reveal part of the truth in order to see the crew's reaction.

Regarding the two unbelievably automatic cyberwarfare cruisers, Li Yao's explanation was that they had applied the most up-to-date super artificial intelligence technology from the federation, or to wit, the "Hyper Spirits" technology left by Extraterrestrial Devil Mo Xuan.

Extraterrestrial Devil Mo Xuan created countless artificial intelligence and strengthened eighteen of them into "Hyper Spirits", planning to use them to control the entire federation. That was true.

The Hyper Spirits boasted computational abilities and logic abilities far more advanced than regular artificial intelligence, which allowed them to hack and control a super crystal processor easily. Of course, when they were applied to massive magical equipment complexes, they could significantly improve the automation of the starships or space fortresses. That was also true.

Xiaoming and Wenwen's antecedents were Hyper Spirit No. 17 and Hyper Spirit No. 18. That was also absolutely true.

Of course, however impressive the Hyper Spirits actually were, they were still within the category of artificial intelligence, which was a popular technology in both the federation and the Imperium. It was a necessary ancillary technology to use any large magical equipment. Both the space pirates and the Cultivators of the Imperium had seen too many of such things.

It was an accepted theory that the two starships were controlled by very sophisticated artificial intelligence.

As for Xiaoming and Wenwen, the two lovely kids, Li Yao also admitted to the crew dutifully that they were his children who had naturally endowed abilities that made them very awesome "specialists of artificial intelligence". Only the two of them were capable of controlling the Hyper Spirits on the two starships.

While it was slightly unbelievable, but considering that they were the children of "Vulture Li Yao", the whole thing seemed, well, more understandable now.

As it turned out, the crew got along rather well with the two starships and the kids. The problems that Li Yao was worried about did not happen at all.

Both the space pirates of the federation and the Cultivators of the Imperium were marines. For a marine, what mattered most was always bigger, faster, and stronger starships.

Could there have been a more exciting thing than to be allocated to a starship that boasted the highest cyberwarfare abilities in the entire Imperium?

Although the "Hyper Spirits" almost caused a destructive catastrophe to the federation in the past, weapons themselves were not guilty, and it all depended on whose hands the weapons were. Now that the "Hyper Spirits" belonged to the "Coalition Fleet of Pyromaniacs", of course they should be as strong as possible.

The two kids were particularly amiable too, and they raised a natural sense of protection. After seeing how lovely the kids were, many crew members were secretly complaining about how ruthless Li Yao was to put such little children on starships and involve them in the perilous war. It was simply too risky whatever unique techniques they had!
