All in all, boosted by the crew's pride and sense of unity, and thanks to the automatic performance of the starships, it took only half a month before the two highly-modified cyberwarfare cruisers miraculously gained preliminary combat ability.

The remaining amazing abilities had to be activated and improved in the real battle.

After more than half a month, when the starships of the two branch fleets were mostly done with maintenance and upgrading, Boss Bai supervised a minor drill.

On one side of the drill, it was the fleet commanded by Boss Bai in person in the traditional way.

On the other side, it was the new-generation fleet commanded by the Fist King under the assistance of Xiaoming and Wenwen, which was more intelligent and automatic.

As it turned out, while Boss Bai boasted more experience and elite warships that were faster and fiercer, the victory did not come easily. He had essentially tried everything he could before he tore apart the battle formation of the Fist King's fleet like peeling an onion.

Despite the failure, the Fist King's fleet still led one third of the starships to retreat in order, keeping the seeds of the troop alive, under the delicate projection of firepower, the marvelous coordination, and the absolutely calm and emotionless command.

Of course, such a marvelous "failure" was not entirely to the credit of the Fist King, Xiaoming, and Wenwen, but more like the first perfect cooperation of the newborn information lives and human beings.

Boss Bai was quite right. The pure information lives had obvious vulnerabilities. Their existence and strength were highly dependent on their communication with each other, and in the vast, boundless space, communication could be a burdensome task that was prone to interference.

Human beings, on the other hand, had the greatest advantage that they could process fuzzy orders independently. In the most extreme cases, a human soldier would be an army—an army of himself!

Human beings plus information lives, that was perhaps the right way to fight future wars, and the only path for their civilization to surpass the black wall and reach beyond the Pangu universe.

Seeing that Xiaoming and Wenwen were stronger and better integrated into human society after every day, Li Yao, however, felt a little self-contradictory.

Even though they had revealed part of the truth, some information was still retained. Once the crew learned the whole truth, knowing that the so-called "artificial intelligence" actually had their own minds and will and did not necessarily have to follow the instructions of human beings, what would they think?

Would they be happy, or would they be dreaded, when they found out that the "artificial intelligence" was so similar to human beings?

Human beings could be corrupted and completely lost because of power, wealth, or strength. Was it also the case for artificial intelligence, or information lives?

Right now, Xiaoming, Wenwen, and the Fist King were still relatively weak, and Li Yao believed that they were cooperating with mankind sincerely, but if they mastered the strength far exceeding that of human beings someday, making them ten times, a hundred times, or a thousand times stronger than mankind, what could he do?

Information lives were the creatures and children of mankind. Li Yao had already been taught too many good lessons on the evilness and brutality of mankind. As the saying went, "the apple doesn't fall far from the tree". If that was what mankind was like, what would their descendants be?

Although he had been making blood-boiling claims in front of everyone, claiming that all the problems could be solved through "love" and "communication", he wondered if that was really the case when he considered in secret at night.

In the end, it was Boss Bai who calmed him down.

"I believe in the evil nature of mankind, and space pirates are the evils of the evils, but do you know that even the most ferocious and selfish space pirates on Spider Den in the Flying Star Sector also worked together a lot? Why? Was it because their nature changed? No, it was only because a more formidable enemy had appeared, say, the troop of the Cultivators, and the wicked space pirates were forced to hug each other and warm each other up.

Boss Bai said, "Everybody was born selfish and extremely evil, but in a hazardous, primitive world, an individual was so weak that even the evilest person had to cooperate with other people in order to survive. In the forced cooperation, the initial understanding and trust came into being. That was the case for brothers, family, society, or country. They were not born because of 'love', but only because of some kind of…'have to'.

"Is it possible that civilizations are exactly like human beings?

"Perhaps all civilizations are selfish, heartless, and malevolent in nature. They would not be able to survive otherwise.

"However, faced with the unexpected threats in the vast universe, selfishness, heartlessness, and malevolence alone are far from enough. After they are developed to a certain extent, civilizations will have to team up in order to fight the great threats together.

"So, the focus is not whether or not the Pangu civilization, the civilization of mankind, the information civilization, or the wizardly civilization is evil, because they are all genetically evil. What we should focus on is whether or not there is a threat that they absolutely cannot resist on their own. It will force them to temporarily abandon their evilness and wariness of each other and be united, or to generate a weird thing called 'love'.

"Luckily, it seems that we happen to have such a threat—the Flood."

The Flood that was destroying all the civilizations was a fluke?

It might sound absurd, but it was exactly what the Fist King expressed before.

In the history of mankind, most glorious civilizations originated from the river basins that were frequented by floods. Floods were destruction but also life. Floods brought infinite desperation but were also the harbinger of hope.

Faced with the overwhelming flood, the creatures in the dark forest were left with no choice except to summon all their wisdom and courage, walk out of the forest, and stride forward together bravely, to be the new master of the new world after the flood ebbed!

Chapter 2495: A Letter to Hometown

One month had passed.

At the border of the radiation range of "Blue Emperor", the star closest to the Blue Sky Space Zone, a cluster of alloy that was as thin as paper and as smooth as silk, slowly unfolded, while the ripples on its surface flowed like veins and nerves.

It was both like some sort of liquids flowing with metal colors, and a flag fluttering in the wind, or a beautiful flower blooming slowly.

When it was stretched out into a gigantic metal plate more than ten kilometers in diameter from the highly-compressed liquid metals, the sophisticated, overlapped rune arrays on its surface were completely revealed too. Then, with the strokes of those runes as the fold lines, the metal plate was slightly creased, constituting a complicated structure. In such a way, it looked even more like a huge metal rose.

Absorbing the radiations and waves released by "Blue Emperor", the metal rose shivered and emanated the most glamorous brilliance.

It was exactly one of the five hundred "antennae" of the super-remote communication magical equipment.

Five hundred metal roses were shivering and blossoming in space at the same time.

Li Yao, Boss Bai, the Fist King, Xiaoming, and Wenwen, on a starship, were anchored at the center of the formation made of five hundred metal roses.

When Li Yao came to the Imperium by himself, he carried a portable super-remote communication base.

However, the device was too small in size, and therefore had an unsteady performance and a low confidentiality, meaning that the information it sent out could be captured and intercepted by third parties. Also, it had been riddled with holes in the fierce battles afterward. Naturally, it had been abandoned.

Now that Boss Bai had provided sufficient resources and a steady base, and the Fist King, Xiaoming, and Wenwen could help with the design of the new super-remote communication base, it was possible for them to build a more advanced, secret, and even half-permanent base.

Although he was absolutely confident about the performance of the brand-new base, for absolute safety, Li Yao had still decided to only send information to the federation and ask the federation to not send any message back.

Keeping the one-way communication could perhaps make the federation hide longer at the edge of the cosmos, at least until the situation at the center of the cosmos was clearer.

The federation was not very powerful, and it was more suitable to perform a cutthroat attack at the most critical moment as an unexpected army than to be summoned right now.

Under the help of Xiaoming and Wenwen, Li Yao wrote most of the things he had experienced after he came to the Imperium into a report, including the current situation of the Imperium, the complicated relationships of the reformists, the four families and the Covenant Alliance, and so on, with an appendix containing the introduction to the Imperium's armed forces, the basic structure and performance parameters of the starships of all types, the leadership of the main fleets, etc. Everything was packed and sent back to home.

He believed that the information would be of great help for the construction of the Star Glory Federation in the next step.

Of course, there was still the Pangu universe and… the Flood.

Before he sent everything over, Li Yao had already reminded his hometown that it was a one-way secret document, and that they were fine right now and in no need of reinforcements from home.

In the future, they would send a secret document home every month, announcing that they were still safe and could handle everything. Uncontrollable events had happened only if their message was not received for three months in a row.

The above was all about business.

What came next was naturally the personal issues.

Li Yao naturally did not forget to send a few words to Ding Lingdang by the way. However, he bit his fingernail for a long time, only to come up with no explanation for the existence of Xiaoming and Wenwen.

Thinking for a long time, he decided to do it in the most dutiful and silly way.

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao said in the most lighthearted tone, "Honey, before I knew it, we've already been separated for such a long time. I miss you so much. Did you miss me? Hahahaha! Let me tell you, everything is fine in the Imperium, and I've been keeping a low profile without running into any accident. Also, I've found a few old friends, including Long Yangjun and Boss Bai. Long Yangjun is that sissy eunuch from the Ancient Sages Sector, and you know who Boss Bai is! You should feel safe knowing that I'm with them, right?

"Right, guess who else have I also encountered? It's certainly not what you have guessed. I've met Xiaoming and Wenwen, our biological children! Isn't it a pleasant surprise? Hahahaha!

"Listen to my explanation, honey, just take a deep breath and listen to my explanation. Well, this is not what you have imagined. It is actually very serious, very magnificent, and very influential, involving all human beings and the future of our entire Pangu universe…"

With a headful of sweat, Li Yao managed to finish his speech before he launched the secret file to the infinite void with the five hundred fluctuating metal roses that were powered by the energy of "Blue Emperor".

He looked back, only to discover that Long Yangjun was observing the outside of the starship through the window cautiously in a heavy crystal suit.

Li Yao found it odd. "What are you doing? Are you scared that an enemy might break in?"

"No, I'm scared of your wife."

Long Yangjun said, "Based on the way you talked just now and your wife's character, I'm under the impression that she might tear apart the void and reach the center of the cosmos the next second after she receives such a 'pleasant surprise'."

Well, despite Li Yao's unrefined way of talking, the Star Glory Federation, under the leadership of Ding Lingdang, had basically learned the situation at the center of the cosmos, as well as the vaster and more unpredictable universe beyond.

As for Earth, Li Yao had shared the secret with Ding Lingdang before he left the federation. They were both doing their best, preparing themselves for the trip to Earth in the future.

The completion of the first super-remote communication also marked the conclusion of the preliminary combination of the "Coalition Fleet of Pyromaniacs".

The new crew, new starships, and even new life forms were all understanding and melting with each other at an unexpected speed.

Of course, the company of human beings and information lives was not entirely lacking of setbacks. Even though the two parties could communicate without reserve on the surface, there was still a long way to go before they reached the real "understanding". For example…

Chi! Chi! Bam!

The No. 9 maintenance workshop of the Blue Sky Market was the Fist King's personal training field.

At this moment, besides the hundred busy maintenance puppets controlled by the Fist King himself, there was nobody else except Liu Li, who was wearing overalls with pockets all over her body that were all stuffed with maintenance tools and who looked like a rather professional mechanic with the oil on her pinky face.

However, Liu Li's performance today was disproportionate to her professional style. In her absentmindedness, she used too much strength and burnt up a chip inside the head of one of the Fist King's bodies.

"Miss Liu Li, you were distracted again. The AC control chip inside me has been burnt, and my voice has been transformed into this."

The Fist King spoke with a high-pitched voice that sounded like a hen. "In the past seven days, the precision of your operations has dropped by 5%, and your faulty rate has increased by 11%. Your brainwaves are always in a disordered and unsteady state too. Considering that you haven't suffered any physical injury, what caused your psychological issues? Was it Han Te?"

"No!"

Liu Li jumped like a cat whose tail was stepped on. She hurried to cover herself by saying, "No, you're wrong, Master Fist King!"

"Your breath, your heartbeat, and the constriction of your pores are highly abnormal and match with the average changes of biophysical parameters that indicate 'guilty' in my database. There is a 98% chance that you are feeling guilty. So, this is truly a Han Te situation, isn't it?"

The Fist King thought for a moment and tried to comfort Liu Li, "I don't know what is bothering you. As far as I know, it is true that Han Te and some of the women of the Big Bai Pirate Gang, the Cultivators' fleet, and the Blue Sky Market have shared their genetic information with each other and stimulated certain nerval synapses inside their bodies in certain special postures, resulting in shivering and cramping. But I assure you that they were all voluntary, and that there was no deception or coercion. He brought joy to himself and other people. So, what are you mad about?

"Ah, I see. Han Te brought joy to so many people but not to you. It seems that he is unwilling to exchange genetic information with you. Is that the reason for your dissatisfaction?"

"Of course not!"

Liu Li's face was so red that blood seemed to be dripping. She gritted her teeth and stomped. "What nonsense are you talking about, Master Fist King? I-I couldn't care less whom Han Te brought joy to or whom… with whom he exchanged the damn genetic information!

"I just feel that the friend that I've known for almost twenty years has changed. He has changed so much after stepping into space from our hometown that he is like a different person now!"

"A drastic change in the environment always leads to a change of character. That is growth."

The Fist King said calmly, "You've also changed a lot in the past year. Just look at yourself in the mirror. Can you still see the young girl who held the Gold Barley on the Land of Sins a year ago? It is only natural that Han Te has also changed."

"But he has become someone who is too cocky and egotistical!"

Liu Li put the maintenance tools into her pockets and declared angrily, her arms akimbo and her cheeks puffed out, "It's not just about… what we said just now. He is also always saying weird stuff nowadays, like 'don't laugh at a young man when he is still poor' or 'Lord Han Te does not need to explain what he does to anybody else'. He is now also eccentric and extreme. A few days ago, a crew member on Stainless Mouse pissed him off, and he beat the guy's bones into dozens of pieces ruthlessly. Dear heavens. Was he still the Han Te that I knew? How did he change into this?

"I-I truly don't know. He is the only 'family' that I have after coming to such a distant place from our hometown, and we are supposed to be nice to each other. But I truly don't like him right now, the 'Lord Han Te'!"

"Got it. What about Li Jialing?"

The Fist King said, "You've been spending a lot of time with Li Jialing recently, Miss Liu Li. Do you like him? Do you want to stimulate the nerval synapses and exchange genetic information with him?"

Chapter 2496: The Fist King's Prescription

"Of course not!"

Liu Li covered her burning cheeks and shrieked, "What are you talking about? There is nothing between me and Big Cat! Recently, Grandpa Yao has been staying in the Blue Sky Market, and I certainly seized the opportunity to learn the knowledge of refining from him. As it happened, Big Cat was training with Grandpa Yao too. So, the two of us chatted when we were free! I don't like him… No, it's not like I don't like him. He is still a nice friend except that he may look taciturn. At the very least, he is ten thousand times better than the philandering Han Te! But, aya, his hairy appearance! All in all, he and I are not in that kind of relationship at all. Also, why are you asking about this, Master Fist King? This is truly meaningless!"

"I have two reasons."

The Fist King completely disregarded the girl's embarrassment and extended his two iron fingers. "Firstly, I reached a deal with Li Yao. As long as I keep you and Han Te safe, he will do everything possible to help me upgrade or even grow into the strongest being in the universe.

"Up until so far, the deal has turned out to be rather good. I have benefited a lot from it. Also, I have seen Li Yao's real capabilities, and I believe he can still provide tremendous resources for me in the future.

"I see no reason to cut the deal. So, I have to keep you and Han Te safe.

"Han Te is now training with Boss Bai and growing by a thousand miles a day. He can at least protect himself right now. Also, the guy has absolutely no sense of shame. On the psychological level, he is an unkillable cockroach. So, I do not need to worry that he may suffer great damage physically or mentally for now.

"Miss Liu Li, however, is different. You are not a battle-type trainer, and your mind is much more feeble than his. Now, my test result shows that you are in a protracted trauma. As per the agreement I have reached with Li Yao, I have to cure and protect your mind, preventing it from being bothered and hurt again.

"Secondly, and more importantly, you are already my personal assistant for maintenance and upgrade. It is not exactly a highly-technical job, but it requires a 100% understanding of my body and my data pattern. In other words, we must have absolute trust with each other.

"It is difficult to raise someone suitable for the job. Considering that your performance has been generally satisfactory so far, I do not want to waste my time on grooming a new one. Do you understand? You are irreplaceable.

"With that in mind, I hope that you can be 100% devoted. You must not affect the critical work or undermine my combat ability because of your psychological issues. Even if my combat ability is only diminished by 1% because of your unsteady performance, it may result in catastrophic ramifications. Do you understand?"

Liu Li was rather stunned. Looking at the breadboard of chips inside the Fist King's opened skull that had been burnt up because of her mistake, Liu Li lowered her head and said in a low voice, her cheeks even redder, "Got it. Master Fist King, I'm sorry. It was all my fault."

"It's not your fault, just your body malfunctioning. A malfunction caused by the irregular secretion of the hormones inside your body."

The Fist King said comfortingly, "All high-precision creatures and machines may malfunction. Even I can malfunction too. Malfunctioning is not scary. Don't cover them, don't evade them, but face, analyze, and fix them rationally and calmly. Then, which one do you prefer, Han Te or Li Jialing?"

"Why are we still discussing that?"

Biting her lips softly, Liu Li subconsciously drew on the floor with the tip of her foot. Thinking for a long time, she finally replied, rather embarrassedly, "I-I don't like either of them. I feel that Han Te is too passionate and arrogant, and Big Cat is too cold and proud. All in all, I think that neither of them has learned to control their feelings. That is… That is, they are still too immature!"

"Okay."

The Fist King said, "You are fond of mature men, like?"

"Like…"

Liu Li was even more embarrassed. She lowered her head and said, "Someone whom I can absolutely trust and who will always protect me, like Grandpa Yao."

Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep!

The Fist King's crystal processor that was exposed to the outside had huge noises, with smoke popping up.

"Huh?"

Liu Li bulged her eyes. "What are you doing, Master Fist King? Why did you suddenly consume so much computational ability? What complicated problem were you calculating?"

"I'm calculating the possibility of inviting Li Yao to come and exchange genetic information with you as well as the danger coefficient."

The Fist King was silent for a long time before he said, "I'm sorry. I don't think that my combat ability is higher than Li Yao's, and it's difficult for me to capture him here. Also, I was told that his wife is known as the best expert at the edge of the cosmos. If I really do that, I'm afraid that I would place you in great danger, which is against my purpose to keep you safe. So, is there a second candidate that can restore the tranquility of your mind?"

"Aya! Aya! Aya!"

Liu Li stomped on the ground angrily. "What were you thinking, Master Fist King? That was only an example that I made with someone that we were both familiar with. It's not like I would like anyone as mature as Grandpa Yao. He should also be tall, strong, handsome, and sunny!"

"Okay…"

The Fist King pondered for 0.1 seconds and nodded his head. "In that case, Li Yao is truly not a suitable candidate."

"Master Fist King—"

Liu Li tried to hold it back but failed. "Since you asked such embarrassing questions, it makes me wonder, are you ever in love with anyone?"

"Yes, and no."

The Fist King said, "I've never experienced it in person, but I just downloaded a lot of literary works on love as well as the relevant biophysical and psychological researches. All the data have been read. I certainly have abundant theoretical experience. So, trust me, Miss Liu Li, I will definitely cure your trauma and let you be devoted to your work again."

"What are you talking about? Love does not need theories. It needs practice. Practice!"

Liu Li and the Fist King had been together for almost a year, and she gradually became bold in front of the cold machine. "All in all, there is no need for you to worry about me, Master Fist King. I am certainly not one of those fragile, emotional girls. I grew up on the dangerous Land of Sins too!

"I'm just like the Gold Barley on the Land of Sins, which seems small and insignificant but is actually strong and highly resistant to wind and coldness. Hahahaha!

"It's not like I need a man to live on. I can live perfectly well, carry out my dream, and enjoy my little, happy life even without a man!

"I can treat my wounds and face everything by myself. Just let 'Lord Han Te' go to hell!

"Besides… Besides, there's still Master Fist King. Master Fist King will always protect me, right?"

The little girl's face was as unpredictable as the weather in June. She was embarrassed and infuriated a moment ago, and yet she was now refreshed and leaning to the Fist King in a smile.

"That's not right."

The Fist King, however, ignored the girl's smile heartlessly. "First of all, my protection for you is based on my deal with Li Yao. Of course, if you keep making progress in your maintenance skills, proving yourself to be a great help during my maintenance and upgrade, then I will continue to protect you for my own survival even if the deal is canceled.

"But on the other hand, even if I want to protect you all the time, I fear that I cannot 'always' do so. The day will come that I am destroyed.

"So, I cannot protect you forever in any case. The best I can do is to protect you until the moment of my destruction. Please understand, Miss Liu Li."

"… Has anybody told you that you have a weird way of talking, Master Fist King?"

Liu Li frowned. "Also, why do you sound normal when you speak to other people but so weird when you are talking to me?"

"Because this is the real me?"

The Fist King said, "I talk to other people with a simulated language logic, but doing so for a long time will result in data overflow and system redundancies that need regular cleaning. Since nobody else is here, I prefer the concise and accurate way of expressing myself."

"How quirky. I haven't met many spectral Cultivators, but Master Fist King is certainly the quirkiest of them all."

Liu Li rubbed her nose and scanned the Fist King's crystal processor carefully again. "It seems that a 'Light Arrow – IV' chip has been burnt. I'll find a new one."

Liu Li hopped out of the maintenance workshop and was going to the storage room of crystal chips when she was taken aback by the person outside the door.

"Han Te?"

Liu Li's smile was immediately pulled down into a frown. She asked, "What are you doing here? You don't need to ingratiate yourself with your big sisters?"

"I'm sorry, Liu Li, I was wrong."

The moment he saw Liu Li was out, Han Te immediately bowed ninety degrees at Liu Li and said sincerely, "I've thought about it for a long time. I have been too absurd, shameless, and hilarious in the past half a year. I have no idea how I turned into this. Perhaps it is because the world outside is too splendid, and our hometown was too monotonous. All in all, I'm not looking for an excuse for myself, and I only want to apologize to you sincerely.

"I'm sorry, Liu Li. Will you forgive me?"

"Well…"

Stunned, Liu Li was lost for a while.

It had been forever since she last saw Han Te so solemn with such pure eyes, which reminded her of the difficult but simple life on the Land of Sins.

In a trance, she was about to accept his apology when she vaguely felt that something was off. Staring at "Han Te" for a long time, Liu Li summoned her courage, extended her finger, and jabbed the forehead of "Han Te".

Her finger entered into the forehead of "Han Te" more than easily, and she did not feel coldness and hardness until her fist knuckle was submerged.

Without any question, it was not a real human being, but a puppet that had been fabricated into the appearance of Han Te with light and shadow.

"Ah!"

Liu Li backed off and screamed.

"What's up?"

The iron puppet immediately retreated the camouflage, turning from "Han Te" into a bald and cold look. Somewhat at a loss, it let out the Fist King's voice. "Is there a problem?"

"Is… Is there a problem?"

Liu Li was almost crying. Pointing at the Fist King, she asked, her voice shivering, "Was it you who did it, Master Fist King?"

"Yes."

The Fist King said in a dull voice, "I thought that it would cure you and make you happy. Or was I wrong to have chosen the skin of 'Han Te'? Would it have been better to use Li Jialing or another… mature man who is tall, strong, handsome, and sunny?"

Chapter 2497: Hot Machines

Liu Li was stunned for a long time and finally collapsed. Angrily, she drew a wrench from her pocket and hit the Fist King's head. She shouted and hit, hit and cried, and cried and shouted.

"It seems that I've truly made a mistake."

The Fist King controlled the iron puppet to sit down. While Liu Li relentlessly hit him, he held his chin and fell into deep thought. "Let's not be devoted to hitting just yet. Can you tell me what I did wrong? I can write the error in my log, and I guarantee that it will not happen again."

"Who… Who asked you to apologize to me in the appearance of Han Te?"

Clinging to the giant wrench, Liu Li sobbed and said, "Is Han Te's apology so precious? When I said I'm fine, I'm fine! Nobody asked you to treat my wounds for me! Besides, it's not the real Han Te. It's all fake! Everything is fake!"

"I knew that it was a bit too unconvincing."

The Fist King said solemnly, "It can't be helped. There are only limited resources in the Blue Sky Market, and it's impossible to create a 100% simulated puppet. However, I promise you that as long as you work hard for me, I will gather the most expensive materials in the Imperium and build a vivid puppet for you that will be covered in the softest silica gel and embedded with the tiniest sensors. Its specifications will be absolutely the same as the real Han Te's.

"Not just Han Te, if you want Li Jialing or anybody else, I can help you make all of them for you. Even ten will not be a problem. They will be around you all day and cater to you like you are the queen of the Imperium. I'm sure you will be happy, right?"

"Happy my a*s!"

Liu Li was so exasperated that she completely forgot that she was faced with the tough Fist King. Weeping hard, she continued hitting him with the wrench. "Who wants those puppets? Fake is always fake. However vivid it is, they can't be real! Who wants them?!"

"Is… that so?"

All the indicators on the Fist King dimmed as he mumbled, "Fake is always fake, and fake can't turn real… So, that was my mistake."

The puppet's shoulders gradually lowered, as if the vigor was leaking from invisible gaps.

"M-Master Fist King?"

As if waking up from a dream, Liu Li finally realized what she had done. She was bold enough to attack Master Fist King, the most ferocious dominator of the Land of Sins. She was truly out of her mind!

Liu Li immediately panicked. She was wondering what she should do when she noticed that all the puppets in the workshop dimmed and slowed, as if the Fist King had suffered a major mental blow.

Liu Li had never seen the Fist King like this before. Overwhelmed, she went back to the room and shouted, "What's wrong, Master Fist King? Are you alright? I-I'm sorry. I was too excited and said things I shouldn't have said. I was also insane enough to hit you with a wrench. I probably really need a rest!"

"That's alright. If you feel uncomfortable, just go and take a rest."

Controlling a new clone to raise its head, the Fist King said casually and paused for a moment. Then, he said again, "So, you are not fond of fake things, are you?"

"Of course not."

Liu Li said matter-of-factly, "Is there anybody who likes fake things? But what's wrong about that? I still don't understand. Why is Master Fist King suddenly so down? Can you tell me? Didn't you say that malfunction must be analyzed and faced bravely when it occurred? I think that it is not me who's malfunctioning, but you!"

"Probably. This is strange. After I met Li Yao and you, my abilities have improved a lot, but the malfunction rate is also higher and higher. Sometimes, the most bizarre malfunctions are happening, ones that I never encountered before."

The Fist King lowered his head and looked at his hands that were lackluster due to the unremoved rust. "You're right. Nobody likes fake things, and fake can never turn real. But what if I am a fake thing? I am… not a real human being?"

"Is that a big deal?"

Amused, Liu Li waved her hands and said, "Everybody knows that. You are a ghost. It's no biggie. There's no need to worry about it at all!"

The Fist King said, "You're not scared of ghosts?"

"What's scary about ghosts?"

Liu Li's tears turned into a smile. "On the Land of Sins, the most horrible things were all done by people. Ghosts never harmed anyone. It is needless to say that Master Fist King is such a great ghost with a strong sense of justice!"

The Fist King hesitated for a moment and asked again, "But what if I am neither a human nor a ghost?"

"Huh?"

Liu Li bulged her big round eyes. "Neither a human nor a ghost? Then what are you?"

"A machine."

The Fist King's voice was mixed with vague sorrow. "A cold machine."

Liu Li asked, "What's that supposed to mean?"

The Fist King said, "Literally what it means. If I am just a cold machine, will it affect our affiliation or your devotion and efficiency in your work?"

"Well…"

Liu Li scratched her head with the big wrench, accidentally smearing the oil and dirt on her hair. "Master Fist King, you always talk about the stuff that other people can't understand. I haven't read many books, and I grew up in an isolated environment. I don't know humans, ghosts, machines, or any of that, but I can tell who is nice to me and who is not! Since Master Fist King will always protect me—"

"Not always—"

The Fist King corrected Liu Li again. "But until the moment of my destruct—"

"Aya!"

This time, Liu Li interrupted him impolitely. "That's just a rhetorical device. You don't have to be so pedantic. Anyway, since Master Fist King will always protect me, I will certainly work hard with a high efficiency every day!

"Also, hehe, allow me to point out a minor mistake of yours. Machines are not necessarily 'cold'. Just feel your skull. How about it? Isn't it burningly hot inside? The chips inside are almost on fire. How are you cold?

"In terms of temperature, the human body needs to be kept around 37 degrees, but machines can work well above a hundred degrees. So, machines are not necessarily cold but can be as hot as fire!"

Catching the loophole in the Fist King's words, Liu Li blinked with a gloating smile.

The Fist King couldn't help but touch and feel his skull. It was indeed hot.

Of course, he realized that it was unnecessary after he did that because he could've monitored the temperature of the crystal processor in real time with the sensors inside the skull.

It was an unnecessary, redundant movement.

Recently, he had more and more redundant movements. Was it because of a certain new, major malfunction that had not been detected yet?

"According to my test, your breath, heartbeat, and secretion of sweat have been back to normal."

The Fist King refreshed the interface and resumed tranquility. He said, "You seem to have walked out of your coma. Are you alright now?"

"Yes, I think I am. Because of the episode just now, I think I forgot the other things!"

Liu Li was slightly stunned. Thinking for a moment, she shook her finger at the Fist King quickly. "However, I need to inform you with all solemnity that what you did wasn't right, Master Fist King. You must not simulate other people's look or voice without their permission, because it may cause major trouble to them."

"Got it."

The Fist King said, "I promise that I will not simulate Han Te's appearance without the permission of you and Han Te."

"Not, it's not just about Han Te and me."

Han Te pouted her cheeks and put her hands on her hips. As if she were the Fist King's little housekeeper, she declared, "You cannot mimic anybody else because that will lead to a mess! Besides, the Fist King is already a magnificent man. Why do you need to assume somebody else's appearance?"

"… Fine."

After a brief silence, the Fist King said, "I promise that I will not simulate anybody else without their permission."

"Really?"

Her eyes narrowed, Liu Li looked at the Fist King suspiciously. "No, Master Fist King has picked up the bad habit of lying now. You can't make a promise with nothing. Let's do a pinky swear!"

The Fist King said, "I'm aware of the pinky swear as a social custom, but I doubt if it has any guaranteed effect."

"Aya, it doesn't matter. I just want to pull fingers with Master Fist King. Come on!"

In a playful mood, Liu Li pulled the Fist King's iron arm over and curled the little finger. "Haha. We've already done a pinky swear. You must see to your promise, Master Fist King!"

"I still did not sense any effect on the spiritual energy or the soul…"

The Fist King's artificial eyes were blinking. "However, as long as you are happy and can get back to work calmly, so be it. Are you recovered now?"

"I've fully recovered, in full health!"

Liu Li waved her arms and declared, but her belly suddenly bubbled. The little girl's face was reddened again. "Excuse me, Master Fist King. I haven't eaten much in the past few days due to my bad mood. I-I'll refill a can of high-energy drugs before I get back to work."

"No."

The Fist King shook his head.

"Huh?"

Liu Li was stunned again.

"I have learned a lot from the literal works and the masterpieces on emotions. You just walked out of a major trauma. How can you eat the tasteless high-energy drugs? Such lousy rapid food will only ruin your mood and sabotage your perfect work state."

The Fist King said, "For safety reasons, you should have a decent, delicious meal."

"It does make sense."

Liu Li smiled. "It… It will take a long time. Also, I'm not the greatest cook. Han Te used to say that the dishes I made could poison people. Forget it. I think I can deal with it."

"No, your work state is concerned with my combat ability. You must not tolerate anything."

The Fist King said calmly and stood up, going straight out of the room.

Liu Li: "Master Fist King, where are you going?"

The Fist King: "To cook."

Liu Li: "Huh? Huh? Huh? Can you cook, Master Fist King?"

The Fist King: "I've never tried it before, but I've downloaded 192 recipes of multiple schools of multiple worlds that cover all flavors. I'm sure that one of them will be satisfactory for you."

Liu Li: "For me? M-Master Fist King is going to cook for me?"

"Exactly. It has occurred to me that I have overlooked your daily life and your emotional changes and regarded you as another cold machine, which resulted in your unsteady work state. This is unacceptable negligence since a great battle is coming near. Therefore, starting from this moment, I will micro-adjust the mode of our relationship. I'll have to ask for your cooperation too. Feel free to offer your opinions or suggestions if you have any so that I can modify my behavior."

The Fist King finished his words, but his cold eyes pierced through the ceiling to the sky. "Let the new mode begin with a hot meal! However, first things first—"

Chapter 2498: The Competition of Two Young Men

In the place that Liu Li could not see, two young men were confronting each other in the midair in flames.

They were Han Te and Li Jialing.

Li Jialing's face was hidden below the golden hair, but even the golden hair that was fluttering in the wind could not cover his indifference and mockery. He said casually, "Han Te, if you keep on pestering me like this, it will make people doubt if you have transitioned and that you are even interested in men now."

"Cut the crap!"

Blushing, Han Te pointed at Li Jialing's nose and shrieked, "Golden-haired boy, just admit it. Have you been with Liu Li a lot, harassing her every day?"

Without raising his eyes, Li Jialing said coldly, "Does it have anything to do with you?"

"Stop dodging and just answer my question!"

Han Te burst into fury. "Liu Li has grown up together with me since childhood. I have never regarded her as a junior sister but as my own biological sister. I will never allow anyone to harm her! A young master from an extravagant noble family like you can't be anything good. You must be dallying and philandering with her! I will not allow such a thing to happen. So, listen up, stay away from Liu Li in the future!"

"Did my ears just deceive me?"

Li Jialing finally raised his head slightly, but it was contempt that was beaming out of his golden eyes. "As the greatest playboy yourself, you have the audacity of accusing me of tricking and seducing women? Just mind your own business with your 'big sisters'. Are you even qualified to meddle between Liu Li and me?"

"I promised Liu Li's father that I would take good care of her no matter what!"

Blushing, Han Te tried to defend himself. "You do not belong in the same world at all. You cannot bring happiness to her. So, just stop messing around with her!"

"First of all, there is nothing between her and me at all. We were just training with Brother Yao together."

Li Jialing narrowed his eyes, with the golden hair on his back rising. Wickedness suddenly beamed out of his eyes as he said with a cold smile, "Secondly, a scumbag like you is utterly unqualified to say the bulls*it like 'protect her and bring her happiness'. Are you not the person who really hurt and devastated her in the past month?

"You are also interfering with her regular friend-making under the excuse of protecting her? Hehe. You are just satisfying your own boring possessiveness. You're only angry because the girl that has been following you is taken by somebody else.

"You are truly a sucker, Han Te. You have so many 'big sisters' whom you flirt with every day, and you never care about Liu Li's feelings or tend to her daily life. But as it turns out, the moment she finds a regular friend that is me and talks to me once in a while, you can't take it anymore and jump out in a hurry?"

Han Te was rendered speechless by what Li Jialing said. Stunned for a long time, he raised his voice and shouted, "Cut the crap. Are you leaving Liu Li or not?"

"What can you do if I don't? Who do you think you are to stand between Liu Li and me?"

The wickedness in Li Jialing's eyes was more and more intense. "Leaving aside the fact that we are just regular friends, and there is no such thing as me leaving her, even if we were really together, should it be your concern at all?"

"Ah-ha!"

His eyes popping out, Han Te waved his hands and shouted, "You've finally revealed your evil intentions and dropped your disguise of 'regular friend'. You are admitting that you want more from Liu Li, aren't you? No, I will not allow someone like you to tarnish Liu Li!"

Tilting his head, Li Jialing thought for a moment and replied, "…'Tarnish', as in how you 'tarnished' your big sisters?"

"That's totally different!"

Han Te's face was so red that his nostrils were almost spurting blood. "Liu Li is different from them!"

"I'm different from you, too. I think with my upper half body, unlike certain automatic cannons that will fire at any hole—my apologies, certain rapid automatic cannons."

Li Jialing said, one word after another, "So, just rest assured. Even if Liu Li and I were really together, I would certainly make her the happiest woman in the entire universe."

"You—"

The moment he pictured Liu Li with another man, Han Te felt that he was struck by a lightning, and Liu Li was suddenly broken away from the shackle of "biological sister".

His face immediately turned pale. Finding such a scene utterly unacceptable, he demanded, "You can't be together. You absolutely can't. You can't bring happiness to Liu Li. Li Jialing, I've seen through you. I can see the real you deep in your bones. You are a cold and heartless man who does not care about personal feelings. What you pursue after is… something higher, deeper, and greater. For that, you can abandon everything, including your love!"

"Just like what I said, it's not your business."

Li Jialing smiled. The cruelty deep inside him that was inherited from Li Linghai gradually bloomed. He was like a big cat looking at a mouse when he said unhurriedly, "Did you know? I thought to be Liu Li's regular friend only, but I've changed my mind. Now that I think about it, Liu Li has such a miserable life as such a lovely girl. Not only was she born on the horrible Land of Sins, but she also has a crazy and stupid big brother whom she grew up with. Tsk, tsk, tsk. Are you planning to cage her forever to prevent her from getting in touch with men of her age?

"Such a life seems too poignant. It appears to me that the helpless, tortured girl is in dire need of a handsome, gentle, and comforting golden-haired prince to save her from the despair!"

Han Te was dumbfounded for a long time. "What… golden-haired prince? You are not talking about yourself, are you?"

"Of course I am."

Li Jialing shook his head of flying golden hair and said, "I will be Liu Li's savior. Is there a problem?"

"There certainly is!"

Han Te yelled, "You narcissist! You think you are a 'savior'? Damn it. You are even more haughty than I am. You are a pervert!"

"Hehe, whatever you say, I'm going to save Liu Li. The person she should be leaving is not me but you. She should be as far away from her sucker brother as possible, and the further she is, the happier she will be!"

Li Jialing's lips curled. "All in all, I will not let Liu Li return to the dark life where everything was controlled by you. Before, she didn't have a choice, but she has me now. I am her savior, I am her star, and I am her light of hope! You are no longer needed here, Han Te. Just get lost and play with your 'big sisters'!"

"A*shole!"

The electric arcs on Han Te's iron arm glowed brighter and brighter. The young man from the Land of Sins bellowed, "You are pushing me into this. Since you are being utterly unreasonable, let's decide to whom Liu Li will belong in the most straightforward way!"

"You are…"

Li Jialing's eyes were deep. "… asking to be killed!"

"Ahhhhh!"

Han Te roared and charged at Li Jialing in a streak of brilliance.

Of course, only one tenth of a second later, he was blown backward in exactly the same way, only three times faster with a head as swollen as that of a pig.

"Ignoring the giant difference in our capabilities in the past…"

With his right hand behind his back, Li Jialing had blown Han Te away with only the left arm. He said unconcernedly, "Let's just talk about the one month after we came to the Blue Sky Market. While I trained myself hard, you were having a good time with your big sisters. What can you use to defeat me, Han Te?"

Han Te was so humiliated that blood was almost shooting out of his eyes. He shrieked and charged forward again. Without any suspense, he was blown back again for a second time.

A third time, a fourth time, a fifth time… It was still the case for the tenth time.

After ten rounds, there was not any part of Han Te's skin that was still intact. He was like a sponge that was fully saturated with water, utterly losing the shape of a human being.

But his eyes that had been squeezed into tiny gaps were as stubborn as before!

"I've had enough fun."

Li Jialing yawned and said, "The result will be the same even if you come for another hundred rounds. Why do you have to humiliate yourself? You know as clearly as I do that you are absolutely no match for me."

"It's true that… I am no match for you right now!"

Han Te's blue-and-black face was stained with thick blood. He was so ashamed that he was almost tearing a hole in the air to hide himself in. However, clinging to his dignity, he stood straight before Li Jialing and pointed at the young lion, shouting, "However, listen close. Don't laugh at a young man when he is still poor! If you think you are capable, just give me ten years, and I will certainly become unimaginably strong after training, before I come back and blow up your head! I dare you to a deal of ten years! Let's have another match ten years later!"

Li Jialing was dumbfounded, as if he were intimidated by the daunting vibe Han Te suddenly unleashed. Then…

Shua!

Li Jialing opened his fingers and released his spiritual energy, grabbing Han Te by his neck remotely and dragging the guy to his front.

"I'm sorry. I've been misunderstanding you, Han Te."

Li Jialing said solemnly, "I thought that you were simply arrogant; it was not until this moment that I realized that you were not. You are just a pure moron. Let me ask you, why on earth would I make a deal of ten years with you and give you ten years to train yourself so that you can blow up my head after you come back when I have such a great advantage right now?"

"Well, well—"

Han Te struggled hard. "How can you do this? Where are your morals?"

"I am Li Yao's brother."

Li Jialing asked back, "Do you think I care about morals?"

"… Then just kill me!"

Han Te was both infuriated and embarrassed, but he could not erase the stubbornness in his bones. "After all, the people of the Land of Sins are born to be the toys for the young masters of noble families like you to kill. You can kill me, but I will not give in or surrender!"

"I've been tolerating you for a long time, Han Te."

Li Jialing's narrowed eyes were like two gold daggers. "Do you really think I dare not kill you?"

"Then bring it on!"

Veins protruded on Han Te's forehead. "A man can be executed but must not be insulted. Life for the people of the Land of Sins is exactly death, of which we are absolutely not scared. So just bring it on!"

"You—"

Faced with the scoundrel, Li Jialing was somewhat caught in a dilemma.

Right then, an earsplitting sound echoed from behind them. It was the sound of a storm of bullets coming close!

Chapter 2499: Exactly So Biased

The pupils of Li Jialing and Han Te constricted at the same time. They had absolutely no time to dodge when they were blown to the ground. When they finally avoided the scourge of bullets in a haste, a streak of silver brilliance ten times faster than the bullets arrived from the sky and knocked the two young men to the ground heavily.

"Aya!"

Li Jialing and Han Te exclaimed at the same time when they were thrown into the soil, their faces swollen.

The silver brilliance gathered into a tall, magnificent, elegant, glittering battle puppet. It slowly landed between the young men and opened its arms, with two clusters of silver electric arcs in the two palms, preventing them from further fighting.

"Enough."

The Fist King's calm and nonhuman voice came from the silver puppet. Shifting the artificial eyes on the skull to Li Jialing's side on the rail, he said, "I'm sorry, no matter how annoying Han Te is, I have to ensure his safety and cannot allow anybody to threaten his life according to my deal with Li Yao."

"Who would ever waste time on threatening the life of such a sucker?"

Li Jialing wiped the blood on his lips and glanced at the Fist King. "It was he who had been pestering me. I did not bother to deal with him at all. Since Master Fist King is here, he is all yours. But I suggest you give him a good lesson. Otherwise, even if I don't kill him, he will eventually get himself killed one way or another."

After saying that, Li Jialing left without looking back.

"Hey, hey, hey, golden-haired boy—"

Han Te was more than angry. He was about to chase after the guy when the Fist King grabbed the back of his neck as quickly as a bolt of lightning and tossed him to the ground heavily.

"That's enough!"

The Fist King said, "You are absolutely no match for Li Jialing. Chasing after him will only place an unnecessary threat to your life. I will not allow anybody to do that, yourself included."

"Then help me beat him!"

Han Te rose quickly, like an unkillable cockroach, and spat toward Li Jialing's back that was disappearing. "Master Fist King, we are both from the Land of Sins. I'm sure you're on my side, aren't you?"

"Yes, I'm on your side."

The Fist King said, "But it's only about keeping you safe, not about horsing around with you. Also, I feel that Li Jialing did have a point. You've been too arrogant recently. Or rather, your head is overwhelmed by the torrents of changes, resulting in your high psychological instability.

"Such a trouble-making mental state will greatly increase your odds of encountering dangers and the difficulty for me to protect you. So, I suggest that you slightly change your character and lifestyle?"

"I'm an arrogant troublemaker?"

Han Te bulged his eyes and looked at the Fist King in disbelief. "Master Fist King, even you think that it's my fault, and I've been too arrogant and annoying?"

Pondering for a moment, the Fist King said, "I do not like to judge human beings morally. What you did had nothing to do with me too. However, from the perspective of protecting your safety, it's true that you've been acting rather arrogant and annoying. As far as I know, several guys are already preparing to work together and beat you up in secret. If you do not change yourself, it won't be surprising that you somehow get yourself killed, like what Li Jialing said."

"Even Master Fist King has taken the side of that rich golden-haired boy?"

Han Te stepped back in shock, almost unable to believe his ears. A vague smile of sorrow struggled to appear on his swollen face, and he gritted his teeth for a long time before he finally burst out, "Yes, I am arrogant, I am cocky, I am conceited, but so what?

"From when I was born on the Land of Sins to right now, I've always been a minor cockroach. However tough I am, the experts can easily step on me and kill me, can't they?

"I've been living in fear and may get killed any second since childhood. I've been struggling to survive every second. Nobody has ever given me any chance to be cocky!

"Only in here, only during the brief time when I was in the Big Bai Pirate Gang and the Blue Sky Market can I slightly show myself off. I'm not like you, Master Fist King, you are a big shot. You, Grandpa Yao, and Boss Bai are all magnificent, intimidating big shots. You can be arrogant whenever you want. If not today, tomorrow still works.

"But I am just the humblest person. If I do not show off today, chances are that I will be killed tomorrow. I'll be killed in the vacuum, without leaving the slightest remains!

"Then, why shouldn't I enjoy my life and show off? Who knows where I will be tomorrow? Who knows!

"I'm so scared. I'm scared that I will die an unknown death before I do anything, like so many people did on the Land of Sins and in space! I'm not scared of death, but I want more people to remember me, remember my name…"

Han Te buried his head in his arms and his legs. His shoulders shuddered for a moment before he finally said in a sullen voice, "Master Fist King, does my performance in the past few months really stink?"

The Fist King: "Yes."

Han Te: "Have I really disappointed a lot of people?"

The Fist King: "Yes."

Han Te: "Am I really no match for Li Jialing, and I am not even as good as a little finger of that golden-haired narcissist?"

The Fist King: "In every aspect, yes."

Han Te: "Hey, hey, hey, Master Fist King, you don't have to be so straightforward. Can't you say something nice and comfort my wounded heart?"

The Fist King: "While your recent performance stinks and is absolutely no match for Li Jialing, you are still rescuable. Speaking of which, I may have made a mistake by entrusting you to Boss Bai. He is an excellent fleet commander and a most distinguished space pirate, but he is hardly a qualified life teacher. I will communicate with Boss Bai and change the way of your education. In the meantime, you better control your private life."

"Huh?"

Han Te was confused. "What's that supposed to mean?"

"I am certainly not interfering with your private life. You know who I am. That's the last thing I care about."

The Fist King said, "I'm just reminding you that it will be better for everyone if you could slightly contain yourself, and Liu Li will be less devastated."

"What?"

Han Te picked his ears hard. "My… My ears were humming because of the golden-haired narcissist's beating. I didn't catch what you said in the end. What about Liu Li?"

"Nothing."

After a brief silence, the Fist King said, "You heard wrong. All in all, control your private life and do not let everybody on the street know whom you slept with last night, alright?"

"Okay."

Han Te rubbed his nose and said casually, "I'll try!"

"… You'll try?"

His artificial eyes blinking, the Fist King was in deep thought. A moment later, he leaned closer to Han Te expressionlessly.

In cracking sounds, his two mechanical arms were dismembered, revealing the glittering operation facilities inside.

Han Te was chilled. Sensing a strong crisis from the guy's cold eyes, he screamed in fear, "Master Fist King, what's up? You… You are not going to beat me, are you? You can't beat me. You are supposed to protect me. You can't hurt me!"

"That's right. I certainly will not hurt you but will try to protect you with everything possible."

In the gentlest tone, the Fist King said, "I'm going to protect you from any threat, including viruses."

"Viruses?"

Han Te was stunned. "W-What viruses?"

"All kinds of viruses, particularly those caused by a messy private life."

The Fist King's right arm was thrown over like a whip and took off Han Te's pants. "Considering your recent performance, it is my judgment that your lower half body is under the severe threat of various types of viruses and germs. For your safety, I am going to help sanitize you."

"Lower half body? Sanitize?"

Han Te's face was pale. Looking at the sanitation equipment that the Fist King's left hand presented, he was even more scared and shrieked, "What's that long and thick needle about? That's too unbelievable!"

"It's for the sanitation of the inner wall."

The Fist King fixated Han Te to the ground and explained, "If you want to sanitize a water tube, the shell is only secondary, and the key is the inner wall, right?"

"Ahh!"

Han Te was about to say something, but he was in too much pain to make any meaningful syllable. He simply cried in agony, "Ahhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhh!"

It was not until three minutes later when he became as twisted as a cooked lobster when the Fist King stopped.

"Alright, your little water tube is as good as new and will not suffer any threat from bacteria and viruses for now."

The Fist King said, "However, if you keep on flirting with girls, the odds that you are invaded again will be quite high. So, I will decide according to your lifestyle whether or not you need new preventive therapy—a comprehensive sanitation from inside to outside."

"I was wrong."

Hardly breathing, Han Te held his belly and cramped. "I was really wrong, bro!"

"Good."

The Fist King nodded his head and suddenly changed the topic. "Right, do you know Liu Li's favorite food?"

"Huh?"

The weird question made Han Te forget the pain in his groin, which seemed to have just been hit by a meteoroid. "What's going on?"

"I want to give Liu Li a surprise to keep her in the perfect work state while my body is being polished."

The Fist King stared at Han Te. "Is there any type of food whose flavor she has forgotten herself?"

The nonhuman eyes made Han Te shudder hard again. Covering his belly even more tightly, he stuttered, "… I think so. Liu Li loves her mother's noodles with soft-yolked eggs. There were always two eggs, one on the top and the other buried at the bottom of the bowl.

"Eggs were the most precious ingredients on the Land of Sins because they broke easily during the airdrops. So, only when it was Liu Li's birthday would her mother make the food for her. The egg buried below would be the 'surprise'. Liu Li was very happy every time.

"However, nobody made the food for her again after her mother passed away. Even when noodles were made, one egg on the top would be best. We never had the extravagance to bury a second egg below."

"Got it."

Deep in thought, the Fist King rose and said, "I'm off. You were rather badly wounded. Hurry and go to the infirmary to get some balms for yourself."

"How can I go anywhere right now?"

Han Te felt like crying. In a shivering voice, he asked, "Master Fist King, bro, can't you bring me to the infirmary?"

"No."

The Fist King rejected his request coldly. "I'm in a hurry to cook noodles with soft-yolked eggs for Liu Li to treat her trauma, which is very bad. As for your minor wounds, they will not kill you. According to my scanning, there is no fatal threat around. You can totally move yourself to the infirmary one step after another."

"Cook noodles… Minor wounds… Can't kill me…"

Han Te struggled to lower his head. Looking at his bruised and swollen body, he whined aloud, "Master Fist King, Grandpa Yao entrusted Liu Li and I to you and asked you to protect us together! Our privileges are too different. You can't be that biased toward me, can you?"

The Fist King had already walked away, but hearing that, he turned around and squatted before Han Te, nodding his head solemnly.

"Yes."

The Fist King said solemnly, "I'm exactly so biased."

Chapter 2500: A Total Mess

Cling! Cling! Cling! Cling! Cling! Cling!

Two bullets that had been carved with complicated stripes, leaving two streaks of brilliance behind, one red and the other white, were chasing and colliding with each other. They were now gathering and now splitting, drawing the most sophisticated maze in the midair.

When one of the white bullets was losing and about to be blown off by the red bullet, another two white bullets flew close out of nowhere and joined the battle, making the red and white lines and curves even more complicated.

If one were to observe it more carefully, they would discover that it was not four bullets, but a hundred of them in red and white respectively, which were flying randomly as if they were on a 3D chessboard. It was also like the most astounding and magnificent battle of space fleets.

Every bullet was a chess piece and a starship. The collision of bullets was exactly like the capturing of chess pieces and the mutual destruction of starships.

The spiritual energy around the red bullets was obviously more abundant, giving them a higher speed and a greater strength. In the one-versus-one competitions, the white bullets were almost crushed.

However, the deployment of the white bullets entailed the most erudite arts of fleet commanding. Now that they had been gathered, there were thousands of variations. When the battle formations blossomed one after another, the red bullets were often separated from each other and attacked both sides.

The two parties moved faster and faster with their respective advantages until the only things that could be seen were two fuzzy mists of light. The mists of light crashed and infiltrated each other, almost passing each other's "defense line" at the same time and darting at the enemy's commander behind the scene.

The two commanders took action at the same time. Performing the skill of remote manipulation to the maximum, they cut the enemy's force field at first before they captured all the bullets within their own field. The bullets were condensed inside their palms into four scorching and explosive metal balls and tossed at the center of the battlefield. Two deafening explosions immediately echoed, and the blast almost destroyed the entire training room.

When the blast and the smoke gradually dispersed, Li Yao and Boss Bai, the two commanders, laughed at the same time.

"A hundred years ago, when I first saw you, I thought that you were just a cunning, sleazy, and fearless thief. I never expected that you would achieve what you have achieved today!"

Shaking the metal scraps on his hands off, Boss Bai looked at the ground that was riddled with holes by the shrapnel and said with mixed feelings.

"I never thought that I would be able to meet Boss Bai again, or that you would have such… wonderful experiences," Li Yao said sincerely.

His comment was not just for Boss Bai but also for Yan Xinjian, the other soul that had been melted into Boss Bai's body.

Deep below the ground of Spider Den, Li Yao had tried to break into a new level under the help of the training log that Yan Xinjian left behind. It took him five years before he finally broke out of seclusion and completed the first major leap of his life.

Therefore, Yan Xinjian could almost be called a half master for him.

Because of such a connection, his relationship with Boss Bai was naturally highly unusual.

One and a half months had already passed since they occupied the Blue Sky Market. The construction of the Coalition Fleet of Pyromaniacs had been preliminarily finished. The battle missions had been issued. The tactical deception and the intelligence collection work regarding the outside world were being carried out in order too.

The war outside of the Blue Sky Space Zone had already begun. The edge of the Imperium was on fire. The four families had asked the "National Protection Army" that had been resting in the Blue Sky Market to join the war many times. It was time to march out and teach the Immortal Cultivators of the Imperium a lesson about the awesomeness of "pyromaniacs"!

Li Yao was going to the "Seven Seas Grand Market", the nest of the Business Alliance of Ten Thousand Sectors, the next day. That was why he had the time to have the final practice with Boss Bai in the training room today.

"Li Yao, I have a bad memory right now."

Looking at Li Yao, Boss Bai said, "Remind me. Did I ever thank you for saving Bai Kaixin?

"Forget it. I can never thank you enough for that thing. Thank you, Li Yao. Also… It's great to see you again. Really."

When he mentioned his son, the head of space pirates who craved to rage and dominate the multiverse looked and sounded much more gentle. His eyes were particularly sincere when he looked at Li Yao.

"I'm happy to fight side by side with Boss Bai again!"

Greatly moved, Li Yao couldn't help but propose, "Why don't we go to the Seven Seas Grand Market together tomorrow? After all, the Fist King, Xiaoming, and Wenwen are already capable of simulating the basic fleet tactics. There shouldn't be a problem as long as the battle is not too erratic. There will be a better chance to convince Jin Yuyan if you come with me, Boss Bai!"

Boss Bai was also rather moved. "No can do!"

Li Yao: "…"

Boss Bai patted Li Yao's shoulder and said solemnly, "Li Yao, your stealth skills and your silver tongue are famous all over the universe. The victory will certainly be ours even if you go there by yourself, not to mention that you have tricked—no, persuaded Li Jialing to go with you, right? If I go with you, wouldn't it be an insulting gesture that implies I don't trust you at all?"

Li Yao was rather suspicious. "Is that so?"

"Of course. Alright, enough talking. We have a guest waiting outside of the training room!"

Boss Bai stopped talking and waved his hands, turning off the defensive shield and the sound-blocking system around the training room. His eyes suddenly grew sharp as he stared at the "guest" waiting outside before he said coldly, "Marquess Yongchun, you are truly a trustworthy man, and you have indeed returned to report again. You must've been troubled!"

The person observing Li Yao and Boss Bai's exercise from the outside was exactly Li Wuji, Marquess Yongchun, who was previously loyal to the four families.

Li Yao did not hide himself before Li Wuji.

It was because he chopped off the head of the flagship of the Flying Rainbow Fleet under the watch of everyone, which was impossible to be kept a secret.

As long as they were not going to kill Li Wuji, the guy would learn the matter sooner or later. So, it might be a better option to show himself in public and further intimidate Li Wuji!

Li Yao's deterrence had proved to be rather effective because it was not the first time that Li Wuji had heard the name of "Vulture Li Yao".

Back when the reformists rose unstoppably and barged into the capital, the name of "Vulture Li Yao" had already soared. Everybody in the four families knew that "Vulture Li Yao", driving the Colossus "Gold Vulture", was one of the best warriors of the reformists.

Of course, Li Wuji, who was better informed, also knew that the remarkable warrior of the reformists and the super expert who miraculously rose from nobody knew where had already become the most wanted criminal of the reformists.

The reason why the reformists wanted Li Yao was rather hilarious. It was said that he had betrayed them and went to the four Kurfürst families' side.

That was rather perplexing for Li Wuji, who found it impossible to figure out the stance of Li Yao, Boss Bai, and the rest of them, or the current situation.

While the four families were in cooperation, they were still different interest groups. It was possible that some other family talked this "Vulture Li Yao" into defection without informing the Li family because of their own plan.

All in all, for Li Wuji who had run to a dead end, it was not a bad thing that Li Yao, a "traitor" of the reformists, had appeared. At the very least, it suggested that Boss Bai was not lying, and they were truly not on the reformists' side. So, there was still a great room for cooperation.

Stubbornness was the most untreatable disease. Once one could think it through, there would be nothing they wouldn't do.

When Li Wuji contemplated above the septic tank, he had already thought everything through. He had lost all his troops and run into such a brutal man like "Vulture Li Yao". He could not defeat the man, and he did not want to die. What choice did he have except to cooperate with the guy?

Getting to the bottom of it, this was just a civil war of the Imperium. Whoever won in the end, the stew would still be in the pot. As long as it was guaranteed that he could have a bowl of it and an intact head to eat it in the end, nothing else seemed to matter.

All in all, after negotiating with Boss Bai and Li Yao for a whole night and settling all the terms, Li Wuji returned to the territory of the four families with the "bodyguards" that Boss Bai selected for him. He actively spoke for the "National Protection Army" and helped them collect the latest intelligence. He had even come back to the Blue Sky Market to report in person a month later.

Li Wuji had been working hard for the "National Protection Army" without any second thought. Considering his weakness, it was impossible for him to play any trick too.

The moment he returned to the Blue Sky Market, he had witnessed the advanced competition between Li Yao and Boss Bai. It was both deterrence and a pacifier. For a standard Immortal Cultivator like Li Wuji, it was their natural instinct to submit to the strong. The stronger Li Yao and Boss Bai were, the more loyal he would be.

"I'm never troubled to serve Fellow Cultivator Li and Commander Bai."

Li Wuji bowed at them humbly and said sycophantically, "Fortunately, I have accomplished the task that you entrusted to me. The route of the four families' army, the distribution of the army, and the time of their gathering have all been collected!"

According to Li Wuji, the battle at the peripheral worlds of the Imperium had long begun, but it would not be a quick battle of fleets, but a protracted war of attrition.

On the four families' side, they had just lost the capital and the emperor. They were also scared that the reformists in the capital would ambush their territory. So, they were unwilling to mobilize their elite troops to attack the rebels at the edge of the Imperium.

The reformists in the capital seemed to have a bright future, but they simply had too few troops, and they could not afford any failure or attrition. So, they were unwilling to attack easily either.

On the other hand, the Imperium still had tremendous elites in the "newly-recovered territory" that was just taken back from the Covenant Alliance. They were known as the expedition army.

The expedition army was a complicated, gargantuan group, and the branch fleets came from various places. They did not have a leader, but a lot of young officers in the expedition army respected Lei Chenghu, the highest commander of the Astounding Thunder Fleet, as their commander.

Theoretically, Lei Chenghu was a great general of the reformists, who had entered into the heartland of the Li family like a nail brutally. But in the half a year that followed, he did nothing at all, as if he had completely vanished.

The whole thing couldn't be more interesting.
